**[00:44:13.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000087)  
Good morning to everyone.

**[00:44:18.330] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000088)  
To our Department of Agriculture family,

**[00:44:21.470] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000089)  
led by Secretary Francisco Kiko Tiu Laurel Jr.,

**[00:44:26.070] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000090)  
in the National Irrigation Administration family,

**[00:44:29.630] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000091)  
led by Administrator Eduardo Eddie Guillen,

**[00:44:35.170] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000092)  
our partners from the Civil Society Organization

**[00:44:38.950] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000093)  
and all colleagues participating in today's budget hearing,

**[00:44:44.850] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000094)  
good morning.

**[00:44:47.520] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000095)  
Today, our task is not merely to review our profession,

**[00:44:53.160] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000096)  
but to support in sharpening the agri-sector's direction,

**[00:44:57.260] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000097)  
raise local production,

**[00:45:00.000] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000098)  
improve the incomes of farmers and peasant folks,

**[00:45:04.740] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000099)  
and ensure that assistance reaches producers in the right place and at the right time.

**[00:45:13.380] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000100)  
As President Ferdinand Marcos Jr. reminded us,

**[00:45:20.730] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000101)  
our national budget serves as our moral and economic compass,

**[00:45:26.410] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000102)  
always pointing toward making life better for our people.

**[00:45:34.670] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000103)  
The fiscal year 2027 NEP proposes P198.45 billion for the DA in its attached corporations,

**[00:45:47.530] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000104)  
while P46.36 billion is proposed as budgetary support

**[00:45:56.090] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000105)  
for the DA in its attached corporations.

**[00:45:56.410] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000106)  
We support the Department's direction to treat these resources as one value-chain strategy,

**[00:46:10.080] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000107)  
from land, water, credit, and mechanized production to poor harvest facilities,

**[00:46:19.220] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000108)  
logistic, market matching, and better return for producers.

**[00:46:24.620] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000109)  
The P16 billion program,

**[00:46:27.660] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000110)  
the Farm-to-Market Road Program,

**[00:46:30.720] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000111)  
the P250 million CADIWA program,

**[00:46:35.040] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000112)  
cold storage facilities,

**[00:46:37.660] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000113)  
consolidation,

**[00:46:38.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000114)  
and digital market information should work together

**[00:46:43.860] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000115)  
to reduce transport costs and spoilage,

**[00:46:48.880] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000116)  
improve farm gate prices,

**[00:46:52.040] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000117)  
and connect farmers and peasant folks with reliable buyers.

**[00:46:59.540] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000118)  
And because water is the lifeblood of our field,

**[00:47:05.260] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000119)  
the fiscal year 2027 NEP proposes P46.36 billion in budgetary support for NIA,

**[00:47:15.380] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000120)  
with only 2.20 million hectares developed out of the country,

**[00:47:23.700] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000121)  
3.13 million hectares of potential irrigatable area,

**[00:47:29.540] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000122)  
and more than 1.5 million hectares of land.

**[00:47:30.300] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000123)  
We support NIA's direction to accelerate irrigation development

**[00:47:38.160] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000124)  
while giving equal attention to refer restoration,

**[00:47:43.980] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000125)  
climate adoption, and service road improvements,

**[00:47:48.900] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000126)  
irrigation, funding, and projects should be coordinated

**[00:47:53.120] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000127)  
with the DA community plans,

**[00:47:56.120] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000128)  
crops, calendars, LGUs, and irrigation.

**[00:48:01.340] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000129)  
We also support NIA's commitment to improve the quality of the water and the environment

**[00:48:13.980] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000130)  
so that every new or restored system immediately support additional production in diversified cropping.

**[00:48:15.420] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000131)  
Production support must also include timely and affordable agricultural credit,

**[00:48:22.800] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000132)  
particularly for small farmers,

**[00:48:26.240] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000133)  
peasant folks, cooperatives, and farmers' associations.

**[00:48:31.340] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000134)  
Mechanization through PELMIG in the DA Banners Program

**[00:48:36.040] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000135)  
must improve labor and output efficiency,

**[00:48:41.900] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000136)  
expand harvesting, drying, storage, and processing capacity,

**[00:48:48.440] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000137)  
and reduce losses between harvest and market credit.

**[00:48:54.400] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000138)  
Equipment, training, insurance, and market access should be delivered,

**[00:49:01.340] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000139)  
as an integrated package in coordination with LGUs and local farmers

**[00:49:08.760] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000140)  
rather than as separate interventions.

**[00:49:13.860] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000141)  
Every allocation proposed for the year 2027

**[00:49:18.860] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000142)  
must be authored on a clear purpose,

**[00:49:23.340] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000143)  
a defined intervention in a missionable result.

**[00:49:29.800] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000144)  
We must be able to explain,

**[00:49:31.340] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000145)  
not only how much we are requesting,

**[00:49:35.580] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000146)  
but why we are requesting it,

**[00:49:38.500] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000147)  
what problem it addresses,

**[00:49:42.220] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000148)  
who will benefit in what improvement.

**[00:49:45.520] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000149)  
We expect to see where program have demonstrated results.

**[00:49:51.460] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000150)  
We must identify how to sustain our scale of this gain,

**[00:49:56.920] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000151)  
where implementation challenges have been increased,

**[00:50:01.340] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000152)  
why it's being considered,

**[00:50:03.140] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000153)  
and how to be the key to the improvement.

**[00:50:04.180] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000154)  
This must be addressed through better planning,

**[00:50:06.160] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000155)  
stronger education, and more program design.

**[00:50:11.520] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000156)  
Our objective is clear,

**[00:50:14.100] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000157)  
to arrive at a budget proposal that is credible,

**[00:50:19.500] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000158)  
responsive performance informed,

**[00:50:23.560] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000159)  
and capable of delivering meaningful results

**[00:50:27.380] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000160)  
for the Philippines agriculture.

**[00:50:30.140] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000161)  
Accordingly,

**[00:50:30.340] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000162)  
I hope that the government of the Philippines will provide us with a number of means for developing a more robust and sustainable program.

**[00:50:31.340] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000163)  
The committee will look for clear regional and community targets, accessible credit, operational irrigation system, lower post-harvest losses, stronger market, matching, and better day-to-day coordination that gives local farmers a direct voice in planning and implementation.

**[00:50:57.100] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000164)  
At this point, before the agencies begin their representation, I would like to introduce the sponsor of today's budget, Vice Chairman Dr. Anna York, our lead sponsor.

**[00:53:40.730] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000192)  
So, may we know whether we should add some more minutes because five minutes will not be sufficient for both big agencies of government with separate budgets.

**[00:53:51.230] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000193)  
May we know what will be the consideration of the Chair?

**[00:53:57.020] Committee member** (asr-000194)

**[00:53:57.520] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000195)

**[00:54:00.540] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000196)

**[00:54:08.620] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000197)

**[00:54:18.180] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000198)  
But, boss, siguro, flexible na lang natin kung lalampas tayo ng five minutes.

**[00:54:29.800] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000199)  
Siguro, hindi ko na...

**[00:54:31.900] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000200)

**[00:54:37.640] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000201)

**[00:54:38.380] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000202)  
And flexibility.

**[00:54:39.700] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000203)

**[00:54:43.220] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-000204)  
Thank you.

**[00:54:44.160] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000205)  
Thank you.

**[00:54:45.460] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-000206)  
Mr. Chairman.

**[00:54:47.080] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-000207)  
Mr. Chair.

**[00:54:48.360] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-000208)  
May I?

**[00:54:50.120] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-000209)  
Mr. Chair.

**[00:54:52.500] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000210)  
Congressman, your witness.

**[00:54:54.520] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-000211)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[00:54:55.680] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-000212)  
I joined the manifestation of Honorable Rodriguez, Mr. Chair.

**[00:55:00.760] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-000213)  
In fact, this representation also had so many concerns, not only on the DA, but more particularly on the NIA.

**[00:55:08.380] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-000214)  
So, I think more than five minutes is enough for us, Mr. Chair.

**[00:55:14.380] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000215)  
Thank you.

**[00:55:17.420] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000216)  
And then, before the day of presentation, let us hear from the Vice Chair Bundok, my co-lead for her message.

**[00:55:32.920] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000217)  
Thank you so much, Pong Tevez and Vice Chair Tevez.

**[00:55:38.660] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000218)  
Thank you so much, everyone, and good morning.

**[00:55:40.680] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000219)  
I hope you can hear me.

**[00:55:43.240] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000220)  
Good morning, everyone.

**[00:55:44.840] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000221)  
I am the Vice Chair in charge of the Department of Agriculture, and I am pleased to welcome everyone to our consultation and budget presentation of the Department of Agriculture for the 2027 budget.

**[00:56:00.740] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000222)  
Unfortunately, I cannot join you and cannot be physically present today because of severe and persistent flooding in Central Luzon, Pampanga, and most especially in my district.

**[00:56:12.280] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000223)  
Yes.

**[00:56:13.240] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000224)  
The San Simon-Enlex exit, which has now caused a 10-hour backlog and a Carmageddon, which is causing problems not only for our district, but for the entire Metro Manila and the entire north, is in my district also.

**[00:56:29.460] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000225)  
Nevertheless, I am honored to welcome everyone today.

**[00:56:33.340] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000226)  
Thank you so much to Secretary Francisco Kiko Tiu Laurel Jr. of the Department of Agriculture and to the DA family.

**[00:56:40.940] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000227)  
And thank you also.

**[00:56:43.240] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000228)  
I would like to recognize our NIA Administrator, Admin Eduardo Eddie-Guillen today.

**[00:56:50.660] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000229)  
To my colleagues who are taking over for me today, Congressman Joseph Tan, who is representing the NIA, Vice Chair Pong Tevez, and also Vice Chair Christopher Gua.

**[00:57:02.780] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000230)  
Thank you so much for assisting me during these difficult times.

**[00:57:06.860] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000231)  
Nevertheless, we are here today to address so many issues facing the DA.

**[00:57:12.280] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000232)  
Number one of them is…

**[00:57:13.080] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000233)  
Number one of which is...

**[00:57:13.120] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000234)  
Number one of which is…

**[00:57:13.240] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000235)  
which is food security, the stability of food prices, the welfare of farmers, support for our economy.

**[00:57:22.400] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000236)  
And I think one of the most important issues today is recovery from the flood,

**[00:57:27.020] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000237)  
which is the worst flood which has ever occurred in the Philippines,

**[00:57:30.240] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000238)  
and sadly has occurred in the rice granary of the Philippines, Central Luzon.

**[00:57:35.360] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000239)  
And of course, we must face the challenges of climate change,

**[00:57:39.040] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000240)  
which is one of the major causes of the El Nino flood, which is ravaging Philippine agriculture today.

**[00:57:46.800] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000241)  
Together with the DA family and the NIA, we welcome your concerns and will try to address them accordingly.

**[00:57:56.670] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000242)  
Thank you for your questions and we will work together with you,

**[00:58:00.170] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000243)  
hopefully to a successful support for the 2027 budget of the Department of Agriculture and the National Irrigation Authority.

**[00:58:10.290] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-000244)  
So thank you so much, everyone. Maya Payabak po, and good morning.

**[00:58:16.990] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000245)  
Thank you, Vice-Chair Bundok.

**[00:58:21.640] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000246)  
Secretary Laurel, you may now proceed with your presentation.

**[00:58:29.340] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000247)  
Honorable members of Congress, distinguished colleagues, ladies and gentlemen,

**[00:58:34.060] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000248)  
good morning to all.

**[00:58:37.220] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000249)  
We are here today to present the Department of Agriculture's proposed budget for fiscal year 2027

**[00:58:42.580] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000250)  
and to seek the continued support of the Department of Agriculture and the National Irrigation Authority.

**[00:58:44.300] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000251)  
The President emphasized in his budget message,

**[00:58:55.680] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000252)  
the national budget must ultimately make a difference in the lives of our people.

**[00:59:00.140] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000253)  
For the Department of Agriculture, that means two things that are inseparable,

**[00:59:07.500] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000254)  
ensuring that the Filipino families have sufficient, safe, nutritious, and affordable food,

**[00:59:11.660] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000255)  
and making sure that farmers and fisher folks,

**[00:59:14.300] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000256)  
who produce that food, can earn a decent and sustainable living.

**[00:59:18.300] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000257)  
We cannot have one without the other.

**[00:59:20.800] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000258)  
We cannot demand affordable food while leaving those who produce it struggling to cover their costs.

**[00:59:26.600] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000259)  
And we cannot expect our farmers and fisher folks to keep producing for the nation

**[00:59:31.100] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000260)  
if farming and fishing no longer provide them with a viable livelihood and a better life for their families.

**[00:59:38.800] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000261)  
This is why agriculture must be properly and sufficiently funded.

**[00:59:43.100] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000262)  
Every investment we make in agriculture is, at its core, an investment in food security.

**[00:59:48.940] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000263)  
It is an investment in the people who put food on our tables,

**[00:59:52.360] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000264)  
and it is an investment in the future of our country.

**[00:59:55.400] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000265)  
Our farmers and fisher folk continue to face high production costs,

**[00:59:58.760] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000266)  
changing weather,

**[01:00:00.000] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000267)  
patterns climate related risks market uncertainties and other challenges that can quickly turn

**[01:00:06.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000268)  
a good harvest or a good catch into a difficult year yet despite these challenges we must

**[01:00:12.800] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000269)  
continue to ensure that food reaches filipino families in sufficient supply and at prices that

**[01:00:19.520] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000270)  
they can afford our proposed 2027 budget is designed to help bridge that gap we will continue

**[01:00:26.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000271)  
investing in programs that will raise productivity improve farm and fishery infrastructure reduce

**[01:00:32.800] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000272)  
post-harvest losses strengthen logistics and market access and expand the use of appropriate

**[01:00:38.480] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000273)  
technologies we will strengthen agriculture and fisheries value change so that the farmers and

**[01:00:43.760] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000274)  
fisher folk can capture greater share of the value they create we will also continue building

**[01:00:50.160] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000275)  
a sector that's more resilient to climate change disasters market disruptions and other risks

**[01:00:57.600] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000276)  
in a moment our undersecretary for finance undersecretary nora c oliveras will present

**[01:01:02.880] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000277)  
the details of our proposed budget and the programs and investments behind these priorities

**[01:01:08.320] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000278)  
but beyond the numbers we must always ask what these investments will deliver

**[01:01:13.520] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000279)  
mga katulong baang ma eto upang higit na dumami ang produksyon at lumaki ang kita ng ating mga

**[01:01:18.320] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000280)  
magsasaka at manging isda makakabawas baang ma eto sa pagkaluget makapaghatid ng pagkain sa

**[01:01:26.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000281)  
pangmahalikan ng mas mabilis at mas mahusay makapagbibigay ba ang ma eto ng mga mas

**[01:01:33.440] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000282)  
matatag sapat nigtas at abot kayang supply ng pagkain para sa sambayanan at higit sa lahat

**[01:01:39.600] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000283)  
mga katulong baang ma eto upang magkaroon ng maas maayos at mas manangal na buhay ang mga taong

**[01:01:46.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000284)  
araw-araw na nagsisikap upang may pagkain sa ating mga hapag ito ang mga kongkretong resulta na

**[01:01:52.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000285)  
dapat nating sama-samang pagsikapang makamit

**[01:01:56.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000286)  
na rin ng kagawaran na ang bawat pisong ipagkakatiwala sa atin ay pera ng taong bayan

**[01:02:01.440] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000287)  
kaya dapat itong gamitin ng wasto maingat mahusay at may buong pananagutan patuloy nating pagbubutihin

**[01:02:10.560] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000288)  
ang pagpapatupad ng ating mga programa palakasin ang monitoring at accountability at itiyakin ang

**[01:02:17.440] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000289)  
servisyo at suporta ng pamahalan ay tunay na nakakarating sa mga magsasaka manging isda at mga

**[01:02:23.200] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000290)  
pamayanang higit na nangangailangan nito

**[01:02:26.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000291)  
honorable members the proposed 2027 budget is about more than funding government programs

**[01:02:33.780] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000292)  
it's about deciding what kind of agriculture we want to build and what kind of future we want

**[01:02:39.320] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000293)  
for the people who produce our food we want food security we must invest in people in the systems

**[01:02:46.200] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000294)  
that make it possible if you want affordable food we must help farmers and fisher folks become more

**[01:02:51.960] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000295)  
productive competitive and profitable and if you want a better life for all you need to

**[01:02:56.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000296)  
support our people we must also build a better life for those who feed the nation with the

**[01:03:00.940] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000297)  
continuous support of congress we can do more to modernize philippine agriculture strengthen our

**[01:03:06.740] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000298)  
fishery sector improve productivity incomes and build a food system that is more secure

**[01:03:12.940] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000299)  
resilient and responsive to the needs of a growing population this is not simply an investment in

**[01:03:18.460] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000300)  
agriculture it is an investment in food security it is an investment in our farmers and fisher folks

**[01:03:23.980] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000301)  
sangalan

**[01:03:24.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000302)  
Sa

**[01:03:24.680] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000303)

**[01:03:25.680] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000305)

**[01:03:26.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000307)  
Agrikultura,

**[01:03:27.140] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000308)  
buong

**[01:03:27.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000309)

na

Kapulungan

ito

sa

aming

budget.

**[01:03:35.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000325)  
Sama-sama nating itaguyod ang isang agrikultura na hindi lamang nagpapakain sa bansa kundi nagbibigay rin ng mas maayos, mas marangal at mas magandang buhay sa mga taong nagpapakain sa atin. Maraming salamat po sa inyong suporta at sa inyong patuloy na pagtulong na maisulong ang isang masaganang bagong Pilipinas. Thank you, Mr. Chairman. Now, we would like to ask Usec

**[01:04:07.350] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-000326)  
Asis Perez and Nora Oliveros to do the presentation, Mr. Chair, if you may allow.

**[01:04:12.660] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000327)  
Yes.

**[01:04:14.000] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000328)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary, with the permission of the Chair.

**[01:04:19.490] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000329)  
Mr. Chair, Honorable Members of Congress, colleagues, ladies and gentlemen,

**[01:04:23.810] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000330)  
it is our honor to present the Department of Agriculture's

**[01:04:28.270] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000331)  
2027 National Expenditure Program.

**[01:04:32.310] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000332)  
Before we dive into the details of the National Expenditure Program,

**[01:04:35.790] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000333)  
we would first like to provide a brief snapshot

**[01:04:39.570] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000334)  
of the current status

**[01:04:40.930] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000335)  
and situation of agriculture and fisheries sector

**[01:04:44.110] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000336)  
and the planning paradigms that guide the Department

**[01:04:47.530] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000337)  
in its priority interventions.

**[01:04:50.310] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000338)  
This will provide the broader context

**[01:04:52.710] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000339)  
for understanding on how our priorities

**[01:04:56.090] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000340)  
are translated into programs, investments under the NEP.

**[01:05:01.070] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000341)  
After this overview, Undersecretary Nora Olivares

**[01:05:04.610] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000342)  
will walk us through the details

**[01:05:07.050] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000343)  
of the National Expenditure Program.

**[01:05:09.810] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000344)  
If I may proceed, Mr. Chair.

**[01:05:10.930] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000345)  
The PSA reported 3.1% growth in agriculture,

**[01:05:33.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000346)  
forestry, and fisheries gross value added in 2025,

**[01:05:38.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000347)  
rebounding from 1.5% contraction in 2024.

**[01:05:43.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000348)  
Despite this recovery, the long-term remains work-in-progress,

**[01:05:50.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000349)  
as crops, livestock, and fisheries will continue to grow,

**[01:05:52.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000350)  
and farmers will continue to grow.

**[01:05:53.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000351)  
In addition,

**[01:05:54.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000352)  
agriculture and fisheries have yet to return

**[01:05:55.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000353)  
into their peak levels,

**[01:05:56.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000354)  
leaving poultry the sole exception, with 9.1% growth.

**[01:05:59.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000355)  
Consequently, agriculture and fisheries' contribution

**[01:06:04.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000356)  
to the overall economy fell to 7.9% in 2025,

**[01:06:12.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000357)  
in contrast to the past growing sector.

**[01:06:16.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000358)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:06:19.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000359)  
But behind this number are our food producers,

**[01:06:21.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000360)  
as of July 15, 2026,

**[01:06:24.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000361)  
the Registry System for Basic Sector in Agriculture,

**[01:06:28.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000362)  
RSBSA,

**[01:06:29.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000363)  
lists 10.44 million Munich individuals.

**[01:06:35.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000364)  
This aligns with PSA estimate that agriculture,

**[01:06:39.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000365)  
fishery, and forestry employs around 10 million people,

**[01:06:44.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000366)

**[01:20:15.430] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000574)  
A simple simulation demonstrates a clear potential to substantially narrow the country's agricultural

**[01:20:23.790] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000575)  
deficit in the coming years, and I would like to thank Congress for passing the Animal

**[01:20:30.810] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000576)  
Industry Reform Law, or AIDCA, which provided 20 billion for the animal industry.

**[01:20:37.530] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000577)  
We believe that through this law, we can narrow down the importation requirement.

**[01:20:44.510] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000578)  
By sustaining 5% of the annual growth rate in agricultural export, while gradually cutting

**[01:20:51.190] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000579)  
agricultural imports by 5%, we will be able to reduce the cost of agricultural exports

**[01:20:53.770] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000580)  
by year 2008.

**[01:20:56.090] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000581)  
The trade best deficit can shrunk to about 44%.

**[01:21:00.330] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000582)  
We already did 11, so hopefully we can do 44.

**[01:21:05.650] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000583)  
This projection reflects expected progress, particularly in achieving livestock and poultry

**[01:21:12.170] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000584)  
self-sufficiency under AIDCA framework.

**[01:21:14.770] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000585)  
Again, we would like to thank Congress for passing that important bill.

**[01:21:20.190] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000586)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:21:22.330] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000587)  
This accomplishment demonstrates a clear potential to substantially narrow the country's agricultural

**[01:21:23.330] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000588)  
deficit in the coming years, and I would like to thank Congress for passing the animal

**[01:21:24.330] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000589)  
industry reform law, which demonstrated significant progress in addressing logistics, market

**[01:21:28.330] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000590)  
access bottlenecks through improved roads, bridges, flatlanding facilities, cold storage,

**[01:21:35.570] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000591)  
and ice plants.

**[01:21:37.270] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000592)  
Despite these gains, persistent gaps remain in the cold value chain.

**[01:21:43.850] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000593)  
Moving forward, expanding cold value chain systems must complement investments in roads,

**[01:21:50.650] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000594)  
ports, storage facilities.

**[01:21:52.690] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000595)  
to close the remaining infrastructure gap and ensure these efforts will yield higher

**[01:21:59.290] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000596)  
return for local fishers.

**[01:22:01.450] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000597)  
Now, looking at the budget, Mr. Chair, although the Department's agriculture overall budget

**[01:22:19.350] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000598)  
has expanded significantly during the PBBM administration, its share in the national

**[01:22:27.460] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000599)  
budget has remained relatively low, averaging just 2.6% over the past two decades.

**[01:22:41.280] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000600)  
PAO data underscores this gap, showing the Philippines allocated 2.84% in 2023 and 3.33%

**[01:22:51.220] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000601)  
in 2024.

**[01:22:54.760] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000602)  
We have lower expenditure priorities compared to our regional peers like China, Vietnam,

**[01:23:02.280] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000603)  
Thailand, Cambodia, and Bangladesh.

**[01:23:06.160] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000604)  
Now, let me just focus a little bit on Indonesia, Mr. Chair.

**[01:23:11.280] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000605)  
A big factor that we are looking at is in the case of Indonesia, because in the case

**[01:23:16.800] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000606)  
of Indonesia, their expenditure is the same, 2.6% of their annual budget.

**[01:23:24.780] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000607)  
However, Indonesia's focus on agricultural extension in 2022.

**[01:23:32.260] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000608)  
The robust extension services served as a vital mechanism for technology transfer and

**[01:23:40.440] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000609)  
farmer decision support.

**[01:23:42.960] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000610)  
And as a result, Indonesia is no longer importing rice in 2025.

**[01:23:48.420] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000611)  
That is why, Mr. Chair, members of Congress, thank you for working heavily on this agriculture

**[01:23:55.660] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000612)  
extension system that is currently pending before Congress.

**[01:24:02.340] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000613)  
On the priority legislative measures.

**[01:24:04.280] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000614)  
I'm just listing down, Mr. Chair, the request of the Department of Agriculture.

**[01:24:10.580] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000615)  
We believe that alongside a robust budget, we'll require structural reforms by way of

**[01:24:17.960] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000616)  
law and legislation.

**[01:24:20.280] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000617)  
And so, we have a list of our wish for this Congress, and hopefully, combined with proper

**[01:24:29.080] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000618)  
budgeting, we can really set the tide of agricultural growth.

**[01:24:32.900] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000619)  
Thank you.

**[01:24:34.080] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000620)

**[01:24:48.080] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000622)  
set by the Secretary that guides the formulation of our budget.

**[01:24:51.880] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000623)

on the part of those who produce.

**[01:25:05.440] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000626)

**[01:25:35.400] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000635)  
We also set up a criteria for evaluating all our PAP to ensure that programs and budgets

**[01:25:45.300] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000636)

**[01:25:51.160] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000637)  
These core policy shift anchors the department broader reform agenda.

**[01:25:56.200] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000638)  
Taken together, they establish the strategic policy reference for guiding resources prioritization,

**[01:26:01.440] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-000639)  
and we are looking to implement it.

**[01:41:18.690] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000860)  
for the National Irrigation Administration.

**[01:41:22.490] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000861)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:41:25.310] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000862)  
Good morning, good afternoon,

**[01:41:27.710] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000863)  
honorable Chairperson of the Committee of Appropriations,

**[01:41:30.630] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000864)  
Honorable Congresswoman Swan-Zing,

**[01:41:33.430] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000865)  
distinguished members and representatives

**[01:41:36.510] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000866)  
of the House of Representatives, officials and staff of NIA,

**[01:41:41.510] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000867)  
of the DA, and fellow public servants.

**[01:41:43.650] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000868)  
On behalf of the National Irrigation Administration, we are honored to present our fiscal year

**[01:41:49.550] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000869)  
2027 budget proposal.

**[01:41:52.610] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000870)  
This proposal aims to accelerate the development of new irrigation systems, rehabilitate and

**[01:41:58.570] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000871)  
restore existing facilities, expand climate-resilient irrigation technologies, and strengthen the

**[01:42:05.630] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000872)  
sustainability and reliability of irrigation services nationwide.

**[01:42:12.270] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000873)  
Our fiscal year 2027 budget proposal is firmly anchored on NIA's seven-point strategic agenda,

**[01:42:18.710] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000874)  
which serves as the strategic framework guiding the agency priorities and investments.

**[01:42:25.070] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000875)  
These seven strategic agendas include the irrigation infrastructure development, the

**[01:42:30.310] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000876)  
Irrigators Association capacity building, climate smart farming systems, management

**[01:42:36.990] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000877)  
and governance, monitoring and evaluation, capability building of human resources, and

**[01:42:42.250] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000878)  
strategic communication and advocacy.

**[01:42:47.490] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000879)  
These strategic priorities ensure that NEA's investments go beyond the construction and

**[01:42:52.610] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000880)  
rehabilitation of irrigation facilities.

**[01:42:55.370] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000881)  
They also focus on strengthening farmers' organizations, promoting climate-resilient

**[01:43:00.110] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000882)  
technologies, enhancing institutional efficiency, developing human resources, and improving

**[01:43:07.650] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000883)  
stakeholders' engagement.

**[01:43:10.650] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000884)  
Because of this integrated approach, NEA is committed to improving the infrastructure,

**[01:43:11.650] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000885)  
infrastructure, and irrigation facilities.

**[01:43:12.250] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000886)  
In addition to these efforts, NEA aims to deliver sustainable irrigation services that contribute

**[01:43:16.890] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000887)  
to increase agricultural productivity, food security, and improve livelihoods for Filipino

**[01:43:22.590] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000888)  
farmers.

**[01:43:24.130] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000889)  
The National Irrigation Master Plan, or NMIP, for 2020-2030 serves as the country's strategic

**[01:43:31.570] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000890)  
blueprint for irrigation development.

**[01:43:34.270] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000891)  
This provides a comprehensive framework for expanding and modernizing irrigation systems

**[01:43:38.290] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000892)  
to increase irrigated agricultural areas and enhance farm productivity.

**[01:43:39.490] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000893)  
The National Irrigation Master Plan, or NMIP, serves as the country's strategic blueprint

**[01:43:40.490] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000894)  
for irrigation development.

**[01:43:41.490] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000895)  
This provides a comprehensive framework for expanding and modernizing irrigation systems

**[01:43:42.550] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000896)  
to increase agricultural productivity, food security, and reduce poverty in rural communities.

**[01:43:45.070] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000897)  
To strengthen our planning framework, NEA updated the country's potential irrigable

**[01:43:47.230] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000898)  
area through the original Irrigation Master Plan.

**[01:43:50.370] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000899)  
The updated assessment incorporates the largest geospatial and technical data from agencies

**[01:43:54.690] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000900)  
such as the BSWM, DNR, WRB, and Fillbox, etc.

**[01:43:55.850] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000901)  
The comprehensive plan also includes the largest geospatial and technical data from agencies

**[01:44:04.850] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000902)  
such as the BSWM, DNR, WRB, and Fillbox, etc.

**[01:44:06.490] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000903)  
The national Irrigation Master Plan also includes the largest geospatial and technical data from

**[01:44:07.490] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000904)  
agencies such as the BSWM, DNR, WRB, and Fillbox, etc.

**[01:44:08.490] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000905)  
The national IRG plan integrates environmental initiatives have so-called NMIP commitments,

**[01:44:09.490] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000906)  
even national irrigations.

**[01:44:10.490] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000907)  
The purpose of this initiative is to ensure that our investment planning is based on accurate

**[01:44:16.570] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000908)  
and validated data, allowing us to prioritize areas with the highest irrigation development

**[01:44:21.930] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000909)  
potential.

**[01:44:22.930] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000910)  
Based on the updated methodology, we expanded the slope criterion from the traditional 3%

**[01:44:28.770] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000911)  
to up to 8% of countries' estimated net potential irrigable area, increased by more than 4 million

**[01:44:36.790] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000912)  
hectares nationwide.

**[01:44:37.790] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000913)  
In certain regions, however, suitable areas for irrigation development may extend the

**[01:44:42.710] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000914)  
slope up to 45%, particularly where technologies such as the solar power pump irrigation systems

**[01:44:50.470] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000915)  
and climate-resilient irrigation solutions can be effectively adapted.

**[01:44:54.830] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000916)  
This expanded assessment provides greater opportunities for irrigation development,

**[01:45:00.000] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000917)  
in upland and underserved agricultural areas.

**[01:45:05.300] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000918)  
Based on our previous estimates,

**[01:45:07.280] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000919)  
the remaining area for irrigation development

**[01:45:09.140] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000920)  
was approximately 1 million hectares.

**[01:45:12.800] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000921)  
However, with the updated assessment

**[01:45:14.860] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000922)  
of potential irrigable areas,

**[01:45:16.440] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000923)  
the remaining development potential

**[01:45:18.280] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000924)  
has increased to more than 3 million hectares nationwide.

**[01:45:23.980] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000925)  
As of December 31, 2025,

**[01:45:26.140] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000926)  
the country has developed 2.2 million hectares

**[01:45:28.720] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000927)  
out of the total estimated potential irrigable

**[01:45:32.060] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000928)  
of 3.13 million hectares,

**[01:45:34.380] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000929)  
representing 70.25% of the country's irrigation potential.

**[01:45:40.680] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000930)  
Around 1 million hectares, or approximately 30%,

**[01:45:43.180] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000931)  
remain to be developed.

**[01:45:44.220] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000932)  
Of the total developed area,

**[01:45:46.720] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000933)  
1.79 million hectares, or 82%,

**[01:45:49.780] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000934)  
are under NEA-assisted systems.

**[01:45:52.520] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000935)  
This highlights both NEA's substantial contribution

**[01:45:55.040] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000936)  
to agricultural development

**[01:45:57.080] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000937)  
and continuing the need investment

**[01:45:58.700] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000938)  
to expand irrigation coverages nationwide.

**[01:46:03.560] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000939)  
From 2010 to 2025,

**[01:46:06.180] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000940)  
irrigation development posted a cumulative growth of 17%,

**[01:46:09.360] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000941)  
equivalent to around average annual increase of 1.14%,

**[01:46:14.340] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000942)  
while it reflects steady progress over the years.

**[01:46:17.360] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000943)  
The remaining undeveloped irrigable areas

**[01:46:19.180] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000944)  
underscore the need for larger and more strategic investment

**[01:46:23.000] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000945)  
to accelerate irrigation development

**[01:46:24.800] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000946)  
and support the country's food security,

**[01:46:28.700] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000947)  
agriculture, and agriculture.

**[01:46:31.000] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000948)  
For NEA's FY2027 proposed budget,

**[01:46:35.740] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000949)  
the agency proposed a total of P233.85 billion.

**[01:46:44.560] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000950)  
Honorable members of Congress,

**[01:46:46.380] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000951)  
we fully understand and understand your concern

**[01:46:51.640] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000952)

**[01:46:55.020] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000953)  
Each district has its own needs.

**[01:46:58.700] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000954)  
We are committed to ensuring the quality of the irrigation

**[01:47:00.900] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000955)  
and that each deserving community

**[01:47:03.120] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000956)

**[01:47:06.900] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000957)  
to the government's fund.

**[01:47:10.040] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000958)  
However, the FY2027 net provides only P47.43 billion,

**[01:47:19.240] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000959)  
which is even lower than our 2026 budget of P65 billion.

**[01:47:26.860] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000960)  
This highlights the significant,

**[01:47:28.480] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000961)  
but yet constraint for FY2027

**[01:47:32.480] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000962)  
and the need to prioritize critical implementation-ready

**[01:47:37.920] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000963)

**[01:47:40.680] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000964)  
with the available budget.

**[01:47:51.200] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000967)  
Nakita niyo po dyan po yung aming proposed.

**[01:47:58.480] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000970)  
around P250 plus ang ating mga congressman,

**[01:48:00.980] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000971)  
so ang proposal po namin was P232 billion.

**[01:48:04.820] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000972)  
For general administration and support,

**[01:48:07.440] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000973)

**[01:48:09.380] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000974)  
while the FY2027 net provides P12.5 billion,

**[01:48:13.600] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000975)  
which is 17% higher than the FY2026 GA.

**[01:48:18.120] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000976)  
This supports the agency's general administrative

**[01:48:21.380] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000977)  
and management requirements.

**[01:48:28.480] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000980)

**[01:48:30.660] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-000981)  
while P3.42 billion was provided under the NEP.

Despite the reduction from the proposal,

For operations,

but only P30.44 billion was included.

This is 39% lower than the FY2026 GA,

By expense class,

For personal services,

**[01:49:28.460] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001000)  
while FY2027 net provides P7.29 billion,

Here are the ongoing programs

**[01:49:58.040] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001012)

The agency aims at increasing

**[01:50:01.580] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001015)  
and improving cropping intensity.

**[01:50:04.020] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001016)  
For fiscal year 2027,

**[01:50:06.240] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001017)  
NEA is proposing major investments

**[01:50:08.440] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001018)  
under the Irrigation System Restoration Program

**[01:50:11.080] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001019)  
for both national and communal irrigation systems.

**[01:50:14.900] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001020)  
This program aims to restore

**[01:50:16.580] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001021)

**[01:50:18.360] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001022)

**[01:50:19.660] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001023)  
of irrigation systems,

**[01:50:21.320] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001024)  
and safeguard water supply

**[01:50:23.420] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001025)  
through strategic climate adaptation interventions.

**[01:50:26.800] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001026)  
Under this program,

**[01:50:28.460] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001027)  
NEA is proposing

**[01:50:30.140] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001028)

**[01:50:33.440] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001029)

**[01:50:37.500] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001030)  
P223 million for the climate change adaptation work,

**[01:50:41.660] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001031)  
communal irrigation systems,

**[01:50:43.340] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001032)  
and P473 million for the rehabilitation

**[01:50:45.440] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001033)  
and protection of watershed management areas.

**[01:50:49.440] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001034)  
However,

**[01:50:50.020] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001035)  
only limited funding was included in the NEP.

**[01:50:54.080] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001036)  
For the service roads,

**[01:50:56.900] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001037)  
these are vital components

**[01:50:57.780] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001038)  
of irrigation systems.

**[01:50:59.880] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001039)  
Their purpose is to provide NEA personnel

**[01:51:04.800] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001040)

**[01:51:06.660] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001041)  
and maintenance activities

**[01:51:08.300] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001042)  
while also improving farmers' access to production areas.

**[01:51:13.140] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001043)  
For the improvement of service roads,

**[01:51:15.640] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001044)  
NEA proposed 17.71 billion

**[01:51:19.680] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001045)  
for the national irrigation systems

**[01:51:22.040] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001046)  
and 2.39 billion for the communal irrigation systems.

**[01:51:27.040] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001047)  
However,

**[01:51:28.600] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001048)  
only 250 million was included in the FY2027 NEP.

**[01:51:34.360] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001049)  
The program aims to improve access

**[01:51:36.160] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001050)  
to irrigation facilities and agricultural areas

**[01:51:38.520] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001051)  
with proposed targets of 32.79 kilometers of gravel road

**[01:51:43.080] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001052)  
and 1.3 kilometers of concrete roads.

**[01:51:46.980] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001053)  
The repair program addresses deteriorated

**[01:51:49.220] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001054)  
and damaged canal structures and facilities

**[01:51:51.360] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001055)  
that affect irrigation efficiency.

**[01:51:54.100] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001056)  
Its purpose is to restore operational,

**[01:51:57.040] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001057)  
functionality,

**[01:51:58.340] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001058)  
and prevent further deterioration of existing systems.

**[01:52:01.580] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001059)  
For the repair of irrigation systems,

**[01:52:04.540] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001060)  
NEA proposed 38.26 billion

**[01:52:08.160] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001061)  
for the national irrigation system,

**[01:52:11.000] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001062)  
20.52 billion for the communal irrigation system,

**[01:52:16.280] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001063)  
and 10.42 billion for the pump irrigation system,

**[01:52:20.580] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001064)  
or around 70 billion lahat.

**[01:52:23.780] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001065)  
However,

**[01:52:24.920] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001066)  
the FY2027 NEP provides

**[01:52:27.040] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001067)  
only 2,920,000,000 and 3,000,000,000 respectively.

**[01:52:32.820] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001068)  
These investments are intended

**[01:52:34.900] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001069)  
to restore damaged facilities

**[01:52:36.960] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001070)  
and improve the reliability

**[01:52:38.300] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001071)  
and efficiency of irrigation services.

**[01:52:42.740] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001072)  
The restoration program focuses on irrigation systems

**[01:52:45.840] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001073)  
that have become non-operational

**[01:52:48.460] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001074)

**[01:52:51.020] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001075)

**[01:52:51.800] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001076)  
and severe structural damage.

**[01:52:54.060] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001077)  
Its purpose is to establish irrigation services,

**[01:52:56.980] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001078)  
and recover productivity in agricultural areas.

**[01:53:00.860] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001079)  
For the restoration of irrigation systems,

**[01:53:04.760] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001080)  
NEA proposed 7.26 billion

**[01:53:08.160] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001081)  
for the national irrigation system,

**[01:53:11.240] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001082)  
13 billion for the communal irrigation system,

**[01:53:14.800] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001083)  
or a total of more than 20 billion.

**[01:53:17.320] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001084)  
However,

**[01:53:18.580] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001085)  
the FY2027 NEP provides only

**[01:53:21.000] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001086)  
1 billion and 250 million respectively.

**[01:53:25.580] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001087)  
The program aims

**[01:53:26.900] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001088)  
to restore about 2,243 hectares

**[01:53:30.640] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001089)  
of previously irrigated areas

**[01:53:32.640] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001090)  
and bring back into productive use.

**[01:53:35.940] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001091)  
The next major program

**[01:53:37.020] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001092)  
is the Irrigation System Development Program

**[01:53:39.060] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001093)  
which serves as NEA's primary strategy

**[01:53:41.820] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001094)

**[01:53:44.720] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001095)  
This objective is to construct new irrigation systems

**[01:53:48.960] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001096)  
and provide reliable and sustainable water supply

**[01:53:53.280] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001097)  
to agricultural lands that remain unirrigated.

**[01:53:55.880] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001098)  
For the Irrigation System Development,

**[01:53:58.380] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001099)  
NEA proposed 34.94 billion

**[01:54:01.060] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001100)  
for the new national irrigation system projects,

**[01:54:05.080] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001101)  
or 11.8 billion for the small reservoir irrigation projects.

**[01:54:09.220] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001102)  
However,

**[01:54:10.180] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001103)  
the FY2027 NEP provides only

**[01:54:12.980] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001104)  
7.56 billion and 4.58 billion respectively.

**[01:54:19.420] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001105)  
This project aims to develop

**[01:54:21.020] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001106)  
new irrigation areas and expand irrigation coverage,

**[01:54:24.480] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001107)  
which is proposed

**[01:54:25.800] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001108)  
with a target of 1,226 hectares of new areas.

**[01:54:31.140] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001109)  
Priority projects under this program include

**[01:54:34.180] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001110)  
the ongoing Halaor River Multipurpose Project,

**[01:54:37.900] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001111)  
the Balog-Balog Multipurpose Project Phase 2,

**[01:54:40.940] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001112)  
the Ilocos Norte-Ilocosor Abray Irrigation Project,

**[01:54:43.780] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001113)  
and the Tomawini River Multipurpose Project,

**[01:54:47.220] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001114)  
among other strategic irrigation infrastructure projects nationwide.

**[01:54:52.020] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001115)  
This slide presents NEA's other major developments,

**[01:54:55.800] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001116)  
including the development of new irrigation systems

**[01:54:57.800] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001117)  
and the development of new irrigation systems.

The project aims to expand irrigation coverage

**[01:55:01.800] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001119)  
to upland, remote, and water-deficient areas

**[01:55:03.800] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001120)

**[01:55:06.800] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001121)

**[01:55:09.800] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001122)  
For FY2027,

the NEP includes 7.92 billion

**[01:55:14.800] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001124)  
for the establishment of pump irrigation projects,

4.58 billion for the special irrigation projects,

200 million for the extension and expansion

**[01:55:25.780] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001128)

These investments are expected to generate

**[01:55:33.780] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001130)

**[01:55:42.780] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001133)
