This investment is essential

**[01:56:02.780] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001141)  
to accelerate irrigation development,

**[01:56:04.780] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001142)  
restore and rehabilitate existing systems,

**[01:56:07.780] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001143)  
adapt climate-resilient technologies,

**[01:56:10.780] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001144)  
and contribute to countries' food security.

**[01:56:13.780] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001145)  
With your continued support,

**[01:56:14.780] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001146)  
NEA remains committed to providing efficient,

**[01:56:17.780] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001147)  
sustainable, and resilient irrigation systems

**[01:56:20.780] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001148)  
for our Filipino farmers.

**[01:56:22.780] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001149)  
Thank you very much,

**[01:56:24.780] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-001150)  
and we are ready to respond to your questions.

**[01:56:26.780] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001151)  
Thank you, Administrator Eddie Guillen.

**[01:56:34.290] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001152)  
May I remind our distinguished colleagues

**[01:56:45.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001153)  
that each member is allowed five minutes

**[01:56:50.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001154)  
for questions or interpolation,

**[01:56:54.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001155)  
excluding the responses of the resource persons.

**[01:56:59.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001156)  
When the bell sounds,

**[01:57:01.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001157)  
the member has 30 seconds

**[01:57:05.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001158)  
to conclude.

**[01:57:07.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001159)  
And also,

**[01:57:09.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001160)  
may I remind also my colleagues,

**[01:57:13.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001161)  
there will be a consultation on Thursday,

**[01:57:17.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001162)  
September 3,

**[01:57:19.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001163)  
where your parochial concern

**[01:57:22.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001164)  
may be addressed by the Department of Agriculture.

**[01:57:26.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001165)  
So that we don't have to wait

**[01:57:29.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001166)  
for the interpellation of the DA.

**[01:57:32.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001167)  
Please.

**[01:57:35.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001168)  
Consistent with the long-standing practice

**[01:57:39.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001169)  
of the House,

**[01:57:41.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001170)  
the Chair shall recognize minority,

**[01:57:46.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001171)  
and then independent,

**[01:57:49.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001172)  
and majority members

**[01:57:51.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001173)  
in an alternating manners

**[01:57:54.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001174)  
when practicable.

**[01:58:07.340] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001175)  
The Chair first recognize

**[01:58:09.340] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001176)  
the Honorable Representative Sara Ilago.

**[01:58:17.720] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-001177)  
Minority.

**[01:58:19.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001178)  
Maraming salamat

**[01:58:20.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001179)  
at magandang araw sa lahat.

**[01:58:22.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001180)  
Mr. Chair,

**[01:58:23.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001181)  
dahil sa habagat at sunod-sunod na bagyo,

**[01:58:25.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001182)  
tinatayang nasa 2.5 billion pesos

**[01:58:28.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001183)  
ang agricultural damage.

**[01:58:29.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001184)  
At sa presentation nga ng DA,

**[01:58:31.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001185)  
sinabi nila na yung estimated damage and losses

**[01:58:34.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001186)  
from 2024 hanggang ngayong taon

**[01:58:37.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001187)  
ay nasa 114 billion pesos

**[01:58:39.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001188)  
na nakaka-apekto sa halos 3 million

**[01:58:41.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001189)  
na mga magsasakat may ngisda.

**[01:58:43.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001190)  
Ano po ang aksyon na ginagawa dito ng DA?

**[01:58:46.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001191)  
At paano makaka-apekto

**[01:58:47.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001192)  
yung lubusan na pagkabawas ng inyong pondo

**[01:58:50.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001193)  
para sa 2027

**[01:58:52.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001194)  
sa madalian na aksyon natin

**[01:58:54.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001195)  
para tulungan ang mga affected farmers and fisherfolk?

**[01:58:58.720] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001196)  
Mr. Chair,

**[01:59:04.850] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001197)  
Mr. Chair,

**[01:59:06.850] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001198)  
for next year, sa 2027,

**[01:59:09.850] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001199)  
mas nakahanda kami, no?

**[01:59:11.850] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001200)  
Nakausap, nagpulong kami kahapon actually

**[01:59:14.850] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001201)  
with the President,

**[01:59:16.850] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001202)  
and doon yung DBM and other government agencies.

**[01:59:19.850] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001203)  
And,

**[01:59:20.850] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001204)  
pagdating sa mga tulong sa tao

**[01:59:23.850] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001205)  
in terms of yung support sa immediate needs,

**[01:59:29.850] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001206)  
parang may kanya-kanya na kaming assignment, no?

**[01:59:33.850] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001207)  
Basically, DSW will take care of yung immediate needs

**[01:59:36.850] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001208)  
sa food and shelter.

**[01:59:38.850] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001209)  
DOLE will take care of yung rehabilitation.

**[01:59:43.850] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001210)  
Meanwhile, the DA,

**[01:59:46.850] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001211)  
we are, bigyan ng budget kami, of course,

**[01:59:49.850] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001212)  
sa QRF namin para mapabilis.

**[01:59:53.850] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001213)  
At nabigyan ng instruction ang DBM,

**[01:59:56.850] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001214)  
Mr. Chair, na pag nangyari ganyan,

**[02:00:00.000] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001226)  
hindi na maghihintay ng mahabang panahon para ma-release yung Quick Response Fund.

**[02:00:05.140] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001227)  
And basically, yung QRF namin will be used for yung replanting na tinatawag, no?

**[02:00:11.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001228)  
Pagdating sa seeds, tsaka yung abono na kailangan.

**[02:00:15.740] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001229)  
But meanwhile, we're still trying to shorten yung sa indemnification payment ng PCIC,

**[02:00:22.160] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001230)  
among others, tsaka yung mga loan programs na available like yung sa SURE natin for the affected areas, no?

**[02:00:31.940] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001231)  
Of course, meron din taming, for this year, meron din kami na may na-allocate din sa amin ng dole na certain amount

**[02:00:40.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001232)  
na pwede naming, lalo lang sa mga fisher folks na hindi makapaglaot dahil sa lakas ng habagat,

**[02:00:49.340] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001233)  
meron kaming, pwede kaming magbigay ng tupad konti.

**[02:00:52.160] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001234)  
Through dole, of course, no? Para doon sa mga, and others.

**[02:00:58.000] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001235)  
Then, doon sa El Niño response din, meron kami na may pasok na 6.7 billion na pondo ngayon

**[02:01:06.180] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001236)  
to be ready for the El Niño and that is one plus yung sa fuel issue.

**[02:01:12.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001237)  
So, half of that will be for fuel subsidy and the other half will be for a transport subsidy.

**[02:01:21.880] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001238)  
Yung para matulungan yung mga farmers and fisher folks na madala yung kanilang mga produkto at lower cost to the markets.

**[02:01:31.160] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001239)  
Among others, Mr. Chair.

**[02:01:33.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001240)  
Mr. Chair, may indemnification, meron din loans, no?

**[02:01:36.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001241)  
Pero pagdating sa mga subsidies, meron ba kayong naging proposal para may direkta na production subsidy

**[02:01:43.020] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001242)  
sa ating mga bumabangon na farmers at fisher folks?

**[02:01:45.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001243)  
Na-mention nyo din po yung role dito ng DSWD.

**[02:01:48.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001244)  
Meron na ba kayo ngayong figure?

**[02:01:51.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001245)  
Kung magkano?

**[02:01:51.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001246)  
O yung nakalaan para sa calamity compensation sa affected farmers and fisher folks?

**[02:01:57.400] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001247)  
Well, sa 2027, the idea is through this package is about 10 billion din.

**[02:02:05.360] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001248)  
Which partly will be under presidential assistance for farmers and fisher folks.

**[02:02:10.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001249)  
Plus yung QRF na pwede naming i-replenish, Mr. Chair.

**[02:02:14.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001250)  
Mr. Chair, yung presidential assistance kasi kulang na kulang.

**[02:02:18.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001251)  
Pagdating sa production subsidy, may mga proposals dito sa House.

**[02:02:21.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001252)  
Na 80,000 no kada magsasaka per cropping season.

**[02:02:26.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001253)  
Meron din pong nasa 30 to 40,000 kada mangingisda.

**[02:02:29.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001254)  
Ayan, kaya yan po dapat ay sa DA na mismo manggaling na ang kailangan ng mga magsasaka at mangingisda.

**[02:02:36.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001255)  
Hindi po dagdag na pa-utang, kundi dagdag na direkta ang subsidiya.

**[02:02:39.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001256)  
Ngayon naman, Mr. Chair, nais ko pong magtanong dun sa mga pagkakataon na napipilitang magtapon, magsira,

**[02:02:46.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001257)  
o iwan ng mga magsasaka yung kanilang ani.

**[02:02:48.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001258)  
Dahil wala pong bumibili.

**[02:02:50.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001259)  
Dahil napakababa.

**[02:02:51.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001260)  
O dahil kulang na kulang ang storage facilities.

**[02:02:55.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001261)  
At mahal din yung gastos pagdating sa logistics.

**[02:02:59.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001262)  
Lalo na po nitong pagsirit ng presyo ng langis.

**[02:03:03.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001263)  
Ilan po ba ang documented cases ng Department of Agriculture tungkol dito?

**[02:03:08.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001264)  
At meron ba kayong naging mekanismo para naman humaisal ba itong mga produkto ng ating mga magsasaka?

**[02:03:15.680] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001265)  
Mr. Chair, well recognized niyong ating mga proponente.

**[02:03:21.880] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001266)  
Ang problema sa, like recently dito sa Habagat, tumuhumal ang benta sa mga merkado sa ating mga palengke.

**[02:03:31.000] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001267)  
Kaya medyo tumuhumal din yung bentahan ng high value crops galing sa Benguet.

**[02:03:37.140] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001268)  
At ang nadesisyon na ito actually earlier this year na magtatayo tayo ng cold storage na 5 mega cold storage

**[02:03:50.820] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001269)  
at processing center dun sa lupa ng Benguet State University

**[02:03:56.300] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001270)  
as one of the solutions to have a 15 day buffer stocking area and processing for yung mga excess production natin.

**[02:04:07.140] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001271)  
Meanwhile, meron din tayong food hub na ipapabid na sa Clark with the 2026 gaan na 2 billion pesos

**[02:04:16.120] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001272)  
by next, ah this month, September, no?

**[02:04:19.380] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001273)  
So that will be completed.

**[02:04:20.820] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001274)  
So that will be complimentary plus yung tinatapos natin na FTI na 500 million na mega cold storage sa Taguig.

**[02:04:28.320] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001275)  
So that is part of the network na tinatayo natin for the high value crops.

**[02:04:33.160] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001276)  
At para nga meron tayong shock absorber at storage systems to address yung spikes or highs and lows ng production.

**[02:04:42.940] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001277)  
And of course, pati yung mga weather to address yung effects nito sa mga weather disturbances din.

**[02:04:50.820] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001278)  
So that we will have market stability among others.

**[02:04:54.300] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001279)  
Ah, Mr. Chair.

**[02:04:55.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001280)  
Mr. Chair, hingin po natin yung ulat ng DA pagdating dito sa pagpapayaman ng ating tulong pagdating sa logistics chain natin sa pagkain.

**[02:05:05.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001281)  
At dagdag na rin po yung matagal na nating panawagan.

**[02:05:08.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001282)  
Sana naman yung DA na rin yung manguna sa pagtatanggal ng VAT at excise tax sa langis.

**[02:05:14.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001283)  
Dahil kayo mismo yung pinaka nakakaalam, di ba?

**[02:05:18.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001284)  
Kung gaano po kailangan-kailangan ng mga magsasakal.

**[02:05:20.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001285)  
At may isda ang krudo pagdating sa kanilang produksyon.

**[02:05:24.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001286)  
Ngayon naman, Mr. Chair, nananatili pa rin ho ang Pilipinas na number one na rice importer sa buong mundo.

**[02:05:31.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001287)  
At sa harap ngayon ng bantanang super-aluminium, climate emergency, mga gera, at mga potential na disruption sa ating global rice supply.

**[02:05:40.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001288)  
Hindi ba delikado para sa ating food security na ang Pilipinas po ang laki pa rin ng pag-asa ng kanyang dependence sa ibang bansa para sa ating panggawa?

**[02:05:50.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001289)  
Ano na yung pagkain? Ano ho ang aksyon ng DA para rito?

**[02:05:54.440] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001290)  
Mr. Chair, definitely, ang pangarap po natin ay mabawasan yung ating dependency sa importasyon.

**[02:06:01.600] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001291)  
At napaka-importante hung ma-suportahan natin ang NIA sa pag-irrigate ng more of our flatlands na tinatawag, lalo na sa rice production.

**[02:06:14.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001292)  
Meanwhile, tuloy-tuloy naman yung ating development of the...

**[02:06:19.060] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001293)  
for use ng ating development ng inbred seeds natin at pati yung locally produced hybrid seeds natin.

**[02:06:27.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001294)  
At actually, unting-unting tumataas ang ating yield per hectare base sa datos.

**[02:06:34.380] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001295)  
So, we're in the right path but we really need more irrigated land.

**[02:06:38.520] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001296)  
As far as food security natin for rice for year 2026, I'm happy to announce na we are quite secure.

**[02:06:49.060] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001297)  
Sa ating rice requirements till end of 2026.

**[02:06:54.920] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001298)  
At that is because yung...

**[02:06:58.980] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001299)  
As of last week, 3.8 million tons na ang na-import ng ating bansa dahil ito ay liberalized.

**[02:07:06.320] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001300)  
But pumasok na rin ho, nag-umpisa na ho ang harvest season din natin ngayon sa ating mga palayan.

**[02:07:13.900] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001301)  
Like sa sarili kong farm sa Nueva Ecija, nag-harvest na last week.

**[02:07:17.940] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001302)  
So far, maayos naman.

**[02:07:19.060] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001303)  
At of course, it can be better sana but then basang-basa kasi palay.

**[02:07:25.680] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001304)  
Pero we are seeing na although may tama tayo na about 60,000 hectares as of yesterday,

**[02:07:34.880] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001305)  
but mukhang maayos pa rin yung harvest natin itong harvest season na to.

**[02:07:42.720] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001306)  
So that should carry us over until end of the year.

**[02:07:45.540] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-001307)  
Mr. Chair.

**[02:07:46.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001308)  
Mr. Chair, hindi lang sa bigas mataas ang ating food importation.

**[02:07:49.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001309)  
Kung sa taon 1990 hanggang 2022, ang bigas po lumaki ng from 9 to 23 percent.

**[02:07:56.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001310)  
Sa mais, 6.7 to 10.1 percent.

**[02:07:59.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001311)  
Sa beef, 8.5 to 50 percent.

**[02:08:02.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001312)  
Sa pork, 0.1 to 30.4 percent.

**[02:08:05.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001313)  
Chicken, 0.1 to 11.9.

**[02:08:07.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001314)  
At sa galunggong po, maski galunggong, ang taas po ng ating import dependence na ngayon ay nasa 23.4 percent.

**[02:08:13.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001315)  
Sa DA na mismo nang galing po last year, Mr. Chair, na hindi po para sa Pilipinas ang liberalization.

**[02:08:19.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001316)  
Ang full liberalization.

**[02:08:21.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001317)  
Kaya ang dapat yung budget natin ay sumunod din doon sa kagustuhan natin na mabaliktad yung liberalization ng agriculture.

**[02:08:29.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001318)  
Pero ang nakikita po natin ngayon sa budget, puro budget cut po ang national rice program, national corn program.

**[02:08:35.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-001319)  
Paano po natin maabot ang pagpapayaman ng lokal na agrikultura kung puro budget cut ang nakita natin ngayon sa sektor na agrikultura?

**[02:58:23.170] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002362)  
from the National Irrigation Division Manager

**[02:58:25.630] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002363)

**[02:58:27.410] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002364)  
Magandang hapon po.

**[02:58:29.510] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002365)  
So, one of the key criteria

**[02:58:30.850] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002366)

**[02:58:33.070] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002367)

**[02:58:35.050] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002368)

**[02:58:36.310] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002369)

**[02:58:39.790] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002371)

**[02:58:40.610] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002372)

**[02:58:41.610] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002373)

**[02:58:43.170] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002374)  
So, ang pinakamalaki po namin

**[02:58:44.510] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002375)

**[02:58:45.510] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002376)

**[02:58:46.930] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002377)

**[02:58:47.630] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002378)

**[02:58:49.310] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002379)  
At the same time,

**[02:58:50.450] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002380)  
we also take into account

**[02:58:51.590] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002381)

**[02:58:52.630] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002382)  
ng aming administrator,

**[02:58:53.830] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002383)  
we have the

**[02:58:54.410] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002384)  
Regional Irrigation Master Plan.

**[02:58:56.570] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002385)  
So, sa Regional Irrigation Master Plan,

**[02:58:58.330] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002386)  
it was also coordinated

**[02:58:59.470] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002387)

**[02:59:01.290] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002388)  
Meron po tayong

**[02:59:02.250] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002389)

**[02:59:04.010] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002390)

**[02:59:05.770] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002391)  
So, yun po,

**[02:59:06.270] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002392)  
ang mas priority natin.

**[02:59:08.110] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002393)  
Then, second,

**[02:59:08.990] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002394)  
of course, we need to look.

**[02:59:10.210] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002395)  
Yun nga po, malit po

**[02:59:10.990] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002396)  
ang ating fiscal space.

**[02:59:12.310] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002397)  
So, looking with the fiscal space,

**[02:59:13.870] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002398)  
we look into the projects

**[02:59:15.270] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002399)  
whether it is

**[02:59:16.210] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002400)

**[02:59:18.110] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002401)  
So, this is aligned

**[02:59:19.450] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002402)  
with the DEP-DEV criteria.

**[02:59:21.470] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002403)  
Next, we also incorporate

**[02:59:23.350] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002404)  
the stage of development.

**[02:59:25.290] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002405)

**[02:59:27.050] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002406)

**[02:59:27.750] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002407)

**[02:59:29.010] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002408)

**[02:59:30.470] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002409)  
Then, next,

**[02:59:31.350] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002410)  
we also incorporate

**[02:59:32.770] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002411)  
yun po,

**[02:59:33.490] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002412)  
government prioritization project.

**[02:59:36.270] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002413)  
So, kung meron po bang

**[02:59:38.710] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002414)

**[02:59:40.030] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002415)  
PIP, sa TRIP,

**[02:59:42.010] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002416)  
it's also included in the NIMP.

**[02:59:45.270] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002417)  
Next is, of course,

**[02:59:46.890] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002418)  
hindi naman po natin pwedeng

**[02:59:48.070] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002419)  
ipagpasabahala or parang

**[02:59:50.210] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002420)  
wagpansinin

**[02:59:51.650] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002421)  
yun pong mga requirements natin

**[02:59:54.070] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002422)  
when it comes to the statutory

**[02:59:56.330] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002423)

**[02:59:57.690] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002424)

**[03:00:00.000] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002425)  
And then lastly, we also incorporate may mas mataas po ang weight, may points din po when it is in the GEDA or yung geographically isolated and disadvantaged areas po.

**[03:00:11.940] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002426)  
And recently, of course, with the drought incidences, we also incorporated it for the prioritization process.

**[03:00:19.500] NIA Project Planning Division Manager Robert Mark Revillame** (asr-002427)  
Mr. Chair, salamat po.

**[03:00:20.780] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002428)

**[03:00:21.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002429)  
Sir, alam niyo po, pag pinakikinggan ko explanation, dapat kung susundin lang yung master plan, lahat ng irrigable land, meron ng irigasyon eh.

**[03:00:30.920] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002430)  
Alam niyo po, 1963 pa, itinayo yung NIA, kalahating siglo na, hindi pa rin na bibigyan ng irigasyon yung mga lugar na dapat bigyan ng irigasyon.

**[03:00:42.230] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002431)  
Alam niyo po kung anong sinasabi, minumura po ng mga magsasaka yung NIA sa ground.

**[03:00:48.090] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002432)  
Magtatayo kayo ng irigasyon, wala naman tubig.

**[03:00:51.430] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002433)  
Doon sa may sobrang tubig.

**[03:00:53.150] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002434)  
Hindi naman kayo gagawa ng proyekto para ma-maximize yung sobrang tubig, administrator.

**[03:01:01.260] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002435)  
So, kalahating siglo na ito, Mr. Chair, ang NIA, pinupunto ko po dito, paulit-ulit na lang na problema, yung irigasyon.

**[03:01:09.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002436)  
Tapos, sasabihin tayo, meron namang master plan, eh kailan pa matatapos yung master plan na yan?

**[03:01:14.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002437)  
Patay na lahat ng magsasaka, wala pa rin irigasyon.

**[03:01:18.260] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002438)  
So, papaano po ang plano ng NIA para tupdin?

**[03:01:21.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002439)  
Pero yun yung every year, 1% po ang naidadagdag. Tama po ba?

**[03:01:25.460] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002440)  
Eh napakababa nun, Mr. Chair, abutin tayo kupong-kupong dito.

**[03:01:28.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002441)  
Hindi pa ako buhay, problema na ng ano yan, mga magsasaka yan eh.

**[03:01:31.940] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002442)  
Eh baka tumanda na ako at lahat dito, hindi pa rin matatapos yung problema ng irigasyon.

**[03:01:35.820] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002443)  
Anong plano ng NIA, Mr. Chair?

**[03:01:37.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002444)  
Admin, please answer.

**[03:01:38.260] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002445)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[03:01:39.220] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002446)  
Kaya nga po, napansin nyo sa aming proposal kanina, para sana mayangat natin ng malaki ang ating cover aids,

**[03:01:46.460] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002447)  
ay 250 billion mahigit ang budget na nire-request po natin, Mr. Chair.

**[03:01:51.360] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002448)  
Kaya lang, dahil sa fiscal limitation,

**[03:01:55.460] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002449)  
kaya lang tayo ng 46 billion.

**[03:01:57.860] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002450)  
So kailangan po natin doon pagkasyahin.

**[03:02:00.240] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002451)  
At kung napansin po ninyo, Mr. Chair, sinabit naman namin yung aming budget proposal,

**[03:02:06.080] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002452)  
karamihan po doon, ongoing, tier 1.

**[03:02:09.440] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002453)  
Hindi po kami makakapagpasok halos ng bagong mga projects.

**[03:02:12.900] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002454)  
Kaya tinatapos lamang po ng NIA yung mga ongoing projects po natin.

**[03:02:17.540] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002455)  
So kami naman po ay matindi po ang aming kriteria ngayon.

**[03:02:21.880] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002456)  
Ano po? Bago namin ponduhan ang isang project.

**[03:02:25.460] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002457)  
Kailangan sumunod siya doon sa aming kriteriya.

**[03:02:29.320] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002458)  
Right project, right price, right quality.

**[03:02:31.880] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002459)  
Sa right project po, ang kriteriya namin dyan, tao, lupa, tubig.

**[03:02:36.660] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002460)  
May request ba ang mga tao?

**[03:02:38.240] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002461)  
Dahil baka lagyan mo ng patubig, wala naman magsasaka.

**[03:02:40.720] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002462)  
At ang NIA po kasi, very unique yung aming paggawa ng pundo, project.

**[03:02:45.400] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002463)  
Dahil after po ng project, kailangan namin i-turn over sa ating mga farmers yung nagawang proyekto

**[03:02:51.300] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002464)  
para naman sa operation and maintenance.

**[03:02:53.680] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002465)  
Ganon po yung sa NIA.

**[03:02:54.600] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002466)  
So, kailangan.

**[03:02:55.460] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002467)  
Kailangan may tao, tubig. Meron bang source ng tubig?

**[03:02:58.660] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002468)  
At of course, pangatlo, yung lupa nga.

**[03:03:04.960] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002469)  
Ilang hectares ba ito?

**[03:03:07.900] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002470)  
Feasible ba ito na gawin doon sa gagastusin?

**[03:03:11.620] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002471)  
So, again, Mr. Chair, madali po natin sabihin na tapos na sana po ito.

**[03:03:18.340] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002472)  
Pero we should put our money where our mouth is.

**[03:03:21.280] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002473)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair, doon sa sinabi ni Administrator.

**[03:03:23.880] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002474)  
Kasi babanggitin na naman.

**[03:03:25.460] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002475)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair, doon sa sinabi ni Administrator.

**[03:03:26.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002476)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair, doon sa sinabi ni Administrator.

**[03:03:26.540] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002477)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair, doon sa sinabi ni Administrator.

**[03:03:26.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002478)  
Ngayon, umihingi po kayo ng 46 billion.

**[03:03:29.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002479)  
Alam niyo po, yung People's Budget Coalition, flinag kayo, NIA.

**[03:03:34.400] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002480)  
Kayo ay merong recurring pattern of anomalous infrastructure insertions

**[03:03:39.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002481)  
in the same category as DPWH flood control corruption controversy.

**[03:03:47.020] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002482)  
Kasi wala namang mga public project level document trail sa mga NIA project.

**[03:03:53.540] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002483)  
Paano niyo po i-explain ito?

**[03:03:55.460] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002484)  
Yung tagging, yung transparency sa mga NIA projects, Mr. Chair?

**[03:04:01.420] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002485)  
Admin, please answer.

**[03:04:04.140] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002486)  
Ang project po natin ay nag-undergo po ito ng mga technical validation,

**[03:04:11.020] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002487)  
prioritization, costing, and assessment of implementation readiness na po.

**[03:04:16.300] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002488)  
Ang project po, lahat po ng project na NIA,

**[03:04:20.100] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002489)  
ay subject to DBM evaluation and budget rules,

**[03:04:24.080] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002490)  
yung governing procurement laws,

**[03:04:26.680] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002491)  
COA auditing requirements,

**[03:04:28.680] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002492)  
yung approved engineering plans and technical specification,

**[03:04:32.060] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002493)  
environmental and other regulatory requirements,

**[03:04:35.160] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002494)  
monitoring and reporting to Congress.

**[03:04:37.380] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002495)  
Nagre-report din po kami sa Congress.

**[03:04:39.460] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002496)  
And of course, yung aming,

**[03:04:41.100] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002497)  
strict po kami doon sa tinatawag naming right project, right price, right quality.

**[03:04:46.860] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002498)  
Kaya po, unfair, yung NIA po, kung titignan niyo,

**[03:04:50.760] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002499)  
wala naman po kami na i-report na cost projects.

**[03:04:54.080] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002500)  
Wala naman pong substandard projects na i-report, ano po.

**[03:04:58.280] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002501)  
In fact, noong nakaraan po, ay ininbita namin ang Senado,

**[03:05:05.320] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002502)  
ang pamunuan ng Senado, para po personal napuntahan at inspeksyonin

**[03:05:09.580] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002503)  
ang construction methodology, ang implementation ng project ng NIA,

**[03:05:15.000] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002504)  
at kausapin mismo yung ating mga magsasaka kung meron silang reklamo sa NIA.

**[03:05:22.200] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002505)  
E ito ang tuwa naman po ang pamunuan.

**[03:05:24.080] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002506)  
So, I would like to extend the same invitation to the House.

**[03:05:28.720] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002507)  
Puntahan po natin, pasyalan natin ang mga proyekto ng NIA.

**[03:05:32.800] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002508)  
At para po, alam niyo po kasi ito, Mr. Chair,

**[03:05:36.100] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002509)  
importante po kasi ito na hindi po ito kasi para sa ahensya namin,

**[03:05:41.380] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002510)  
ang hinihingi namin tulong.

**[03:05:43.520] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002511)  
Nakita naman po natin ang limited budget po ng NIA.

**[03:05:47.520] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002512)  
Uulitin ko po, nagpapasalamat kami sa Congreso,

**[03:05:50.700] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002513)  
dahil for the past three years, tinadagdagan niyo ang budget namin.

**[03:05:54.080] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002514)  
Pero ang appeal namin kasi dito, it's not for our agency,

**[03:05:58.520] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002515)  
pero para po ito sa ating mga magsasaka.

**[03:06:00.760] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002516)  
Tama po lahat ang concern ng ating mga congressman.

**[03:06:04.120] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002517)  
Kailangan po natin lahat ang irigasyon, pero kailangan po natin magtulungan dito.

**[03:06:09.040] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002518)  
Hindi mo ako pwedeng paghanapan po ng isang napakalaking accomplishment,

**[03:06:14.860] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002519)  
pero napakaliit naman yung aming allocation.

**[03:06:17.460] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002520)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Chair.

**[03:06:18.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002521)  
Mr. Administrator, huwag niyo naman ibatod dito sa Congreso.

**[03:06:24.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002522)  
Pabalik mo dito at admin eh.

**[03:06:26.280] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002523)  
Sa Congress yung problema eh.

**[03:06:28.400] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002524)  
Sinasabi nyo, pinagmamalaki nyo, lakay yung substandard, lakay yung mga ghost project.

**[03:06:32.180] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002525)  
E iba naman ang sinasabi ng mga magsasaka on the ground eh.

**[03:06:36.280] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002526)  
E sasabi mo, hindi totoo, Administrator.

**[03:06:38.700] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002527)  
Pero may disconnect sa kung anong nire-report ninyo sa nangyayari sa mga magsasaka on the ground.

**[03:06:44.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002528)  
Kaya huwag niyo sisihin pabalik dito sa Congreso.

**[03:06:46.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002529)  
Kaya nga tayo nagkakaroon ng hearing para mabusisi natin yung budget ninyo, yung inihingi ninyo.

**[03:06:51.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002530)  
Sinasabi nyo, limited fiscal space.

**[03:06:54.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002531)  
Tignan natin nasaan yung implementation gaps.

**[03:06:56.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002532)  
Tama ba yun, Administrator?

**[03:06:58.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002533)  
O kaya huwag niyo pong masamain, Administrator, kasi parang may namasama nyo eh.

**[03:07:02.280] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002534)  
Hindi po, Mr. Chair.

**[03:07:05.260] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002535)  
Actually, gusto ko lang liwanagin.

**[03:07:07.620] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002536)  
Hindi ko po ipinapasa sa Congreso ang problema ng...

**[03:07:11.100] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002537)  
Huwag ko, nagpapasalamat po ako sa inyong mga tulong po sa amin for the past three years.

**[03:07:15.820] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002538)  
Dahil makikita po for the record, dinadagdagan po talaga ng Congreso yung aming budget.

**[03:07:21.620] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002539)  
You have the power of the purse.

**[03:07:24.080] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002540)  
Mr. Chair.

**[03:07:25.520] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002541)  
At doon po, nag-a-appeal ang NIA ng tulong.

**[03:07:29.600] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002542)  
Hindi po namin binabato po ang sisi po sa Congreso.

**[03:07:34.060] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002543)  
Bagkos, humihingo po kami ng tulong, Mr. Chair.

**[03:07:37.020] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002544)  
Mr. Chair, makikihingi na lang po ako ng NIA Irrigation Projects under Agrarian Reform Fund listahan po.

**[03:07:44.700] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002545)  
At yung guidelines dito sa ARF, Mr. Chair.

**[03:07:49.540] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002546)  
Sana po maibigay agad ng NIA to Mr. Chair.

**[03:07:52.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002547)  
Will the NIA commit to...

**[03:07:54.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002548)  
Will that, Mr. Chair?

**[03:07:56.200] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-002549)  
Yes, Mr. Chair. We will submit it as of po. Thank you.

**[03:07:59.580] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002550)  
Okay po. Mr. Chair, baubos na oras ko. Pero may ilang katanungan lang po ako sa DA ulit.

**[03:08:05.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002551)  
Partikular naman po ito sa Coco Levy Fund, Sekretary.

**[03:08:09.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002552)  
Noong nag-run po, nagkaroon ng committee hearing dito.

**[03:08:13.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002553)  
Inaamendahan po yung batas.

**[03:08:15.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002554)  
Doon po sa batas, ang gusto po ng mga magnunyog, Section 10 particularly, maisama sila sa Trust Fund Management Committee.

**[03:08:24.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002555)  
Doon po ang sabi ng Department of Finance.

**[03:08:26.480] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002556)  
Kaya nagagalit po ako dyan sa Department of Finance.

**[03:08:28.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002557)  
Sabi, eh hindi naman daw dapat kailangan yung mga magnunyog kasama sa Trust Fund Management Committee.

**[03:08:35.300] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002558)  
Eh laking kalukuhan nun, Sekretary.

**[03:08:38.160] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002559)  
Papaanong hindi isasama yung mga magnunyog doon sa Trust Fund Management Committee

**[03:08:42.120] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002560)  
kung yung Coco Levy Fund, pera ng mga magnunyog, ipinagkatiwala lang sa gobyerno.

**[03:08:49.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002561)  
Gusto ko pong malaman muli, makuha, ano po ang posisyon ng Department of Agriculture.

**[03:08:54.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002562)  
ng PCA po, doon sa pag-amyenda particularly on Section 10

**[03:08:59.260] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002563)  
na dapat isama yung farmers representatives, yung mga magnunyog sa Trust Fund Management Committee.

**[03:09:05.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002564)  
Mr. Secretary, please answer.

**[03:09:07.480] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002565)  
Mr. Chair, as far as the DA is concerned,

**[03:09:10.680] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002566)  
palagi namin kasama ang aming stakeholders, lalo na ang aming mga farmers sa lahat ng aming plano.

**[03:09:18.920] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002567)  
So, definitely the DA's position is...

**[03:09:24.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002568)  
Isa lang kami ng mga magsasaka.

**[03:09:27.540] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002569)  
So, mas magandang kasama sila.

**[03:09:28.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002570)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Chair, doon po sa assurance.

**[03:09:31.540] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002571)  
Buti pa ang Department of Agriculture.

**[03:09:34.660] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002572)  
May categorical position.

**[03:09:37.020] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002573)  
Yung Department of Finance, mga luko-loko yung mga nandun eh.

**[03:09:40.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002574)  
Pasensya, Mr. Chair, but I have to say this.

**[03:09:44.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002575)  
Pero yung pera nila, ayaw isama yung mga magnunyog sa Trust Fund Management Committee.

**[03:09:47.520] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002576)  
And I am very happy to hear that from our Secretary sa DA.

**[03:09:51.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002577)  
Nakasangga ang DA ng mga magnunyog natin.

**[03:09:53.540] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-002578)  
With that, Mr. Chair, second round po ako mamaya. Palista na lang po ako.

**[03:09:57.000] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-002579)  
Okay, thank you.

**[03:09:59.720] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-002580)  
The Chair recognizes Rep. Marlo Bancoro from the majority.

**[03:10:08.600] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-002581)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:10:10.760] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-002582)  
Good afternoon, Secretary Laurel. Good afternoon, everyone.

**[03:10:14.580] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-002583)  
Unang-una, Secretary Laurel, thank you very much doon sa commitment natin sa FISPORT.

**[03:10:19.540] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-002584)  
FISPORT.

**[03:10:20.940] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-002585)  
Nabigyan po tayo na ng three-year...

**[03:10:23.540] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-002586)  
...implementation in the amount of 150 million.

**[03:10:26.920] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-002587)  
I just want to make it clear na sana po hindi talaga maputol kasi three-year do eh.

**[03:10:33.360] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-002588)  
So for the next year, may 60 million and that's succeeding year 2028-67.

**[03:10:39.060] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-002589)  
Kasi sayang rin yun yung plano natin na expansion kung hindi maputol.

**[03:10:42.180] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002590)  
Yes, Mr. Chair. Nasa plano na ho yan, Mr. Chair.

**[03:10:45.180] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-002591)  
Thank you very much, Secretary.

**[03:10:46.860] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-002592)  
Second is salamat rin sa yung Hatchery, Mega Hatchery.

**[03:10:51.580] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-002593)  
And I am looking forward to...

**[03:10:53.540] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-002594)  
...see you some of these days para makita mo, ano, one of these days, I mean, para makita mo talaga yung lugar kung saan itatayo natin yung Mega Hatchery.

**[03:11:10.530] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-002595)  
Ang pangatlo ko, Secretary Laurel, na problema natin, yung FMR.

**[03:11:19.050] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-002596)  
Last year, sa NEP, nabigyan po tayo ng apat kasi yung lugar namin, agricultural...

**[03:11:25.530] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-002597)  
...province kami. So, in fact, yung na-validate natin is more than 200 farm-to-market roads.

**[03:11:33.370] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-002598)  
Apat lang yung naibigay sa atin. Unfortunately, nung lumabas ang GAA, dalawa ang nawala.

**[03:11:40.530] Rep. Marlo C. Bancoro** (asr-002599)

tatlong industriya lang

I'd like to ask from you.

Yung palm oil, banana,

and of course, the sugar industry.

**[03:20:11.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002791)

**[03:20:17.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002793)

**[03:20:25.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002796)

**[03:20:26.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002797)

**[03:20:29.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002798)  
Ganito na lang, Mr. Secretary.

**[03:20:33.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002799)

**[03:20:35.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002800)

**[03:20:40.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002802)

**[03:20:43.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002803)

**[03:20:47.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002804)

**[03:20:49.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002805)  
Palm Oil Development Authority.

**[03:20:51.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002806)

**[03:20:54.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002807)  
and I would just like to validate it.

**[03:20:55.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002808)

**[03:20:57.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002809)

**[03:21:01.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002810)  
And of course, kay Rosales.

**[03:21:04.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002811)  
Much appreciated.

**[03:21:05.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002812)  
Full support.

**[03:21:06.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002813)  
I know PCA.

**[03:21:07.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002814)  
Palm Oil, right now, is PCA.

**[03:21:10.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002815)

**[03:21:13.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002816)

**[03:21:16.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002817)

**[03:21:20.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002818)

**[03:21:25.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002819)

**[03:21:28.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002820)  
And of course, mas maganda kasi dalawang kongreses.

**[03:21:35.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002821)  
And certify as urgent for the palm oil industry,

**[03:21:40.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002822)

Mr. Chair, definitely, palm oil is an industry na

**[03:21:48.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002824)

**[03:21:52.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002825)

**[03:21:53.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002826)

**[03:21:57.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002827)

**[03:22:01.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002828)

**[03:22:06.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002829)

**[03:22:10.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002830)

**[03:22:12.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002831)

**[03:22:17.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002832)  
Nakausap po si Sen. Kiko Pangilinan.

**[03:22:20.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002833)  
At kanina rin, kausap-ausap kahapon.

**[03:22:23.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002834)  
Kausap ko rin si Kong Mark Inverga,

**[03:22:26.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002835)

**[03:22:29.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002836)

**[03:22:34.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002837)

**[03:22:38.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002838)

**[03:22:42.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002839)

**[03:22:47.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002840)

**[03:22:50.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002841)

**[03:22:53.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002842)

**[03:22:56.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002843)

**[03:22:59.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002844)

**[03:23:04.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002845)

**[03:23:09.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002846)

**[03:23:14.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002847)

**[03:23:21.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002848)

**[03:23:26.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002849)

**[03:23:30.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002850)

**[03:23:32.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002851)

**[03:23:37.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002852)

**[03:23:43.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002853)

**[03:23:46.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002854)

**[03:24:10.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002860)

**[03:24:16.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002861)

**[03:24:18.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002862)

**[03:24:33.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002866)

**[03:24:42.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002867)  
At the DA definitely supports the certification of the palm oil bill.

**[03:24:47.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-002868)  
As urgent, and I will make my official recommendation tomorrow to the President, Mr. Chair.

**[03:27:42.240] Deputy Speaker Raymond Democrito C. Mendoza** (asr-002910)

**[03:35:56.030] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-003035)

**[03:36:03.130] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-003038)  
Usec Dora Oliveros, Usec for Finance, Mr. Chair.

**[03:36:09.030] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-003039)  
That P20 billion that is allocated for the ANSEP

**[03:36:14.550] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-003040)  
will be taken for as long as we have a tariff collection

**[03:36:21.610] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-003041)  
from the P20 billion.

**[03:36:22.750] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-003042)  
So, as of today, we have checked, our tariff collection as certified by the Bureau of Customs

**[03:36:44.950] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-003043)  
is at P11.1 billion.

**[03:36:47.490] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-003044)  
So, if this is what will be collected, it will be added.

**[03:36:51.710] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-003045)  
But, if it is not collected, it will be added.

**[03:36:52.590] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-003046)  
So, if it is not collected, it will be added.

**[03:36:52.710] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-003047)  
But, until 2027, that is the extent of what we will release and use from the ANSEP.

**[03:37:01.070] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-003048)  
So, that is it.

**[03:37:02.090] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-003049)  
So, it depends on the tariff.

**[03:37:04.310] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-003050)  
Now, the question is, if it will be lacking,

**[03:37:08.190] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-003051)  
we have solutions in mind to do this.

**[03:37:13.990] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-003052)  
And I will turn it to you, Usec ASIS,

**[03:37:18.250] DA Undersecretary Nora C. Oliveros** (asr-003053)  
because he has suggestions about this.

**[03:37:20.690] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003054)  
Commission of the Secretary, Mr. Chair.

**[03:37:24.770] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003055)  
Actually, if we will compete, our total tariff collection under the ANSEP,

**[03:37:32.630] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003056)  
it can amount, currently, it's about P22 billion per year

**[03:37:38.750] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003057)  
because we have imported a lot of meat and meat products in the last four years.

**[03:37:45.570] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003058)  
Now, take note, Mr. Chair, that this is based on a P15, P20 billion.

**[03:37:50.530] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003059)  
This is based on a P25% tariff.

**[03:37:53.390] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003060)  
This is a reduced tariff.

**[03:37:55.670] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003061)  
But, in 2028, Mr. Chair, we will return to our previous tariff regime,

**[03:38:01.050] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003062)  
which is 25 and 40%.

**[03:38:03.250] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003063)  
So, therefore, we can see that the revenue collection is still high.

**[03:38:08.530] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003064)  
And then, the other thing that we're currently doing is,

**[03:38:11.950] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003065)  
there is a portion in the pork tariff called jowl.

**[03:38:18.050] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003066)  
That jowl, that is the tariff.

**[03:38:20.250] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003067)  
At that time, it was only 5%.

**[03:38:21.410] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003068)  
While it should be 15% and 25% as well.

**[03:38:26.030] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003069)  
So, we're working now with the Tariff Commission

**[03:38:28.310] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003070)  
to bring back that particular tariff line to its normal tariff.

**[03:38:32.750] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003071)  
And so, per our estimate, there is more than enough resources available

**[03:38:37.330] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003072)  
for the implementation of ANSEP,

**[03:38:40.230] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003073)  
beginning even next year and the years beyond that, Mr. Chair.

**[03:38:45.410] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003074)  
Thank you.

**[03:38:45.710] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003075)  
Thank you.

**[03:38:46.590] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003076)  
Can you explain what is jowl?

**[03:38:48.970] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003077)  
Jowl?

**[03:38:50.250] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003078)  
Is jowl like the pig's tail?

**[03:38:53.110] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003079)  
When it comes to the whole face,

**[03:39:00.330] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003080)  
what we're doing is the mustache, right?

**[03:39:02.270] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003081)  
When it comes to the whole head,

**[03:39:05.750] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003082)  
the tariff is only 5%.

**[03:39:09.730] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003083)  
But, when it comes to us, it's already removed.

**[03:39:13.690] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003084)  
It's not included in the face.

**[03:39:15.090] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003085)  
That's why we think,

**[03:39:17.970] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003086)  
pardon us for saying,

**[03:39:20.250] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003087)  
technicality that is in favor of those importing it.

**[03:39:25.170] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003088)  
So, therefore, we are working now with the Tariff Commission

**[03:39:28.830] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003089)  
to take that loop out.

**[03:39:31.010] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003090)  
Because what we have seen is that we will get around 4 billion pesos

**[03:39:36.610] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003091)  
additional revenue

**[03:39:38.330] DA Undersecretary Asis G. Perez** (asr-003092)  
if we can arrange the classification of jowl.

**[03:39:43.930] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003093)  
Mr. Chair, thank you.

**[03:39:44.650] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003094)

**[03:42:09.840] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003151)

**[03:42:12.840] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003152)

**[03:42:53.640] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003166)

**[03:43:04.000] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003170)

**[03:43:08.560] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003171)

**[03:43:11.760] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003172)

**[03:43:51.120] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003183)  
right-of-way, emergencies, calamities, etc.?

**[03:43:57.240] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003184)  
Well, Mr. Chair, based on our 2026 implementation experience,

**[03:44:02.360] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003185)  
making individual projects separate budget line items

**[03:44:05.640] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003186)  
created significant operational difficulties, Mr. Chair.

**[03:44:11.880] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003187)  
Once a project is specifically itemized,

**[03:44:14.320] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003188)  
NIA has very limited flexibility to adjust the allocation,

**[03:44:18.360] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003189)  
even when actual field conditions have changed.

**[03:44:20.480] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003190)  
Mr. Chair, for example, detailed engineering may reveal

**[03:44:23.960] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003191)  
a different scope of project cost, right-of-way problems,

**[03:44:27.440] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003192)  
permits, calamities, or other irrigation requirements

**[03:44:31.240] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003193)  
may require immediate reprioritization.

**[03:44:34.960] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003194)  
You know, a project initially proposed may subsequently

**[03:44:37.640] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003195)  
encounter technical and legal implementation constraints

**[03:44:41.080] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003196)  
because the appropriations are tied to specific line items.

**[03:44:45.960] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003197)  
Funds cannot be readily redirected to other validated

**[03:44:49.720] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003198)  
and implementation-ready projects without going through

**[03:44:53.440] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003199)  
a lengthy modification or realignment process, Mr. Chair.

**[03:44:59.160] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003200)  
So, do you mean to tell—

**[03:45:00.000] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003221)  
Tell me, Mr. Administrator, that the list that you submitted to DBM

**[03:45:06.300] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003222)  
is only for the purpose of planning and validation documents

**[03:45:10.400] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003223)  
subject to final costing and implementation readiness assessment

**[03:45:16.440] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003224)  
rather than as a rigid statutory enumeration of projects?

**[03:45:24.400] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003225)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[03:45:25.260] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003226)  
And, of course, you know, under the previous program-based system po kasi,

**[03:45:34.620] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003227)  
if one project encountered an unavoidable delay,

**[03:45:39.040] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003228)  
NIA had greater flexibility subject to applicable rules

**[03:45:42.240] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003229)  
to proceed with another validated, highly priority,

**[03:45:46.700] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003230)  
and implementable-ready project with the same program.

**[03:45:51.280] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003231)  
Kaya, you know, lumiit po.

**[03:45:54.640] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003232)  
So, humina po yung aming BUR, yung budget utilization rate po namin, Mr. Chair.

**[03:46:00.060] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003233)  
Because of the detailed list.

**[03:46:02.460] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003234)  
Napahirap po talaga ang implementation.

**[03:46:05.220] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003235)  
Do we have the representative of DBM?

**[03:46:10.530] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003236)  
Mr. Chairman, do we have a representative from DBM?

**[03:46:15.850] Rep. Joseph S. Tan** (asr-003237)  
Meron na ba tayo dyan? From DBM?

**[03:46:29.000] Rep. Joseph S. Tan** (asr-003238)  
Any representative from DBM?

**[03:46:40.170] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-003239)  
Yes.

**[03:46:42.270] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-003240)  
Chairman Ilagan?

**[03:46:46.530] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-003241)  
Director Ilagan?

**[03:46:59.060] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-003242)  
Or any DBM representative?

**[03:47:21.220] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003243)  
Anyway, well, Mr. Administrator,

**[03:47:29.530] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003244)  
did you or did you not recommend the detailed list

**[03:47:36.070] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003245)  
of individual NIA projects to DBM?

**[03:47:40.150] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003246)  
Or was it DBM-imposed?

**[03:47:44.610] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003247)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[03:47:46.250] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003248)  
The project list submitted during the budget preparation.

**[03:47:48.750] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003249)  
We submitted, Mr. Chair.

**[03:47:51.170] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003250)  
But it should be considered as a planning, prioritization, and validation document,

**[03:47:56.850] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003251)  
Your Honor.

**[03:47:59.150] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003252)  
You know, although projects undergo initial validation conditions,

**[03:48:03.010] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003253)  
may change.

**[03:48:03.910] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003254)  
Ano po ito eh?

**[03:48:04.730] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003255)  
Although naglagay po kami ng project list,

**[03:48:08.490] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003256)  
of course, kung ano man po yung sistema ng paglagay ng DBM sa NEP,

**[03:48:14.090] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003257)  
sa kanila pong discretion po yun, Mr. Chair.

**[03:48:17.430] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003258)  
So, ibig ba po?

**[03:48:18.750] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003259)  
Ibig po bang sabihin, yung buong listahan na isinumit eh,

**[03:48:23.030] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003260)  
hindi ho lahat doon ay implementation ready?

**[03:48:27.470] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003261)  
Kung susumain sa sinasinabi mo?

**[03:48:32.090] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003262)  
Yeah.

**[03:48:32.810] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003263)  
Yeah.

**[03:48:34.030] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003264)  
We submitted, Mr. Chair, again, but

**[03:48:36.410] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003265)  
for technical evaluation.

**[03:48:41.370] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003266)  
Kasi ina-evaluate po nila yan eh.

**[03:48:43.130] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003267)  
Yung aming ano.

**[03:48:43.810] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003268)  
And of course,

**[03:48:45.490] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003269)  
our implementation experience,

**[03:48:48.750] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003270)  
nevertheless, shows that incorporating the entire list

**[03:48:52.310] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003271)  
as rigid budget line items creates operational constraints po

**[03:48:56.850] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003272)  
and adversely affects timely implementation of our BUR.

**[03:49:00.850] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003273)  
There is no consistency, Mr. Chair, in our position.

**[03:49:04.230] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003274)  
We support submitting a complete and validated project list.

**[03:49:08.330] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003275)  
But we recommend that the appropriation itself remain program-based under PLEXI.

**[03:49:13.510] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003276)  
So, that is based on your experience in implementing the fiscal year 2026 project?

**[03:49:17.750] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003277)  
Yes.

**[03:49:18.750] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003278)  
Am I correct?

**[03:49:20.390] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003279)  
Napakahirap po mag-implement kasi talagang, you know,

**[03:49:24.190] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003280)  
pagka, for example, Mr. Chair, yung aming

**[03:49:27.930] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003281)  
mga soropop irrigation project,

**[03:49:31.030] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003282)  
pagka hindi na sapat yung tubig doon sa aming test run,

**[03:49:36.510] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003283)  
hindi na hoon amin basta yun maililipat.

**[03:49:38.670] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003284)  
Kasi may naka-JOT agpo siya.

**[03:49:41.970] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003285)  
So, base po sa sinabi nyo,

**[03:49:44.090] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003286)  
what, which budget structure

**[03:49:48.750] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003287)  
do you prefer?

**[03:49:50.310] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003288)  
A rigid itemized project listing in the GAA as annex

**[03:49:55.170] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003289)  
or a program-based appropriations under program expenditure classification

**[03:49:59.930] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003290)  
supported by a separately submitted, validated, and transparent project list?

**[03:50:05.950] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003291)  
Under PLEXI, Mr. Chair,

**[03:50:08.390] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003292)  
restoring the program-based PLEXI structure would provide...

**[03:50:11.190] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003293)  
Mr. Administrator, I'm not yet finished with my question.

**[03:50:12.670] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003294)  
I'm sorry.

**[03:50:13.970] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003295)  
Which will better serve our farmers?

**[03:50:16.010] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003296)  
Is it a rigid...

**[03:50:18.750] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003297)  
rigid list or itemized list or a PLEXI based on program and sub-program?

**[03:50:27.730] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003298)  
Well, doon po kami sa PLEXI, Mr. Chair, ano?

**[03:50:30.210] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003299)  
In fact, restoring the program-based PLEXI structure

**[03:50:33.210] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003300)  
would provide NIA with sufficient operational flexibility

**[03:50:37.450] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003301)  
while maintaining full transparency and accountability.

**[03:50:41.130] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003302)  
It would allow NIA po to address unavoidable delays,

**[03:50:44.030] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003303)  
utilize legitimate savings, respond to calamities,

**[03:50:48.750] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003304)  
guide emerging irrigation needs,

**[03:50:50.110] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003305)  
and prioritize po validated projects that are ready for immediate implementation.

**[03:50:56.110] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003306)  
It would also help improve our obligation and disbursement performance

**[03:50:59.690] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003307)  
and consequently po our BOR

**[03:51:02.890] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003308)  
without compromising congressional oversight and project-level transparency.

**[03:51:08.890] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003309)  
So you are willing to submit or you have already submitted,

**[03:51:12.430] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003310)  
as you mentioned while ago, to the appropriation to this committee

**[03:51:16.330] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003311)  
about restoring...

**[03:51:18.750] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003312)  
your budget into a program or sub-program-based instead of a detailed list?

**[03:51:24.610] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003313)  
Yes.

**[03:51:25.610] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003314)  
Did you submit?

**[03:51:26.610] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003315)  
So you are formally requesting for an amendment to your budget?

**[03:51:32.010] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003316)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[03:51:34.010] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003317)  
As to the budget classification?

**[03:51:36.010] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003318)  
Yes, we have submitted the letter to the speaker po at Sana po.

**[03:51:42.110] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003319)  
You know, we, again, we respectfully recommend that the detailed project list be submitted

**[03:51:46.690] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003320)  
as a supporting document.

**[03:51:48.590] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003321)  
Yes. We have submitted the letter. To the speaker po at Sana po. You know, we, again, we respectfully recommend that the detailed project list be submitted as a supporting document.

**[03:51:48.610] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003322)  
To the speaker po at Sana po. You know we, again, we respectfully recommend that the detailed project list be submitted as a supporting document.

**[03:51:49.070] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003323)  
For transparency and validation,

**[03:51:50.710] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003324)  
monitoring and oversight,

**[03:51:52.710] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003325)  
instead of incorporating every project as a rigid-line item

**[03:51:56.110] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003326)  
in the NEP or GAA Annex 4.

**[03:51:56.570] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003327)  
instead of incorporating every project as a rigid-line item in the NEP or GAA Annex 4.

**[03:51:58.810] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003328)  
Our objective, Mr. Chair, is not to reduce accountability,

**[03:52:03.730] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003329)  
Our objective is to ensure that the appropriated funds are implemented

**[03:52:07.630] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003330)  
efficiently, and untimed,

**[03:52:09.670] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003331)  
following NIA's strict policy of right project,

**[03:52:12.590] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003332)  
right price,

**[03:52:13.130] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003333)  
right quality.

**[03:52:14.150] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003334)  
Of course this approach will help NIA improve its BOR,

**[03:52:17.370] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003335)  
prioritize and govern.

**[03:52:18.490] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003336)  
going and implementation of the projects

**[03:52:20.210] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003337)  
and deliver irrigation services

**[03:52:22.550] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-003338)  
to our farmers more promptly.

**[03:52:26.130] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003339)  
So, Mr. Administrator, can you submit

**[03:52:28.450] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003340)

**[03:52:30.470] Rep. Allan U. Ty** (asr-003341)
