na 391 million.

**[03:54:32.590] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003396)  
No? At

**[03:54:33.810] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003397)  
ni-request na namin ito sa

**[03:54:35.890] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003398)  
Department of Budget and Management.

**[03:54:39.950] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003399)  
Waiting kami ng for approval.

**[03:54:42.790] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003400)  
No?

**[03:54:45.250] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003401)  
Yun ang main issue namin.

**[03:54:48.610] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003402)  
For that.

**[03:54:48.890] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003403)  
Kasi Mr. Chair,

**[03:54:50.490] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003404)  
sa ngayon po may

**[03:54:51.990] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003405)  
kaso na naman tayo nang iisip

**[03:54:53.930] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003406)  
sa Municipality of Pandan

**[03:54:55.830] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003407)  
and I think BIRAC.

**[03:54:57.930] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003408)  
So, ito po eh,

**[03:55:00.290] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003409)  
natatanong po

**[03:55:01.230] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003410)  
kasi may bago na naman tayong kaso.

**[03:55:05.690] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003411)  
Ito nga,

**[03:55:06.290] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003412)  
hindi pa natin nababayaran.

**[03:55:08.950] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003413)  
Kaya ito po na

**[03:55:10.170] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003414)  
may paabot po natin

**[03:55:11.690] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003415)  
sa inyo,

**[03:55:13.390] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003416)  
Mr. Secretary,

**[03:55:15.970] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003417)  
sa concern po ng aming mga kababayan.

**[03:55:19.130] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003418)  
Lalo na po

**[03:55:19.990] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003419)  
mga hog raiser namin.

**[03:55:20.950] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003420)  
Thank you.

**[03:55:24.070] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003421)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[03:55:26.210] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003422)  
We're pushing

**[03:55:27.290] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003423)  
na pondo ang kami nito.

**[03:55:29.450] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003424)  
Actually, meron ako mga other options

**[03:55:31.810] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003425)  
na on the table now

**[03:55:34.570] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003426)  
na baka pwedeng kapalit

**[03:55:37.030] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003427)  
ng cash na

**[03:55:39.150] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003428)  
indemnification kung papayag

**[03:55:40.830] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003429)  
man yung hog raisers.

**[03:55:42.650] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003430)  
One idea namin is

**[03:55:44.910] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003431)  
bigyan na sila ng growers

**[03:55:46.810] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003432)  
with feeds, no?

**[03:55:48.350] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003433)  
From yung next year sa budget

**[03:55:50.790] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003434)  
namin or

**[03:55:54.230] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003435)  
other alternatives kung sa

**[03:55:55.930] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003436)  
kaling hindi man ma-aprubahan yun sa

**[03:55:57.650] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003437)  
DBM na request namin.

**[03:56:00.830] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003438)  
So, yun yung

**[03:56:01.770] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003439)  
as of the moment, yun pa rin. At

**[03:56:03.310] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003440)  
another option din is kung

**[03:56:05.610] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003441)  
nagpapalagay kami ng

**[03:56:07.310] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003442)  
sa supplemental budget namin din

**[03:56:10.270] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003443)  
for 2027

**[03:56:12.110] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003444)  
dun sa

**[03:56:13.530] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003445)  
kagaya na nabanggit kanina na

**[03:56:15.990] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003446)  
madagdagan tong

**[03:56:17.150] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003447)  
indemnification fund kung hindi man makuha

**[03:56:19.970] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003448)  
namin sa QRF. Para

**[03:56:21.630] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003449)  
finally masettle na talaga ito.

**[03:56:23.450] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003450)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:56:25.930] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003451)  
Thank you. Sa atin po

**[03:56:28.690] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003452)  
sa PhilPayda,

**[03:56:31.810] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003453)  
alam nyo po,

**[03:56:33.030] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003454)  
naturingan kaming

**[03:56:34.110] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003455)  
abaka kapital ng Pilipinas,

**[03:56:37.730] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003456)  
nababahala po kami

**[03:56:39.130] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003457)  
sa patuloy na pagbaba ng

**[03:56:40.590] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003458)  
abaka production

**[03:56:41.550] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003459)  
sa buong bansa.

**[03:56:44.650] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003460)  
Bumababa ang production halos sa

**[03:56:46.330] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003461)  
22% from

**[03:56:47.970] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003462)  
2029 to 2025.

**[03:56:51.790] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003463)  
Sa katandaan naman po,

**[03:56:53.050] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003464)  
itong 2025,

**[03:56:55.930] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003465)  
about 44.7%.

**[03:56:59.430] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003466)  
So,

**[03:57:02.610] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003467)  
ano po ba ang dahilan ng pagbaba

**[03:57:06.780] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003468)  
ng ating abaka production?

**[03:57:09.360] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003469)  
Particularly na sa katandaan

**[03:57:11.300] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003470)  
is ano pong intervention natin

**[03:57:13.300] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003471)  
ngayon, taon na ito,

**[03:57:14.840] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003472)  
2027,

**[03:57:17.340] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003473)  
para may balik natin

**[03:57:18.780] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003474)  
ang sigla.

**[03:57:20.720] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003475)  
Kasi sa ngayon pa lang,

**[03:57:22.240] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003476)  
halos hindi na po natin masolusyonan

**[03:57:24.980] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003477)  
yung bunch it up.

**[03:57:27.020] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003478)  
Itong problema natin, palagi,

**[03:57:29.220] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003479)  
bunch it up,

**[03:57:30.840] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003480)  
bumababa na yung ano ng ano natin,

**[03:57:32.760] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003481)  
yung abaka natin,

**[03:57:35.120] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003482)  
hindi pa rin ito nasolusyonan.

**[03:57:37.200] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003483)  
And then, yung mga abakaleros

**[03:57:39.460] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003484)  
natin, parang tinatamad

**[03:57:41.640] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003485)  
na sila magtanim.

**[03:57:43.660] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003486)  
Dahil, dyan nga sa

**[03:57:45.380] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003487)  
sakit na yan, walang solusyon,

**[03:57:48.580] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003488)  
kaya ang iba,

**[03:57:49.820] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003489)  
nag-iiba na lang

**[03:57:51.580] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003490)  
ng tanim, na pananim, kung ano na lang,

**[03:57:53.400] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003491)  
hindi na minsan abaka.

**[03:57:54.840] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003492)  
At saka yung iba,

**[03:57:56.760] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003493)  
pumapasok na sa construction,

**[03:57:58.140] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003494)  
mumpunta na ng Maynila.

**[03:58:00.560] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003495)  
So, mawawala na po kaming abakaleros

**[03:58:02.580] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003496)  
pag ano na hindi natin ito nasolusyonan

**[03:58:04.620] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003497)  
sa madaling panahon po.

**[03:58:08.720] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003498)  
Mr. Chair,

**[03:58:09.800] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003499)  
basically, yung

**[03:58:11.140] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003500)  
bagyong tumama last year

**[03:58:13.680] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003501)  
dun sa Katanduanes,

**[03:58:15.220] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003502)  
talagang malaking

**[03:58:17.860] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003503)  
epekto nun sa abaka production

**[03:58:19.860] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003504)  
dahil yung matinamaan na puno na nawala,

**[03:58:22.180] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003505)  
hindi naman, in one year,

**[03:58:24.180] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003506)  
hindi naman makakabalik yung production nun.

**[03:58:26.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003507)  
But, meanwhile, I would ask

**[03:58:27.980] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003508)  
our field

**[03:58:29.840] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003509)  
fire the head to give

**[03:58:31.160] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003510)  
more detailed explanation

**[03:58:33.680] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003511)  
and kung ano man ng planos moving forward,

**[03:58:35.980] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-003512)  
Mr. Chair.

**[03:58:38.360] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003513)  
Mr. Chair, may I?

**[03:58:40.440] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003514)  
Actually, I know the problems.

**[03:58:42.180] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003515)  
I've seen it from day one.

**[03:58:44.120] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003516)  
We have so much abaka, actually.

**[03:58:46.080] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003517)  
The whole eastern border of the Philippines

**[03:58:48.000] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003518)  
has abaka.

**[03:58:49.440] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003519)  
From Aurora, Quezon, all the way to Mindanao.

**[03:58:52.420] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003520)  
And, of course, Katanduanes is one of the biggest.

**[03:58:54.700] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003521)  
In fact, by volume,

**[03:58:55.920] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003522)  
supposedly, Katanduanes can

**[03:58:57.840] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003523)  
fulfill the export needs of the Philippines.

**[03:58:59.840] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003524)  
Kaya, nakita lang natin,

**[03:59:01.760] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003525)  
the poorest farmers also are

**[03:59:03.820] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003526)  
second to the coconut farmers

**[03:59:06.200] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003527)  
or abaka farmers.

**[03:59:07.580] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003528)  
And one of the programs that we're doing now,

**[03:59:09.440] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003529)  
para po makamatulungan natin kagad sila,

**[03:59:11.520] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003530)  
is we are now teaching po the wives of farmers

**[03:59:14.300] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003531)  
how to make school bags.

**[03:59:16.740] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003532)  
Dahil alam naman po natin na

**[03:59:17.920] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003533)  
marami po sa atin bumibili naman

**[03:59:20.400] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003534)  
ng mga school bags every year.

**[03:59:22.920] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003535)  
And that creates a demand

**[03:59:24.100] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003536)  
para po doon sa mga asawa ng farmers

**[03:59:26.080] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003537)  
and the farmers. And, of course, we've created

**[03:59:27.840] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003538)  
a lot of products po.

**[03:59:29.840] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003539)  
In fact, looking at

**[03:59:31.900] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003540)  
long term,

**[03:59:33.300] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003541)  
kaya na tayo natin gumawa po ng mga plywood

**[03:59:35.700] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003542)  
made of abaka na dapat ituro

**[03:59:37.860] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003543)  
na sa industriya. We have already

**[03:59:39.760] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003544)  
added it to plastics. Since we

**[03:59:41.880] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003545)  
are importing about 600,000 metric

**[03:59:43.840] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003546)  
tons of plastic, if we can add

**[03:59:45.720] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003547)  
20% to that, then we can help a lot of

**[03:59:47.800] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003548)  
our farmers.

**[03:59:49.400] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003549)  
Sa textile po, meron na tayong abaka rin.

**[03:59:52.500] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003550)  
Ang pinakamaganda pong nagawa natin

**[03:59:53.840] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003551)  
ngayon, and I'd like to show you to everyone,

**[03:59:56.920] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003552)  
we have made

**[03:59:57.520] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003553)  
bulletproof vests po.

**[04:00:00.000] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003565)  
na-tested by AFP.

**[04:00:02.240] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003566)  
Na alam natin that we can have an industry

**[04:00:03.860] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003567)  
in the Philippines para po sa ating

**[04:00:05.280] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-003568)  
process of the Philippines. Thank you po.

**[04:00:08.560] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003569)  
And I hope,

**[04:00:09.700] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003570)  
Mr. Chair, na

**[04:00:10.920] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003571)  
yung represent ng ating

**[04:00:13.520] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003572)  
administrator

**[04:00:16.320] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003573)  
na ma, ano yan,

**[04:00:18.960] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003574)  
mag-materialize.

**[04:00:21.940] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003575)  
Kasi sa ngayon, talagang

**[04:00:23.520] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003576)  
parang nagsisimula pa rin naman tayo

**[04:00:25.740] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003577)  
sa UNO na naman eh.

**[04:00:28.320] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003578)  
Lalo na, mababa ang presyo

**[04:00:29.660] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003579)  
ng abaka. So,

**[04:00:31.600] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003580)  
wala pong, ano, wala pong

**[04:00:32.920] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003581)  
mainggan yung abakaleros

**[04:00:35.620] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003582)  
natin na tuloy-tuloy sila mag-produce

**[04:00:37.860] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003583)  
nito.

**[04:00:39.180] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003584)  
Kasi mababa nga.

**[04:00:41.620] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003585)  
Halos mag-apon sila ro'n eh.

**[04:00:43.620] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003586)  
Kulang pa nilang, ano,

**[04:00:44.740] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003587)  
araw-araw nilang pagkain ng

**[04:00:46.920] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003588)  
pamilya nila. So, dapat eh,

**[04:00:49.980] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003589)  
medyo gagawa tayo ng

**[04:00:51.180] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003590)  
paraan dito sa medyo

**[04:00:53.400] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003591)  
madaling panahon. Hindi pwedeng

**[04:00:54.880] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003592)  
ganito tayo palagi.

**[04:00:57.660] Rep. Eulogio "Leo" R. Rodriguez** (asr-003593)

**[05:04:56.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005280)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[05:04:58.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005281)  
Good afternoon.

**[05:05:01.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005282)  
Secretary Laurel, and good afternoon

**[05:05:04.660] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005283)  
to the DA family.

**[05:05:10.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005284)  
Mr. Chair,

**[05:05:11.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005285)  
DA itself

**[05:05:12.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005286)  
says we still have

**[05:05:14.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005287)  
a 55,050

**[05:05:17.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005288)  
kilometer FMR deficit.

**[05:05:21.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005289)  
Yep, the 2027

**[05:05:23.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005290)  
NEP cuts FMR funding

**[05:05:25.260] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005291)  
from 33 billion

**[05:05:26.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005292)  
in 2026 to only

**[05:05:28.660] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005293)  
16 billion, or

**[05:05:30.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005294)  
a 51.5% cut.

**[05:05:32.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005295)  
May I ask the Secretary, Mr. Chair? Isn't around 1,000 kilometers a year simply too little to make a meaningful dent?

**[05:05:43.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005296)  
Ano po ang implication nito sa agricultural productivity, production costs, post-harvest losses, market access, at income ng ating farmers?

**[05:05:55.740] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-005297)  
Mr. Chair, definitely kulang na kulang, Mr. Chair.

**[05:05:58.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005298)  
Meron po bang scientific database method for determining which area should be prioritized for FMRs?

**[05:06:06.420] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005299)  
Is it based on number of farmers, agricultural production, hectares served, commodities, connectivity to markets, poverty incidents, or other measurable criteria?

**[05:06:19.420] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-005300)  
Yes, meron tayong yung tinatawag na, Mr. Chair, na road influence area, poverty incidents, number of farmers or fisherfolk beneficiaries,

**[05:06:28.320] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-005301)  
distance to network links, present road conditions, climate change adaptation, average daily traffic, plus new production areas, Mr. Chair.

**[05:06:40.300] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005302)  
Kasama na din po dyan ba yung parang ROI? So when we invest in a farm-to-market road, yung economic activity based on our production, and yung balik po sa gobyerno?

**[05:06:56.420] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-005303)  
Well, in general...

**[05:06:58.320] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-005304)  
In general, we do that practice, but on real applications, hindi naman palahat ganon, kasi meron talagang tinatawag na missionary routes or missionary area na sobrang hirap, na konti production, pero ang laki ng potential.

**[05:07:15.820] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-005305)  
So you have to open it up, you have to put the road, and the benefits come later, lalo na sa poverty areas, Mr. Chair.

**[05:07:24.460] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005306)  
Can you tell us, Mr. Chair, ilan po bang kilometro?

**[05:07:28.320] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005307)  
Ilan po bang kilometro ang ideal na dapat fina-fund natin annually?

**[05:07:31.980] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-005308)  
Ah, kung akong tatanungin, Mr. Chair, maybe 60 billion a year?

**[05:07:42.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005309)  
60 billion for FMRs?

**[05:07:44.520] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-005310)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[05:07:45.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005311)  
And how many roads will that build? In terms of kilometers, ilan po, dun sa 55,000 kilometer deficit natin, ilan po ang magagawa nun sa isang taon?

**[05:07:54.880] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-005312)  
Ah, 4,000 kilometers.

**[05:07:56.860] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005313)  
So ideally, yan po ang bare minimum?

**[05:07:59.460] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005314)  
Bare minimum.

**[05:08:00.620] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-005315)  
Bare minimum yan para in 10, 12 years tapos, Mr. Chair.

**[05:08:06.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005316)  
Bakit po hindi yun ang, bakit po hindi yun ang hiningi natin sa, yun ang inindicate natin sa NEP?

**[05:08:15.820] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-005317)  
Fiscal space, Mr. Chair. We were told na ito lang ang fiscal space na binibigay sa amin sa initial NEP namin, Mr. Chair.

**[05:08:25.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005318)  
Mr. Chair, nabanggit niyo po, Mr. Secretary, na ang DA can,

**[05:08:32.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005319)  
construct FMRs at a lower cost than DPWH.

**[05:08:37.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005320)  
Kaya niyo po ba talaga yan? And currently, can DA demonstrate it in terms of its absorptive capacity?

**[05:08:45.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005321)  
And do you have the manpower to do it?

**[05:08:49.360] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-005322)  
For this year, we are, sa 33 billion na aming budget this year, we have allocated 30% muna ang gagawin ng DA.

**[05:09:02.000] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-005323)  
Dahil nga sa absorptive capacity and manpower restrictions.

**[05:09:05.680] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-005324)  
But next year, we are more ready. And of course, the year after, we will be more ready, Mr. Chair.

**[05:09:11.480] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005325)  
Thank you for that, Mr. Secretary and Mr. Chair.

**[05:09:15.620] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005326)  
To the NIA, Mr. Chair, may I ask the Administrator, we noticed a significant change in your fiscal year 2026 NIA budget.

**[05:09:30.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005327)  
Projects became much more specifically itemized.

**[05:09:33.400] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005328)  
And special provision number 8 was added, restricting modifications to the project list.

**[05:09:41.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005329)  
Did these changes, Mr. Chair, help NIA implement its budget better?

**[05:09:48.660] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005330)  
Or did they somehow contribute to the drop in your utilization?

**[05:09:53.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005331)  
What was the actual effect dun po sa inyong implementation?

**[05:10:00.490] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005332)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[05:10:02.150] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005333)  
Actually, bumaba po yung VOR natin.

**[05:10:05.070] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005334)  
Ano po, yung budget utilization rate natin ay bumaba po dahil napaka-strict, napaka-stringent po yung special provisions.

**[05:10:23.030] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005335)  
So, based on your experience, ano hong budget structure ang ire-recommend nyo sa Kongreso?

**[05:10:31.730] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005336)  
That will still preserve transparency and accountability.

**[05:10:35.070] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005337)  
Pero hindi po maaantala yung inyong implementation.

**[05:10:41.090] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005338)  
Well, Sir, Sir Chair, based on our experience, NIA prefers a program-based appropriation under PREC-C.

**[05:10:51.310] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005339)  
Yung gaya po ng dati, before 2026, yung 2025 and prior, ganoon po kasi dati.

**[05:10:58.230] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005340)  
But, of course, it will still be supported by detailed, validated, and transparent.

**[05:11:05.070] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005341)  
A project list submitted separately, of course, to Congress and DBM.

**[05:11:10.090] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005342)  
Magsasubmit pa rin po kami dun, ano po.

**[05:11:13.570] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005343)  
Ang maganda po dito, this arrangement, ano po, provides the best balance between transparency and accountability.

**[05:11:24.470] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005344)  
Congressional and DBM oversight.

**[05:11:28.190] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005345)  
Lalong-lalo na po yung implementation flexibility, ano po, and responsiveness to actual field conditions.

**[05:11:35.070] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005346)  
And yung timely and efficient utilization ng aming mga projects.

**[05:11:41.270] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005347)  
Actually, this does not give NIA unlimited authority po to replace projects.

**[05:11:47.510] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005348)  
Any adjustment must remain within the approved program and comply with budgeting, procurement, and auditing and reporting requirements.

**[05:11:56.270] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005349)  
So, ang sinasabi nyo po, based on your previous experience, bago po i-implement yung Special Provision No. 8,

**[05:12:05.070] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005350)  
wala po kayo naging problema sa utilization nyo.

**[05:12:08.150] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005351)  
At madali kayong, mas nagkaroon kayo ng elbow room para i-implement yung projects.

**[05:12:15.250] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005352)  
Yes, Mr. Chair. Tingnan nyo po, lalo na po ito.

**[05:12:19.210] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005353)  
You have to understand po yung situation ng NIA.

**[05:12:22.550] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005354)  
For previous years, minsan wala kaming QRF.

**[05:12:28.250] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005355)  
Minsan, ngayon, 300 po ang QRF po natin.

**[05:12:30.750] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005356)  
E papasok po tayo sa LNU.

**[05:12:33.750] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005357)  
Ngayon, minsan.

**[05:12:35.070] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005358)  
At pag may bumaha, bumagyo, merong na-wash out na irrigation canal namin, may standing crop,

**[05:12:41.850] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005359)  
halos hindi kami makagalaw eh.

**[05:12:43.270] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005360)  
Hindi namin magalaw ngayon yung aming repair and restoration.

**[05:12:47.090] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005361)  
So, minsan naman po kasi, may mga projects po ang NIA na although okay naman siya nung in-identify namin,

**[05:12:54.030] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005362)  
kaya lang nung pag-implement, nagkaroon ng problema.

**[05:12:57.250] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005363)  
Kagaya po ng aming mga solar pump irrigation projects na groundwater ang source.

**[05:13:03.250] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005364)  
Totoo nga pong nag-replace.

**[05:13:05.070] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005365)  
Nag-gumagawa kayo namin ng georesistivity.

**[05:13:07.510] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005366)  
May tubig, nakikita namin.

**[05:13:09.130] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005367)  
Kaya lang pag-drill mo, minsan may tatama ang bato na malalaki.

**[05:13:13.010] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005368)  
So, kailangan namin ilipat.

**[05:13:14.370] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005369)  
So, imagine our predicament kung naka-geotag yun.

**[05:13:18.170] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005370)  
Eh, kailangan mo lang ilipat.

**[05:13:19.570] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005371)  
Kasi magiging ghost project siya eh.

**[05:13:21.350] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005372)  
Pag wala siya dun sa georesistivity.

**[05:13:23.670] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005373)  
So, kailangan lamang po namin ng kaunting flexibility rin po.

**[05:13:27.650] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005374)  
So, ano ho naging limitasyon na nakastipulate dun sa special provision number 8 na nalimita kayo?

**[05:13:34.470] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005375)  
At hindi nyo nagamit, hindi naging usable yung project?

**[05:13:40.860] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005376)  
May coming experience, Mr. Chair.

**[05:13:42.600] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005377)  
Like, again, yung in-explain ko sa inyo kanina.

**[05:13:44.640] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005378)  
And, for some reason, alimbawa po yung budget namin ngayon, across the board, na nagbawas.

**[05:13:51.340] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005379)  
Kasi may naging problema sa highways.

**[05:13:53.600] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005380)  
Pati kami, nabawasan yung aming budget.

**[05:13:56.800] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005381)  
Pero hindi naman nabawasan yung nakalagay na line item.

**[05:13:59.420] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005382)  
For example, construction of 100 meters irrigation canal.

**[05:14:06.680] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005383)  
At, say, 10 million.

**[05:14:08.700] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005384)  
Ginawa po siyang 5 million.

**[05:14:09.980] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005385)  
The same pa rin yung nakalagay po sa line item eh.

**[05:14:14.000] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005386)  
So, ang dilemma namin po ngayon, paano mo i-implement yun?

**[05:14:17.100] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005387)  
Eh, binawasan ka ng budget, the same pa rin yun.

**[05:14:19.200] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005388)  
So, mas maganda po sana talaga kung nakaprexy lamang siya.

**[05:14:22.880] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005389)  
Para kahit bawasan kami ng budget,

**[05:14:24.780] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005390)  
ang gagawin lang namin, i-discontinue namin yung ibang projects.

**[05:14:29.120] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005391)  
Para, you know, kailangan po namin mag-early procurement eh.

**[05:14:33.680] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005392)  
With this, with what happened,

**[05:14:36.680] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005393)  
na bale wala po lahat yung early procurement namin

**[05:14:39.740] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005394)  
at wala kaming ginawa kundi mag-modify ng project.

**[05:14:42.820] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005395)  
So, lalong delayed na nga kami na wala nang early procurement,

**[05:14:46.260] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005396)  
wala pa kaming ginawa kundi mag-modify ng project.

**[05:14:48.420] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005397)  
So, affected po ang implementation ng project.

**[05:14:52.460] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005398)  
And one more thing, Mr. Chair, ano po,

**[05:14:54.000] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005399)  
hindi po kami kagaya ng DPWX na any time of the year

**[05:14:57.240] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005400)  
pwede mag-implement ng project.

**[05:14:59.100] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005401)  
Kami po,

**[05:15:00.000] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005402)  
Pagka-ungoing yung irrigation namin ng kanals sa mga palayan, hindi namin pwedeng galawin yung aming kanal.

**[05:15:06.880] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005403)  
So kailangan po namin i-timing talaga.

**[05:15:08.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005404)  
Habang ginagamit po yung tubig, hindi pwedeng gumawa?

**[05:15:11.820] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005405)  
Habang ginagamit po, hindi ka pwedeng gumawa sa irrigation kanal.

**[05:15:15.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005406)  
Can you submit a proposal to this committee kung ano po yung mga special provisions na gusto niyong i-indicate?

**[05:15:25.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005407)  
At ano yung proposal niyo in terms of yung, paano yung gusto niyong implementation para maintindihan po namin prior to plenary?

**[05:15:35.180] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005408)  
Yung dati po na recommendation, actually kahit itanong natin ang DBM po,

**[05:15:40.700] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005409)  
yung by prexy lamang po sana, program expenditure, ganun lang po sana ang natin.

**[05:15:45.980] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005410)  
Although yung line items namin, nandoon pa rin po, i-identify pa rin namin lahat, pero hindi siya naka-line item sa GAA.

**[05:15:53.280] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005411)  
Pag-release ng SARO, doon siya.

**[05:15:55.100] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005412)  
Nandoon siya. Para kung may changes, kaya nga po kami GOCC eh.

**[05:15:59.220] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005413)  
Because GOCCs are allowed po na i-change namin yung project namin through sa aming board.

**[05:16:07.060] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005414)  
Pag line agency ka, pupunta ka kay presidente.

**[05:16:09.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005415)  
Can you submit that in writing, Mr. Chair?

**[05:16:12.100] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005416)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[05:16:13.220] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005417)  
Tapos nabanggit niyo rin po, Mr. Chair, ni Administrator, na nabawasan kayo.

**[05:16:19.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005418)  
When an irrigation project po is designed and costed at a certain amount,

**[05:16:25.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005419)  
but Congress substantially cuts that particular line item,

**[05:16:30.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005420)  
ano po ang nangyayari doon sa project?

**[05:16:32.380] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005421)  
Can the remaining amount still deliver the intended functional output?

**[05:16:37.020] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005422)  
Gaganap pa rin po ba yung project?

**[05:16:38.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005423)  
Or ang nangyayari po ba ay if we end up with an incomplete irrigation project, hindi na natin ito magagamit?

**[05:16:47.380] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005424)  
Mr. Chair, just to be clear, hindi po kami magtuturo kung saan nabawasan.

**[05:16:55.100] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005425)  
Ang totoo naman yan, pag nandito kami sa Kongreso, dinadagdagan nyo naman yung budget natin.

**[05:16:59.860] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005426)  
But, you know, ang dilemma po kasi ng NIA, parang pag gumawa ka ng isang dam,

**[05:17:04.860] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005427)  
hindi naman po pwede na bawasan mo yung dam kasi hindi mo matatapos siya.

**[05:17:08.420] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005428)  
Yung isang maliit na gap sa dam na yan, pwedeng ikasira ng buong system.

**[05:17:12.380] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005429)  
Hindi po katulad ng kalsada, na kung magkano lang yung budget, hanggang doon ka lang.

**[05:17:16.620] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005430)  
Sa mga projects po ng NIA, hindi po pwede yung ganoon, Mr. Chair.

**[05:17:20.080] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005431)  
Kaya dapat hindi mo talaga siya gagalawin kung ano yung in-identify ng NIA.

**[05:17:24.540] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005432)  
Naalam namin.

**[05:17:25.100] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005433)  
Hindi na magtatapos doon sa budget, sa project po.

**[05:17:28.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005434)  
Ilan po yung mga projects na priority project, katulad ng mga binabanggit nyo,

**[05:17:31.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005435)  
na nabawasan na ang nangyari hindi na natin nagamit last year?

**[05:17:37.380] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005436)  
Last year, halos lahat po, Mr. Chair.

**[05:17:39.840] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005437)  
Dahil hindi nga po kami nakapreksi.

**[05:17:41.960] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005438)  
Siyempre kung magbabawas na ano yan, across the board yan.

**[05:17:44.760] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-005439)  
So, halos lahat po nagalaw yung aming budget.

**[05:17:48.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005440)  
So, Mr. Chair, hindi naman po pwedeng kalahating dam or kalahating irrigation system ang pupondohan natin.

**[05:17:54.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005441)  
Kaya, when we make adjustments here in the House, or later in the Senate,

**[05:17:59.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005442)  
I would just like to manifest that we have to consider the implementability of these projects,

**[05:18:04.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005443)  
not simply the amount that we want to cut.

**[05:18:09.820] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005444)  
My next question, I'd like to go to BIFAR.

**[05:18:13.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005445)  
Mr. Chair, in 2024, BIFAR left 3.63 billion unobligated.

**[05:18:21.660] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005446)  
Gentle reminder.

**[05:18:24.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005447)  
1.102 billion in unused cash was reverted to the Treasury,

**[05:18:30.000] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005448)  
and capital outlay utilization was only 21.54%.

**[05:18:34.240] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005449)  
According to COA, the underutilization was mainly because projects were not implementation ready.

**[05:18:42.940] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005450)  
Dahil dito, maraming benepisyo ang hindi nakarating sa fisher folk,

**[05:18:48.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005451)  
boats, fishing gears, and safety equipment.

**[05:18:53.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005452)  
Aquaculture infrastructure.

**[05:18:54.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005453)  
Inputs, hatcheries, post-harvest facilities, fishery equipment, at livelihood assistance.

**[05:18:59.340] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005454)  
At habang nasa BIFAR ang pera, hindi rin natin ito nailaan sa ibang programa na ready nang i-implement.

**[05:19:07.340] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005455)  
Yet, BIFAR is asking Congress for 12.412 billion for 2027, including 3.61.4 billion in capital outlay.

**[05:19:19.060] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005456)  
Bakit naman po natin sila bibigyan uli?

**[05:19:21.180] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005457)  
What has changed that should give Congress confidence?

**[05:19:24.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005458)  
That you can actually implement this budget?

**[05:19:27.340] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005459)  
And what are your obligation in disbursement rates for 2026 as of today?

**[05:19:34.150] BIFAR Assistant Director for Administrative Services Zaldi Perez** (asr-005460)  
Good afternoon, Mr. Chair. Good afternoon, Your Honors.

**[05:19:38.230] BIFAR Assistant Director for Administrative Services Zaldi Perez** (asr-005461)  
I am Zaldi Perez, Assistant Director for Administrative Services of the Bureau of Fisheries and Aquatic Resources.

**[05:19:44.610] BIFAR Assistant Director for Administrative Services Zaldi Perez** (asr-005462)  
Please be informed, Your Honors, that the 2024 project was delayed because of, of course,

**[05:19:53.430] BIFAR Assistant Director for Administrative Services Zaldi Perez** (asr-005463)  
yung bidding process po natin.

**[05:19:55.950] BIFAR Assistant Director for Administrative Services Zaldi Perez** (asr-005464)  
It took us some time to prepare the terms of reference as well as the specifications of these boats,

**[05:20:04.130] BIFAR Assistant Director for Administrative Services Zaldi Perez** (asr-005465)  
which comprise po yung 50-meter vessels, 80-meter vessels, and then the 62-footer boats, Your Honors.

**[05:20:11.990] BIFAR Assistant Director for Administrative Services Zaldi Perez** (asr-005466)  
So that's why po nadelay ng konti.

**[05:20:14.470] BIFAR Assistant Director for Administrative Services Zaldi Perez** (asr-005467)  
It's because I think po nabid po namin siya later part na po ng 2024.

**[05:20:21.170] BIFAR Assistant Director for Administrative Services Zaldi Perez** (asr-005468)  
But it was successfully bidded out.

**[05:20:25.950] BIFAR Assistant Director for Administrative Services Zaldi Perez** (asr-005469)  
And then we obligated po preceding year po, which is 2025.

**[05:20:32.510] BIFAR Assistant Director for Administrative Services Zaldi Perez** (asr-005470)  
So all this project po is already ongoing right now.

**[05:20:37.170] BIFAR Assistant Director for Administrative Services Zaldi Perez** (asr-005471)  
And there is already a partial delivery of this patrol, of this 62-footer.

**[05:20:42.830] BIFAR Assistant Director for Administrative Services Zaldi Perez** (asr-005472)  
While that 50-meter and 80-meter is still under construction, Your Honors.

**[05:20:49.970] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005473)  
Can you just submit a list, Mr. Chair?

**[05:20:52.050] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005474)  
Magpapasubmit lang po ako ng list of projects na kukompletuhin nila for 2024.

**[05:20:55.950] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-005475)  
Okay, pakisubmit na lang yung list na hinihingi ni Congresman.

**[05:20:59.790] BIFAR Assistant Director for Administrative Services Zaldi Perez** (asr-005476)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[05:21:00.610] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005477)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[05:21:01.810] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-005478)  
Thank you.

**[05:21:02.270] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-005479)  
Thank you very much, Congresman Robert, the next interpollator from majority.

**[05:21:08.350] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-005480)  
The famous and pinakasikat sa Kongreso,

**[05:21:15.450] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-005481)  
Plum Cagayan Diuro, Representative Ropos Rodriguez.

**[05:21:20.570] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005482)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman, for those kind words.

**[05:21:22.870] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005483)  
And let me now, therefore,

**[05:21:25.950] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005484)  
thank Secretary Kiko Laurel and the undersecretaries.

**[05:21:31.090] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005485)

**[05:21:38.650] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005486)

**[05:21:43.870] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005487)

**[05:21:45.250] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005488)

**[05:21:52.630] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005490)

**[05:21:55.950] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005491)

**[05:21:58.510] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005492)

**[05:22:04.410] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005493)

**[05:22:05.410] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005494)

**[05:22:08.700] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005495)

**[05:22:11.520] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005496)  
Apollo, Apollo, Apollo.

**[05:22:13.300] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005497)  
Apollo Pacamalad, doble sila eh, mga friends ko.

**[05:22:17.120] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005498)  
Also, Mr. Chairman, I ask also for farm-to-market roads.

**[05:22:21.400] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005499)  
And again, you gave double to what I asked, the additional.

**[05:22:24.480] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005500)  
Thank you very much.

**[05:22:27.460] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005501)  
So, it's true.

**[05:22:30.840] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005502)  
You gave me more.

**[05:22:32.680] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005503)  
Now, my question is, Mr. Secretary, what is now the share of Mindanao, six regions, six regions?

**[05:22:40.640] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005504)  
Because last hearing, also August 28, 2025, our share was only 12%, and you promised that it will reach 20%.

**[05:22:49.460] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005505)  
Because Mindanao gives 40% of our food requirements.

**[05:22:55.100] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005506)  
With our corn, 70%.

**[05:22:57.460] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005507)  
With our pineapple, 80%.

**[05:22:59.680] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005508)

**[05:23:01.620] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005509)  
And other crops.

**[05:23:05.640] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005510)  
The time is continued, Mr. Chairman.

**[05:23:08.340] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005511)

**[05:23:13.970] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005512)  
Mr. Chair, mas malaki siya since last year, but still 11% pa lang.

**[05:23:21.510] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005513)

**[05:23:22.790] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005514)

**[05:23:24.050] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005515)  
12 na kami last year.

**[05:23:25.450] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005516)  
No, no, sorry, sorry.

**[05:23:26.650] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005517)  
Mali sinabi sa akin, kinokorek na.

**[05:23:28.930] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005518)  
20%.

**[05:23:29.470] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005519)  
Kasi we expect Mindanao is the food basket of our country, and I am in Mindanao.

**[05:23:34.430] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005520)  
20%, Mr. Chair.

**[05:23:34.990] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005521)  
So, how much is that?

**[05:23:36.110] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005522)  
20%, Mr. Chair.

**[05:23:37.710] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005523)  
20 daw.

**[05:23:38.830] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005524)  
20% na?

**[05:23:40.090] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005525)

**[05:23:41.270] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005526)  
We thank the Secretary and the entire DA family.

**[05:23:44.770] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005527)  
Now, I'd like to ask Yusek, Yusek Aray, Yusek Navarro, and Yusek Ching Bernos to say something.

**[05:23:57.080] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-005528)  
Yeah, your request to Congress.

**[05:24:11.460] DA Undersecretary for Agro-Marine Industrial Systems Arrey A. Perez** (asr-005529)  
Thank you.

**[05:24:12.140] DA Undersecretary for Agro-Marine Industrial Systems Arrey A. Perez** (asr-005530)  
...

**[05:24:12.180] DA Undersecretary for Agro-Marine Industrial Systems Arrey A. Perez** (asr-005531)  
Thank you, Kong.

**[05:24:12.800] DA Undersecretary for Agro-Marine Industrial Systems Arrey A. Perez** (asr-005532)  
Thank you, Chairman.

**[05:24:15.520] DA Undersecretary for Agro-Marine Industrial Systems Arrey A. Perez** (asr-005533)  
Undersecretary for Infrastructure of the Department of Agriculture, of course, we definitely need augmentation for FMRs.

**[05:24:24.720] DA Undersecretary for Agro-Marine Industrial Systems Arrey A. Perez** (asr-005534)  
As clearly mentioned by Representative Nasalo, that we badly needed more roads, particularly for our farms.

**[05:24:39.720] DA Undersecretary for Agro-Marine Industrial Systems Arrey A. Perez** (asr-005535)  
So, on target.

**[05:24:42.180] DA Undersecretary for Agro-Marine Industrial Systems Arrey A. Perez** (asr-005536)  
So, what we want is not just 1,000, which is 16 billion for this year, but hopefully, for next year, hopefully we can triple that.

**[05:24:51.640] DA Undersecretary for Agro-Marine Industrial Systems Arrey A. Perez** (asr-005537)  
Kasi nga, the balance is 55,000.

**[05:24:55.440] DA Undersecretary for Agro-Marine Industrial Systems Arrey A. Perez** (asr-005538)  
Malayo pa po ang ano, napakalayo para mabawasan natin yung gap.

**[05:25:01.940] DA Undersecretary for Agro-Marine Industrial Systems Arrey A. Perez** (asr-005539)  
So, so far po, at least yun, kung may ibigay sa amin, additional 2,000 kilometers, equivalent to 30 billion, that would be much appreciated.

**[05:28:04.050] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005588)

**[05:28:11.530] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005589)  
um, I just wanted to state, Mr. Chair, that the high-value crops,

**[05:28:15.250] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005590)  
development program is one of the priority programs of the DA.

**[05:28:19.170] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005591)  
It's intended to help address food security and in ensuring available, accessible, affordable, nutrient-dense food at all times.

**[05:28:27.310] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005592)  
The high-value crops development program contributes to the following objectives.

**[05:28:31.710] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005593)  
Improve the overall performance of priority high-value crops industries by at least 2.5% yearly.

**[05:28:38.710] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005594)  
Increase farmers' income by at least 50% through diversification and value addition.

**[05:28:44.630] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005595)  
Increase low-income crops by at least 2.5% yearly.

**[05:28:45.090] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005596)  
Increase low-income crops by at least 2.5% yearly.

**[05:28:45.230] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005597)  
Increase local and global competitiveness of high-value crops by looking at premium products.

**[05:28:50.510] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005598)  
And orchestrate localized operating mechanism to connect the transition from production to nutrition

**[05:28:56.950] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005599)  
under the development of local economic enterprises and develop economically-sized production areas.

**[05:29:04.210] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005600)  
With this, Mr. Chair, we humbly request, ah, this honorable body, ah, to, ah, allow us to have at least an 8 billion increase of the budget,

**[05:29:14.610] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005601)  
which is, currently, we only have 1.7 billion.

**[05:29:18.490] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005602)  
Um, this is based po doon sa mga realizations that post-harvest losses is extremely high.

**[05:29:25.410] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005603)  
And, ah, the post-harvest losses does not only start at the production level.

**[05:29:30.630] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005604)  
It goes down to the distribution.

**[05:29:33.250] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005605)  
So, what we humbly request, ah, given these challenges on, um, El Nino, ah, and, ah, the value addition that has to be added to diversify farmers' income,

**[05:29:43.830] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005606)  
is, is to have that increase po, Mr. Chair.

**[05:29:47.250] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005607)  
Since the inception of high-value crops in 1995, we were pegged with only 1 billion, ah, budget.

**[05:29:54.950] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005608)  
So, I think with, with the, ah, multiple, ah, crops that we can look into,

**[05:30:00.000] DA Undersecretary Cheryl Marie Natividad-Caballero** (asr-005609)  
For value addition, this will deserve your kind attention if it can be assisted to help the transformation of the agricultural sector, Mr. Chair.
