**[05:46:05.880] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005935)  
Center regarding our milk production, local milk production, may I ask them to join us

**[05:46:12.880] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005936)  
here?

**[05:46:19.900] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005937)  
The NDA plays a crucial and much appreciated role in the implementation of the Masustansyang

**[05:46:27.900] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005938)  
Pagkain para sa Batang Pilipino Act.

**[05:46:30.900] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005939)  
As one of the lead agencies of the milk feeding program.

**[05:46:34.660] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005940)  
However, I would like to know, get the information from your agency, how much is really being

**[05:46:41.660] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005941)  
produced by our local dairy farmers?

**[05:46:44.660] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-005942)  
Mr. Chair, as of 2025, as of 2024, excuse me, the milk production was just 29 million liters.

**[05:46:57.660] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-005943)  
But as the PSA data shows, as of 2025, we increased it to 43 million, 43 million liters.

**[05:47:05.780] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-005944)  
But still, we agree on the observation that it's still not enough for the entire milk

**[05:47:10.700] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-005945)  
feeding program.

**[05:47:11.700] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-005946)  
That's why we resort to sterilized milk, milk production.

**[05:47:16.420] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005947)  
Okay.

**[05:47:18.580] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005948)  
I see from your report that you have exceeded your targets for 2026.

**[05:47:24.660] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005949)  
In fact, you have produced 21% of your milk.

**[05:47:25.660] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005950)  
Okay.

**[05:47:26.660] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005951)  
Thank you.

**[05:47:27.660] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005952)  
And you have produced 21% more than what you have produced for the same period between

**[05:47:32.060] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005953)  
January to June of 2025 at 25.38 million liters.

**[05:47:38.240] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005954)  
And yet, this is just, what, 90%?

**[05:47:43.340] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005955)  
90% is still imported?

**[05:47:45.940] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005956)  
How much is still the percentage of imported dairy?

**[05:47:49.900] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-005957)  
Madam, Mr. Chair, as of today, as of 2025, we have also increased the milk sufficiency

**[05:47:56.280] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-005958)  
of the country.

**[05:47:57.660] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-005959)  
From 1% for the longest year to 2.2%.

**[05:48:01.020] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-005960)  
You're right.

**[05:48:02.020] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-005961)  
Still, the imported dairy is, in the average, 98%.

**[05:48:07.780] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005962)  
During the hearing with the Department of Finance, as we were discussing the proposed

**[05:48:17.100] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005963)  
progress bill of the President, it was admitted by the Department of Finance that we, that

**[05:48:26.180] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005964)  
they are recommending...

**[05:48:27.140] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005965)  
We are recommending that fermented and flavored milks remain as exempted to the sweetened beverage

**[05:48:35.280] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005966)  
tax.

**[05:48:36.280] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005967)  
And it is because that is what is being used in the feeding program of our school-based

**[05:48:42.380] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005968)  
children.

**[05:48:43.380] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005969)  
And our target for school-based children is, how much is our target?

**[05:48:50.500] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-005970)  
4.5 million?

**[05:48:51.500] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-005971)  
Yes.

**[05:48:52.500] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-005972)  
Mr. Chair, for 2026, it's 4.5 million.

**[05:48:55.500] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005973)  
4.5 million.

**[05:48:56.500] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005974)  
And you have a 3 billion budget for this?

**[05:48:57.500] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005975)  
I mean, the DSWD and the DepEd has allocated for their fresh milk feeding program around

**[05:49:06.340] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005976)  
3 billion.

**[05:49:07.340] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-005977)  
Yes, for NDA, it's about 3 billion.

**[05:49:10.460] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-005978)  
It is just for DepEd's milk feeding program.

**[05:49:13.820] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-005979)  
Not included the DSWD milk feeding program.

**[05:49:17.240] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005980)  
Oh, okay.

**[05:49:18.240] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005981)  
So, it's still higher.

**[05:49:19.240] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005982)  
And it was you who said that that's the best incentive for our farmers.

**[05:49:20.640] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005983)  
That's right.

**[05:49:21.640] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005984)  
To go into the field?

**[05:49:22.640] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005985)  
Yes.

**[05:49:23.640] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005986)  
That's the best incentive for our farmers.

**[05:49:24.640] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005987)  
That's right.

**[05:49:25.640] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005988)  
That's right.

**[05:49:26.380] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005989)  
Yes.

**[05:49:26.500] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005990)  
dairy. Because if there's a demand, because we have a law, there's a demand for fresh milk.

**[05:49:33.760] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005991)  
And let me just emphasize that what we are feeding our children now, these fermented milk

**[05:49:41.260] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005992)  
and flavored milk beverages, a single serving provides around 9 to 21 grams of sugar. That's

**[05:49:50.220] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005993)  
around 2.2 to 5 teaspoons of sugar per serving. So instead of helping our kids be more healthy,

**[05:50:00.220] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005994)  
be healthier, we are giving them this craving for sugar at a very young age. So kailangan po talaga

**[05:50:08.160] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005995)  
i-fast track natin ang ating programa sa dairy production, local dairy production. So can you

**[05:50:15.880] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005996)  
tell us, kanina si Congressman Rufus asked you,

**[05:50:20.220] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005997)  
for cattle, but I want to see the total picture, ano ang ginagawa ngayon ng National Dairy

**[05:50:26.200] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-005998)  
and the Carabao Center for us to at least move forward so that we can produce our own milk

**[05:50:35.160] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-005999)  
for our children? Tama po, Mr. Chair. One of the reasons po bakit hindi dumadami ang ating

**[05:50:40.840] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006000)  
milk production is napakakonti po ng ating mga alagang gatasang hayop. Dahil po sa mahabang

**[05:50:45.880] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006001)  
panahon, ang ating mga iniimport ng mga alagang gatasang hayop,

**[05:50:50.220] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006002)  
duretsong binibigay po sa farmers. At more often than not, ang mangyayari po dyan ay mamamatay.

**[05:50:55.640] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006003)  
But for this year, nakapagtayo po tayo ng mga stock farms. Stock farms na kung saan natin po ilalagay

**[05:51:02.360] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006004)  
ang mga imported animals para sila ma-acclimatize muna. Doon po muna sila magpaparami at pagka

**[05:51:07.460] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006005)  
dumami na po sila, meron na po tayong source ng mga island-born dairy cattle na mas madali

**[05:51:12.420] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006006)  
pong alagaan ng mga dairy farmers. Yan pong ginagawa po natin. Kaya yung mga dadating po natin

**[05:51:16.760] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006007)  
mga imported dairy animals this October, hindi po natin magpaparami ng mga imported dairy animals.

**[05:51:20.200] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006008)  
Yan diretsong ipamimigay muna. Dahil malamang sa malamang, mamamatay po yan. Ilalagay po na

**[05:51:26.900] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006009)  
natin sa stock farms yan para maklimatay sila, dumami sila, at doon natin kukunin yung ating

**[05:51:32.560] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006010)  
ipamamahagi kasi island-born na po sila.

**[05:51:36.380] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006011)  
Aside from yung ating cattle, kailangan rin nila ng particular grass, di ba? Kasama rin po ba yan sa programa ninyo?

**[05:51:44.280] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006012)  
Tama po yan, Mr. Chair. Dahil po, kasama po sa binigay na budget po sa ating

**[05:51:50.160] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006013)  
NDA ay yung pagpapagawa ng mga feed centers. Ito po mga feed centers ang tutugon sa pangangailangan po ng ating mga

**[05:51:56.660] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006014)  
dairy farmers pagdating po sa pakain. Dahil alam naman po natin, ang prinsipyo sa dairy, garbage in, garbage out.

**[05:52:03.000] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006015)  
Pakainin mo ng basura yan, basura ang gatas niyan.

**[05:52:06.140] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006016)  
Sinabi ninyong feeds, ano yun, magtatanim sila ng grass?

**[05:52:10.080] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006017)  
Yes, not just grass po, including corn. Depende po sa lugar. May mga lugar po talaga na maganda magtanim ng mga corn.

**[05:52:17.880] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006018)  
May mga lugar naman po na maganda magtanim ng mga corn.

**[05:52:20.160] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006019)  
Magtanim ng grass, katulad po ng napier. At yung mga harvest po yan, dadalim po yan sa mga feed centers natin,

**[05:52:26.440] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006020)  
kung saan yan ipoprocess para maging silage po, at upang ipamahagi po sa ating mga dairy farmers.

**[05:52:31.660] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006021)  
Sa presenting budget po ninyo, ano po ang na-anticipate ninyong increase sa local milk production?

**[05:52:37.620] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006022)  
And with the additional incentive for our farmers, kasi may ready market, may strong demand,

**[05:52:44.060] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006023)  
ang mga school feeding program, ano po ang nakikita ninyong targets for the next?

**[05:52:50.160] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006024)  
Mr. Chair, actually, pagdating po sa milk feeding program, wala na po kami talaga ang maireklamo dahil napakagandang budget ang naibigay po sa National Dairy Authority para sa milk feeding program.

**[05:53:02.060] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006025)  
Noong mga nakaraang panahon po kasi, ang problema ng ating mga magagatas, tumutulo po ang gatas, walang bumibili, literally tinatapon.

**[05:53:09.380] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006026)  
But because of this milk feeding program, naingganyo po sila na alagaan ng hayop nila magpagatas.

**[05:53:14.980] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006027)  
And that's one of the reasons why nag-increase ang milk production, dahil pinagahanda nila ang milk feeding program.

**[05:53:20.160] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006028)  
Now, pagdating naman po sa budget ng NDA, may iiyak po kayo sa neb na naibigay sa amin.

**[05:53:26.320] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006029)  
Nasa P216 lang po. Taman-tama lang po yung pang-sweldo. Actually, kulang pa po.

**[05:53:31.000] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006030)  
Kaya po, napakasakitignan na ang mga kawani po ng NDA, sumusweldo pero walang gagawin.

**[05:53:37.940] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006031)  
Yung P3 billion na budget para sa milk feeding program ay direct ang mapupunta sa mga farmers at sa mga cooperatives, hindi po ba?

**[05:53:45.760] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006032)  
Yes, po.

**[05:53:46.020] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006033)  
At hindi naman talaga sa NDA.

**[05:53:48.140] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006034)  
Yan po yung pera po ng debit for milk feeding program.

**[05:53:50.160] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006035)  
Yung pera po ng debit for milk feeding program ay binigay po sa NDA at sa PCC upang ipamili namin ng gatas sa ating mga dairy farmers gamit ang sagipsaka.

**[05:53:57.760] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006036)  
Kaya mas madali po natin may bibigay ang ating servisyo o ating mabibiling gatas sa mga farmers dahil diretsyo po natin ipoprofure sa kanila yan.

**[05:54:05.980] NDA Administrator Marcus Antonius T. Andaya** (asr-006037)  
Hindi na po sila magpapatayan sa presyo.

**[05:54:09.440] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006038)  
Okay, so tingnan mo yung sitwasyon ng NDA.

**[05:54:13.000] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006039)  
Huwag mo masyadong, talagang kulang na kulang talaga sila sa pod.

**[05:54:17.000] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006040)  
Yan, yan ang maganda, Chris Van Rufus.

**[05:54:20.160] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006041)  
Is my time over?

**[05:54:22.260] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-006042)  
Not yet.

**[05:54:22.720] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006043)  
Not yet, okay.

**[05:54:24.180] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006044)  
Salamat po, attorney.

**[05:54:25.820] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006045)  
Thank you so much.

**[05:54:26.640] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006046)  
Maraming salamat po, sir.

**[05:54:27.260] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006047)  
Baka kausapin ko na lang po kayo in private yung National, yung Carabao Center.

**[05:54:33.620] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006048)  
May mahalaga lang po akong gustong itanong sa Sugar Regulatory Administration.

**[05:54:41.480] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-006049)  
Hello.

**[05:54:43.620] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006050)  
Good afternoon, sir.

**[05:54:47.300] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006051)  
Ngayon, pinag-uusapan natin ang impact ng training.

**[05:54:50.160] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006052)  
Sa sweetened beverages.

**[05:54:55.020] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006053)  
Nag-impose tayo ng 2018 ng 6 pesos sa refined sugar na ba ito?

**[05:55:02.540] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006054)  
Sugar.

**[05:55:03.000] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006055)  
Raw sugar.

**[05:55:03.720] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006056)  
Raw sugar.

**[05:55:05.200] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006057)  
And sa fructose naman ay mas mataas.

**[05:55:09.480] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006058)  
And palagay ko mas gamay po ninyo yan, sir.

**[05:55:13.480] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006059)  
Gusto namin makuha sana ang trend.

**[05:55:15.840] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006060)  
Since 2017 up to the present.

**[05:55:19.500] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006061)  
Yung volume.

**[05:55:20.160] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006062)  
Volume of sugar consumed and bought by type of industry.

**[05:55:25.260] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006063)  
Hindi lang limited sa mga sugar beverages na industry.

**[05:55:30.940] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006064)  
And isabay na po rin ninyo yung demand for artificial sweetener.

**[05:55:36.160] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006065)  
Kasi I think next week, napag-uusapan po sa Ways and Means Committee,

**[05:55:40.940] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006066)  
yung proposal ng Department of Finance na dagdagan ang buwis para sa sweetened beverages.

**[05:55:48.400] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006067)  
Yes.

**[05:55:50.160] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006068)  
And kailangan po natin makita rin ang impact nitong buwis na ito kung meron man sa ating local sugar farmers.

**[05:55:58.020] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006069)  
Kasi naandito si Congresswoman Subiri, I'm quite sure, interesado siya malaman kung anong impact nito sa local farmers natin.

**[05:56:05.980] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006070)  
So, kailangan po namin yung data na kung maaari maibigay po sa amin para magkaroon tayo ng trajectory ng sugar trading.

**[05:56:14.120] Rep. Cielo Krisel Lagman** (asr-006071)  
And then anong magiging impact ng dagdag buwis kung meron man sa ating local farmers.

**[05:56:20.160] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-006072)  
Yes, po, Mr. Chair, we will provide your office with the data.

**[05:56:24.160] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-006073)  
Okay, thank you, Honorable Lagman.

**[05:56:28.160] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-006074)  
Next, the Chair recognizes Apu Romeo Momos Senor.

**[05:56:35.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006075)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman, Mr. Secretary.

**[05:56:39.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006076)  
I will go direct to my parroquial concern, Mr. Secretary.

**[05:56:42.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006077)  
I have three questions, one for Philpida, one for BFAR, and one for NIA.

**[05:56:46.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006078)  
Mr. Chairman, Mr. Secretary,

**[05:56:49.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006079)  
Surigao del Sur, particularly the Municipality of Marihatag, is considered one of the major producers of high-quality abaca fiber.

**[05:56:57.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006080)  
In fact, ranked number four among the abaca-producing provinces, producing 4,140 metric tons of abaca fiber in 2025,

**[05:57:07.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006081)  
about 7.4% of our national production, and the only abaca-producing province which posted a positive 10% growth rate,

**[05:57:18.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006082)  
while the rest of the provinces producing abaca are all in negative growth rate.

**[05:57:25.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006083)  
Right now, a road network connecting the production site to the main national highway and market centers are about to be started,

**[05:57:36.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006084)  
funded by the PRDP program, the program of the World Bank.

**[05:57:42.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006085)  
And Municipality of Marihatag, kahit na maliit ho siya, six plus munis,

**[05:57:46.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006086)  
six plus municipality was able to post or to share 30% as a counterpart, which is 30 million po.

**[05:57:55.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006087)  
The area being developed is an IP community po, Manubo tribe ho ang nandyan,

**[05:58:00.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006088)  
and just recently been declared insurgent-free.

**[05:58:05.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006089)  
Dahil yung lugar na yun ho dati ay hindi mapuntahan ng tao dahil talagang insurgent area, rebel infested yun.

**[05:58:16.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006090)  
And therefore, most of the farmers are rebel returnees.

**[05:58:20.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006091)  
In order to help the Manubo tribe and the rebel returnees,

**[05:58:24.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006092)  
thus the establishment of Abaca Agro-Industrial Corridor in the Municipality of Marihatag, Surigao del Sur,

**[05:58:32.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006093)  
which was endorsed by RDC as one of the top 30 implementation-ready priority projects in Caraga.

**[05:58:41.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006094)  
Kompleto na ho ng papili sito. May feasibility study ang lahat-lahat.

**[05:58:44.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006095)  
Kompleto na ho ng papili sito. May feasibility study ang lahat-lahat.

**[05:58:46.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006096)  
The total project cost is 739 million.

**[05:58:51.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006097)  
But this year ang hiningi lang nila is 47 million lang po para to start with.

**[05:58:58.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006098)  
Actually ang 47 million is for the establishment of Abaca Consolidation and Processing Center, if I am right.

**[05:59:05.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006099)  
So para lang ho tayo maka-start, para ho mayroon ng bahay kung sa ibig sabihin pa.

**[05:59:11.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006100)  
Para tuloy-tuloy na ho ang ating programa.

**[05:59:14.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006101)  
Mr. Secretary, wala ng ibang provinces ang may ganun potential in Abaca except Surigao del Sur.

**[05:59:25.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006102)  
Kaya nga ho kailangan siguro nating matulungan.

**[05:59:27.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006103)  
My question is ang 47 million ba ay naisama natin sa ating budget this year, 2027?

**[05:59:35.160] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006104)  
Mr. Chair, ang sagot diyan is yes. Kaya lang nasa tier 2, Mr. Chair.

**[05:59:42.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006105)  
When you mean tier 2, Secretary, is it not part of your budget, part of the GAO or your NEP right now?

**[05:59:52.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006106)  
Because there are tier 1 and tier 2s. I would suppose that's part of...

**[06:00:01.390] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006107)  
Mr. Chair, ang problem daw, sabi namin USIC for Finance, hindi na-approve yung Tier 2 namin.

**[06:00:11.100] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006108)  
How do we go about it, Mr. Secretary? I think this is one of the best programs TILFIDA ever had.

**[06:00:16.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006109)  
Sige, sir. Meron kaming option. Kailangan lang talaga natin kalagyan ng bahay. May bahay na ba yan?

**[06:00:23.920] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006110)  
Kailangan lang namin lagyan ng bahay. Meron kaming World Bank na pwedeng i-access, Mr. Chair.

**[06:00:28.960] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006111)  
Actually, the 47 million is just for i-bahay so that the next budget, it will be Tier 1 na. Sana eh.

**[06:00:38.780] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006112)  
Sige, well, Mr. Chair, we will endeavor na mailagay natin. Tulungan natin, Mr. Chair, na malagay sa budget past Tier 1 kahit lagyan lang ng 5-10 million, then kami nang bahala magdagdag.

**[06:00:50.780] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006113)  
Yes, po. In our side, we'll do also our part. My next question is addressed to BIFAR.

**[06:01:00.300] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006114)  
Of the 13 municipalities and one city in my district, 1st District of Surigao del Sur, 13 of these municipalities are situated along the coast.

**[06:01:10.120] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006115)  
And therefore, 40% of our inhabitants, people in Surigao del Sur, are fishermen, Mr. Secretary.

**[06:01:17.960] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006116)  
Our produce, the production, whatever derived or caught from fishing, are supplied.

**[06:01:28.960] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006117)  
Supplied to our neighboring municipalities, provinces, and cities. At dahil dito po, kami ang nagsusupply.

**[06:01:38.120] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006118)  
Right now, ito lang ho ang problema. Right now, although we are producing enough fish for reconsumption of our neighboring provinces and cities, wala ho kaming cold storage po.

**[06:01:51.840] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006119)  
Kaya yung aming mga fishermen ay mapilitan i-binta ng mura kung panahon na maraming kuha.

**[06:01:58.960] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006120)  
So sana po, I have been, in fact, a resolution has already submitted, a resolution from the Municipality of Liangga, where the proposed cold storage will be established.

**[06:02:13.080] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006121)  
E nandito na ho, nasabit na last year pa po. Sana mabigyan ng priority po ito.

**[06:02:18.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006122)  
Yes, Mr. Chair. Saan exactong lugar, Mr. Chair?

**[06:02:21.300] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006123)  
Actually, Mr. Secretary, Barangay St. Christine, Liangga, Surigao del Sur.

**[06:02:28.240] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006124)  
Yes.

**[06:02:28.960] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006125)  
Yes. Liangga po.

**[06:02:34.510] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006126)  
Yes, sir. Ang lagayin ho na ito, Mr. Chair, is ice plant and cold storage.

**[06:02:39.990] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006127)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Secretary.

**[06:02:41.410] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006128)  
And the last, Mr. Chairman. In my district, this is addressed to NIA. In my district, I have two NIS, National Irrigation System.

**[06:02:54.890] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006129)  
And that suggests that we have thousands of irrigated farmlands.

**[06:03:02.070] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006130)  
Producing so much number of rice.

**[06:03:06.230] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006131)  
Kami ho ang nagsusupply ng mga, ng palay sa Gawaw, sa Cagayan de Uro, sa Marawi City.

**[06:03:13.790] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006132)  
Because of the two NIS.

**[06:03:15.970] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006133)  
My only problem now is, bakit ho dalawang NIS, 5 million lang ho ang allocation?

**[06:03:23.390] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006134)  
Dahil sa 5 million lang ang allocation, I would, parang nakikita ko ho na ang mga repair, maintenance, as well as upkeep.

**[06:03:32.070] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006135)  
Of the NIS is never enough.

**[06:03:35.830] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006136)  
And therefore, not enough, no?

**[06:03:37.830] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006137)  
And therefore, it will, of course, reduce the production of rice per hectare.

**[06:03:44.250] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006138)  
Kung hindi natin masusupplyan ng sufficient number of water, volume of water required.

**[06:03:50.210] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006139)  
Kaya nga ho, kailangan ho sana natin madagdagan ang ating requirements.

**[06:03:57.070] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006140)  
Dalawang NIS po, ha?

**[06:03:58.470] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006141)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[06:04:03.320] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006142)  
Siguro nakita ni Honorable Congressman ito.

**[06:04:08.200] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006143)  
Ang proposal namin sa isa, 2 billion eh.

**[06:04:10.620] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006144)  
Talagang 2 billion pa ang proposal namin.

**[06:04:13.900] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006145)  
Ang pet project po namin doon na isa is yung Tago Riz.

**[06:04:18.600] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006146)  
Nag-propose po kami doon nga ng, at saka yung repair ng Santa Filomena CIS, 250 million.

**[06:04:26.780] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006147)  
Pero tama po kayo, dalawang 5 million lang ang lumabas, sir.

**[06:04:29.720] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006148)  
But again, isa po.

**[06:04:33.320] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006149)  
Ito kasi sa priority natin.

**[06:04:34.860] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006150)  
In fact, isa ito sa pinakamalaking hingin nga natin ang punto.

**[06:04:38.060] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006151)  
Kung meron po tayong additional, sir, babalikan po namin ito.

**[06:04:40.480] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006152)  
Mr. Administrator, I have been here in Congress and I've been a vice chairman of the Committee on Appropriation for 8 years now.

**[06:04:47.500] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006153)  
And never in any instance I heard an agency na sobrang kanilang allocation.

**[06:04:52.600] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006154)  
Laging kulang po.

**[06:04:54.080] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006155)  
Ang question ko lang ho, how do you allocate that meager budget you have?

**[06:05:00.240] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006156)  
Kasi po, kailangan equitable ang distribution.

**[06:05:02.720] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006157)  
Not equal.

**[06:05:03.120] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006158)  
But equitable.

**[06:05:04.580] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006159)  
Mindanao po is i-breadbasket natin, di ba?

**[06:05:08.520] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006160)  
Dapat ho, may problema si Ropos, kulang.

**[06:05:12.980] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006161)  
Si Amatod, Congressman Amatong, kulang din.

**[06:05:15.440] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006162)  
Lahat-lahat kami yata, kulang buong Mindanao po.

**[06:05:18.700] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006163)  
Ang irony is, we are the ones supplying ng pagkain dito sa Metro Manila.

**[06:05:23.580] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006164)  
And why not put some bigger share even in the allocation of budget for irrigation?

**[06:05:31.600] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006165)  
Yes, sir.

**[06:05:32.460] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006166)  
Actually po.

**[06:05:33.120] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006167)  
Ang NIA kasi.

**[06:05:33.980] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006168)  
Again, ulitin ko po, Mr. Chair, ang na-priority po talaga na bigyan namin ng pondo are the ongoing projects.

**[06:05:39.820] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006169)  
But kung mapapansin, bibigyan po ko po kaya ng listahan, Mr. Chair.

**[06:05:45.580] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006170)  
Kapag kami mga savings po ang NIA, lagi namin nilalagay po lahat sa Mindanao.

**[06:05:49.980] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006171)  
I will give you po a list, yung modification po namin this year, Mr. Chair.

**[06:05:54.680] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006172)  
Makikita nyo po na pupunta halos lahat sa Mindanao yung aming budget.

**[06:05:58.700] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006173)  
And lastly, Mr. Chairman.

**[06:06:00.740] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006174)  
Lastly, sinabi ni Presidente nung panahon,

**[06:06:03.120] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006175)  
sa Suna, na ang tataposin nila, in fact, that is also the directive to the Department of Public Works and Highways,

**[06:06:09.140] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006176)  
tataposin nila yung mga ongoing projects.

**[06:06:13.040] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006177)  
In my district po, 60 milyon na lang ang kulang ng Santa Pilumina Dam.

**[06:06:20.540] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006178)  
Sana papangsyon na siya.

**[06:06:22.660] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006179)  
Sana po ang lagyan natin yan.

**[06:06:24.400] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006180)  
Kasi that's the priority of the President.

**[06:06:26.760] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006181)  
Yung tapusin yung mga proyekto ng nabimbin po.

**[06:06:30.640] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006182)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[06:06:31.660] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006183)  
Iipa-priority natin po yan.

**[06:06:33.120] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006184)  
Thank you, thank you, Administrator.

**[06:06:35.640] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006185)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[06:06:36.980] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006186)  
And thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[06:06:39.380] Rep. Joseph S. Tan** (asr-006187)  
Thank you, Apo, Rep. Momo.

**[06:06:44.420] Rep. Joseph S. Tan** (asr-006188)  
The Chair recognizes Rep. Reynald Michael Tan.

**[06:06:56.540] Rep. Joseph S. Tan** (asr-006189)  
Si Apo.

**[06:07:01.000] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006190)  
Sorry.

**[06:07:01.440] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006191)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[06:07:04.320] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006192)  
And good afternoon, guys, Secretary.

**[06:07:08.500] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006193)  
And the rest of the administration.

**[06:07:09.680] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006194)  
And the FDA family.

**[06:07:10.780] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006195)  
Anyway, Mr. Secretary, Mr. Chair, I have some few questions lang.

**[06:07:17.340] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006196)  
Pertaining to the FMR of the 2025, which we considered as FLR,

**[06:07:24.560] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006197)  
what is the current status of the farm-to-market roads of the 2025 budget?

**[06:07:32.520] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006198)  
Yung sa 2025, Mr. Chair, yung sa 2025 na farm-to-market na 23 billion,

**[06:07:39.680] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006199)  
na FLR doon is 10.7.

**[06:07:43.280] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006200)  
From what I understand, yung 5.7, i-re-release soon.

**[06:07:49.940] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006201)  
So, what is the reason na i-re-release yung 5 billion plus at saka yung the address na ay hindi ma-re-release?

**[06:08:00.270] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006202)  
I think it's more of the budget yan.

**[06:08:04.810] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006203)  
We always request, the DA always requests, Mr. Chair, na i-release lahat yan.

**[06:08:10.790] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006204)  
Then,

**[06:08:11.470] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006205)  
it depends sa cash flow, I guess.

**[06:08:13.690] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006206)  
Kaya, yung 10.7, 5.7,

**[06:08:16.570] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006207)  
mas buwang in-advise lang kami ng DBM na i-re-release nila for now.

**[06:08:21.130] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006208)  
So, Chair.

**[06:08:22.470] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006209)  
Thank you, Sir Chair.

**[06:08:24.810] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006210)  
And in connection with the farm-to-market,

**[06:08:28.830] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006211)  
for the 2026 budget,

**[06:08:32.250] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006212)  
mabuti din na andito din si Yusek Arig.

**[06:08:35.410] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006213)  
He was in Saumar, I think last month.

**[06:08:38.510] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006214)  
So, at least nakita talaga yung situation namin.

**[06:08:41.470] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006215)  
So, Mr. Chair, I just wanted to raise that

**[06:08:46.130] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006216)  
yung detalye po nung farm-to-market is, I think, 5 meters yung width at saka 2.2 mm or meters yung thickness.

**[06:09:03.130] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006217)  
200 mm yung thickness.

**[06:09:05.930] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006218)  
But, however, kasi yung situation namin sa Saumar, Secretary,

**[06:09:11.470] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006219)  
medyo wala pa kaming masyadong road

**[06:09:15.670] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006220)  
papunta mismo pa lang sa barangay.

**[06:09:18.670] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006221)  
Like, yung na-observe ko in some other provinces,

**[06:09:22.970] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006222)  
na talaga may road na sila access going to the barangay

**[06:09:25.810] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006223)  
and papunta na talaga sila sa farm.

**[06:09:28.550] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006224)  
So, sa amin, because alam mo naman yung sitwasyon natin,

**[06:09:31.750] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006225)  
951 barangays in the entire province.

**[06:09:34.430] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006226)  
So, medyo may mga more or less pang 10% pa kami ng mga barangays na walang road.

**[06:09:40.730] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006227)  
So, we...

**[06:09:41.470] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006228)  
Utilize the farm-to-market leading to the barangay pa lang.

**[06:09:46.430] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006229)  
So, however, nagkakaroon dyan ang mga overlapping.

**[06:09:49.950] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006230)  
Minsan yung DPWH yung nag-fund.

**[06:09:52.810] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006231)  
Minsan naman yung farm-to-market.

**[06:09:54.770] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006232)  
Pero, with the guidelines ngayon na 5 meters yung width at saka 200 mm.

**[06:10:01.510] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006233)  
So, parang it does not align with the DP.

**[06:10:04.810] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006234)  
Kasi sa DP is 6 meters yung width at saka mas thicker yung...

**[06:10:09.870] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006235)  
Like, 0.2...

**[06:10:11.470] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006236)  
23 meters yung thickness.

**[06:10:15.810] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006237)  
So, is it possible ba na pwede ba natin ma-adjust yan?

**[06:10:22.090] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006238)  
At least the same na lang yung sa DP at saka sa FMR.

**[06:10:29.090] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006239)  
Kasi parang medyo masagwa din tingnan na 6 tapos magpa-5.

**[06:10:34.090] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006240)  
Then, sa unahan na naman magsi-6 kung DP naman yung mag-continue.

**[06:10:38.550] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006241)  
So, magkakaroon kami ng konting problema.

**[06:10:40.610] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006242)  
So, parang...

**[06:10:40.810] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006243)  
So, parang...

**[06:10:40.890] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006244)  
So, parang...

**[06:10:40.950] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006245)  
So, parang...

**[06:10:41.470] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006246)  
So, sa physical na hitsura nung road.

**[06:10:45.010] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006247)  
And at the same time, pag na-open na yung road natin, medyo marami na rin yung mga vehicle na pumapasok.

**[06:10:50.790] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006248)  
Malalaki na vehicle.

**[06:10:51.830] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006249)  
So, chances is, the more thick yung road natin, the better yung maglalast din siya.

**[06:10:57.670] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006250)  
And lastly, yun sa 20% na elevation.

**[06:11:04.290] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006251)  
Medyo hilly kasi yung probinsya natin.

**[06:11:07.430] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006252)  
There are a lot of winds hilly.

**[06:11:09.690] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006253)  
So, kailangan natin ng...

**[06:11:11.310] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006254)  
Kanal, kailangan natin ng slow protection, and so on and so forth.

**[06:11:14.290] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006255)  
So, I don't think na kakayanin yung 20%.

**[06:11:19.010] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-006256)  
Baka lumagpas kami ng konti, Secretary.

**[06:11:27.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006257)  
Mr. Chair, definitely.

**[06:11:28.900] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006258)  
Well, binabago na nga namin yung classification instead of concreting.

**[06:11:33.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006259)  
Ginagawa namin construction para mas may flexibility, lalo na doon sa mga terrain na medyo difficult or needs slow protection.

**[06:11:43.680] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006260)  
Meanwhile, yung sa...

**[06:11:45.560] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006261)  
Sinasabi natin na mga bagong road opening at baka kulang nga yung thickness doon sa mga bagong kali dahil dating hindi pa dinadaanan.

**[06:11:57.440] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006262)  
Meron tayong option na using the same budget but using soil stabilizers muna for the first few years para nga makompact muna siya.

**[06:12:09.460] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006263)

**[06:30:00.000] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006734)

**[06:30:20.540] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006735)  
Yes, sir. Actually may out of our request for the Dinagat Island is for, ang na-propose sir sa aming request is 32,600,000 pa po. Yung for 2027.

**[06:30:38.760] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006736)  
Thank you for that information. Alam ko naman na mayroon talagang area na kahit anong gawin, wala talagang tubig.

**[06:30:46.220] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006737)  
Ang hindi ko maintindihan, yan yung reklamo ko. Bakit nilagyan ng budget?

**[06:30:50.540] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006738)  
Doon, alam naman nila na walang tubig. But anyway, nandiyan na yan. Hihintayin ko na lang. Magtatrabaho naman sa tingin ko yung mga naka-assign ngayon doon.

**[06:31:03.280] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006739)  
Now, doon sa national issue, Mr. Secretary, because I represent the power sector, nag-uusap na ba kayo ng Department of Energy?

**[06:31:17.540] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006740)  
Paano natin i-resolve yung issue na...

**[06:31:20.540] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006741)  
Marami na akong nakita na mga solar farm na pang-agriculture ang area?

**[06:31:28.000] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006742)  
Buti natanong nyo, Mr. Chairman. Sa Soma ng NIA po, mayroon na kami na construct na around 100 megawatts po na solar power pumps sa buong bansa.

**[06:31:41.100] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006743)  
At ang assessment namin na yan is, ito lang pong first quarter ng 2026. So nadadagdagan pa po.

**[06:31:50.540] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006744)  
Now, ang gusto sana namin, ang proposal namin po sa Department of Energy is that sana itong mga pumps namin may connect po sa, para makapag-supply kami sa electric cooperatives.

**[06:32:08.440] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006745)  
Kasi tamang-tama po ito doon sa zona ni Presidente na wala ng service. Kasi directa po ito eh. I mean, embedded po ito eh.

**[06:32:20.540] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006746)  
Wala ng transmission charge.

**[06:32:21.740] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006747)  
Wala ng transmission charge. Kaya, suwak na sakpo sana ito.

**[06:32:24.900] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006748)  
By the way, sir, magkakaroon po kami ng pilot project po sa Ilocos Norte with the INEC. Pagka naging successful po yun, tinitingnan namin that would mean around minimum of 600 million po na income sa NIA.

**[06:32:39.700] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006749)  
And we are offering it for only 4 pesos per kilowatt hour po. Mas mababa pa sa singil ng DOE.

**[06:32:45.900] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006750)  
So, Admin, what I am suggesting is...

**[06:32:50.540] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006751)  
Kapitbahay naman po kayo ni Admin Nani. Mag-usap na lang po kayo in the case of electric cooperatives kasi maganda po yung magkaroon ng ugnayan.

**[06:32:59.820] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006752)  
Ang issue po na tinatanong ko kanina kay Sekretary, yung mga lupain natin na sakahan na ginagamit na pang solar farm. Yan po yung tinanong ko kanina, Mr. Chair.

**[06:33:11.300] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006753)  
Mr. Chair, nakatatlong meeting na kami with DOE in the last few months. Actually, last week was yung buong DOT.

**[06:33:20.540] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006754)  
E-team plus yung Sekgarin was in my office. We have a agri-photovoltaic project with them.

**[06:33:28.000] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006755)  
Malinaw sa amin, pag ang high-value crops ang involved, no issue. Dahil meron na kaming study na lumalabas na dun sa ibang high-value crops like sile, eggplant, and others.

**[06:33:40.180] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006756)  
Actually, mas maganda yung production with shaded area kasi nga in between.

**[06:33:46.220] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006757)  
Doon sa bigas naman, yun ang aming main issue as of the moment.

**[06:33:50.540] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006758)  
Hindi pa kami pumapayag totally na maglagay ng solar sa rice land.

**[06:33:55.640] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006759)  
But meron kaming mga research na gagawin very soon sa aming mga DA stations sa Bohol at sa isa pa sa Mindanao to study how this can work.

**[06:34:10.720] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006760)  
But kung wala pa yung conclusive study, as of the moment, ayaw namin kung flat land for rice or others.

**[06:34:18.600] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006761)  
Meron din yung...

**[06:34:19.880] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006762)  
Pero pumapayag kami sa ngayon na yung lahat ng 8% slope, more than 8% slope, walang issue sa amin, Mr. Chair.

**[06:34:28.820] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006763)  
Mr. Secretary, napakahalaga po yung issue na tinutukoy ko kasi pareho natin kailangan pagkain at saka kuryente.

**[06:34:37.900] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006764)  
Ngayon kung matagal po natin mayayos, ang ibig ko pong sabihin, matukoy na natin ngayon at the DOE will be guided kung saan.

**[06:34:49.880] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006765)  
Kasi kung magkakaroon tayo ng project doon tapos masita ni DA, dahil ay hindi pwede yan, gagastos na yung mga investor, etc.

**[06:35:00.420] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006766)  
So mas maganda siguro na kahit inisyal, maka-identify na tayo ng mga area na pwedeng mapaglagyan para guided si DOE.

**[06:35:11.160] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006767)  
Kasi yung transmission natin, ay ididikit po yan doon sa planta.

**[06:35:16.580] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006768)  
Malaki po, billion-billion po yung cost ng transmission.

**[06:35:19.880] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006769)  
Baka makatiwangwang kung ano.

**[06:35:21.860] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006770)  
So what I am suggesting is, these are all issues of national significance, Mr. Chair.

**[06:35:28.360] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006771)  
Yes, Mr. Chair. Meron din kaming unwritten agreement sa ngayon with DOE na all existing projects na naumpisahan na

**[06:35:37.560] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006772)  
o kaya na bigyan na nila ng permit, itutuloy.

**[06:35:42.560] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006773)  
Yung mga bago lang ang hinapahold namin para maayos yung policy framework.

**[06:35:49.000] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006774)  
Meron kaming nabanggit kaninang earlier this morning na i-geotag na namin yung mga reserved areas for agriculture, geofencing pala,

**[06:35:57.680] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006775)

**[06:36:04.520] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006776)  
And we'll hopefully finish the geofencing by end of this year, Mr. Chair.

**[06:36:10.200] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006777)  
Thank you for that information, Mr. Secretary.

**[06:36:13.440] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006778)  
Panghuli na lang, Mr. Secretary.

**[06:36:15.980] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006779)  
Mayroon tayong bagong batas na.

**[06:36:19.000] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006780)  
Mayroon tayong RA 12308.

**[06:36:21.940] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006781)  
Can you brief this representation kung paano natin i-implemento yung Animal Competitiveness Fund?

**[06:36:35.120] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006782)  
Mr. Chair, basically, sa taxes na magkakolekta natin sa importasyon ng mga meats natin ng bansa, from chicken, pork, and others,

**[06:36:47.880] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006783)  
magkakaroon natin.

**[06:36:49.000] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006784)  
Mayroon tayong 20 billion a year ang ating livestock industry or animal industry.

**[06:36:55.740] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006785)  
Majority dyan, initially, ay lalagay natin sa repopulation, about 4 to 5 billion out of the 20.

**[06:37:03.480] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006786)  
So, 25% ng budget na yun will be doon ilalagay.

**[06:37:08.920] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006787)  
Of course, we are creating also a vaccine manufacturing facility.

**[06:37:13.060] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006788)  
Mayroon din tayong herd build-up.

**[06:37:16.160] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006789)  
Mayroon din tayong mga...

**[06:37:19.000] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006790)  
Sa dairy, para taasin natin yung ating production ng dairy, among others.

**[06:37:23.340] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006791)  
Mayroon namang breakdown sa batas na susundin din namin, base dun sa nakalagay sa batas na sinulong natin.

**[06:37:30.380] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006792)  
Mr. Chair.

**[06:37:31.460] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006793)  
Okay.

**[06:37:32.000] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006794)  
Finally, Mr. Chair, gusto ko lang paalalahanan sana.

**[06:37:37.540] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006795)  
Alam ko naman, naalam ninyo ito, na hindi lang budget ang kailangan ng lahat ng government agency.

**[06:37:45.740] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006796)  
Specifically, yung napakahalaga yung agriculture.

**[06:37:48.680] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006797)  
Sa atin, at erikisyon.

**[06:37:50.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006798)  
Kung hindi, mga tauhan na tapat sa servisyo.

**[06:37:54.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006799)  
Kasi yung tatlong taon ko na halos makipag-away na ako sa plenary,

**[06:37:59.740] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006800)  
eh tao lang naman pala ang solusyon.

**[06:38:02.520] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006801)  
Yung tamang tao.

**[06:38:03.660] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006802)  
Kaya, kahit malaki pa yung budget ninyo,

**[06:38:08.500] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006803)  
pag hindi tama yung tao na nailagay ninyo at nautosan,

**[06:38:15.460] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006804)  
magka-problema pa rin tayo sa pagkain.

**[06:38:17.900] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006805)  
Thank you.

**[06:38:18.680] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006806)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[06:38:19.660] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006807)  
Mr. Chair, definitely, tama ko kayo.

**[06:38:22.880] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006808)  
At nangyaring kami ng support pa sa inyo sa pagsulong ko ng agri-extension workers na kailangan na kailangan ng DA

**[06:38:30.840] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006809)

**[06:38:35.920] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006810)  
diretso na nakatutok sa ating mga magsasakat mga isla.

**[06:38:39.700] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006811)

Thank you, Chair.

**[06:38:42.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-006813)

Mr. Chair.

**[06:38:44.360] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006816)

**[06:38:45.120] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006817)

**[06:38:49.300] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006819)

**[06:38:50.660] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-006820)
