**[06:38:57.440] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006821)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[06:38:59.040] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006822)  
Good afternoon, everyone.

**[06:39:00.380] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006823)  
Good afternoon, Sekretary Laurel of DA and kay Engineer Gullian ng NIA.

**[06:39:07.960] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006824)  
My question po is, Mr. Chair, para po sa DA,

**[06:39:13.260] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006825)  
from January to March 2026,

**[06:39:16.180] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006826)  
the average farmgate price of hogs for slaughter was quoted at P176 per kilogram.

**[06:39:24.220] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006827)  
This indicates a decrease of 16.1% from the previous year's same quarter quotation of P209 per kilogram,

**[06:39:34.600] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006828)  
according po to sa Philippine Statistics Authority.

**[06:39:38.180] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006829)  
By July 2026, an average retail price of P306 per kilogram at the national level was reported as P209 per kilogram.

**[06:39:46.080] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006830)  
At the national level was reported as P209 per kilogram at the national level was reported as P209 per kilogram.

**[06:39:46.160] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006831)  
This was noted for fresh pork.

**[06:39:48.020] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006832)  
Fresh pork meat with bones.

**[06:39:51.040] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006833)  
Kasi po, Mr. Chair, I'm from Quezon Province po.

**[06:39:56.960] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006834)  
And sa pag-ikot-ikot ko po sa mga barangay,

**[06:40:00.640] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006835)  
ang aming pong probinsya hindi lamang po mayaman sa dagat at sa mga gulay-gulay.

**[06:40:07.540] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006836)  
Marami din po sa amin ang kabuhayan ay hayupan,

**[06:40:10.940] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006837)  
particularly po ang mga backyard hog raisers.

**[06:40:14.680] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006838)  
Ngayon po, ang presyo na lamang,

**[06:40:16.160] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006839)  
po doon sa 150 pesos to 160.

**[06:40:22.180] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006840)  
Napakababa po.

**[06:40:23.840] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006841)  
Tumaas na po ang feeds at kung ano-ano na.

**[06:40:26.760] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006842)  
Tapos ngayon po bumaba ang presyo ng live weight.

**[06:40:31.500] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006843)  
From DA po, mula po sa inyo,

**[06:40:34.380] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006844)  
ano po ba yung factors na naka-apekto?

**[06:40:36.800] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006845)  
Sino po ang nagbibigay ng presyo sa livestock?

**[06:40:40.060] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006846)  
At ano na po bang nangyayari?

**[06:40:41.620] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006847)  
Bakit patuloy na bumababa ang presyo ng live weight po natin ngayon?

**[06:40:49.000] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006848)  
Mr. Chair, tuloy-tuloy na pinag-aaralan ng DA,

**[06:40:52.040] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006849)  
especially yung ating livestock group,

**[06:40:54.400] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006850)  
ang situation na ito.

**[06:40:56.040] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006851)  
Actually, sa Mindanao, meron pa tayong mga farmgate na 120 na lang.

**[06:41:00.400] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006852)  
Or 130, no?

**[06:41:02.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006853)  
Ang phenomenon na nakikita namin,

**[06:41:04.700] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006854)  
nangyari din ito last year but not as bad,

**[06:41:07.320] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006855)  
is basically yung ASF.

**[06:41:10.680] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006856)  
Natatakot ngayon.

**[06:41:11.500] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006857)  
Natatakot ng mga farmers na tamaan ng ASF,

**[06:41:13.580] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006858)  
lalo na sa rami ng ulan.

**[06:41:15.280] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006859)  
Tuwing umuulan, lumalabas ulit ang ASF

**[06:41:17.680] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006860)  
dahil hindi hoon namamatay ang virus

**[06:41:19.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006861)  
kahit na binaon natin sa lupa ang baboy.

**[06:41:22.020] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006862)  
So tuwing ulan, lumalabas ngayon sa groundwater

**[06:41:24.380] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006863)  
at bumabalik ang ASF.

**[06:41:26.580] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006864)  
At supply and demand ang nagdidikta ng presyo ngayon.

**[06:41:29.700] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006865)  
Maraming hog farmers na nagpa-fast break

**[06:41:33.060] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006866)  
at ina-unload yung kanilang stocks

**[06:41:38.520] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006867)  
dahil nga natatakot na tamaan ng ASF.

**[06:41:41.500] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006868)  
Dahil mayroon na sila sigurong narinig na may ASF na.

**[06:41:45.800] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006869)  
Meron din akong kausap ng mga taga Mindanao

**[06:41:48.940] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006870)  
na biniblame nila yung importasyon ng baboy.

**[06:41:53.600] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006871)  
Pero pinakita ko sa kanila na sa Mindanao,

**[06:41:56.760] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006872)  
wala naman importasyon masyado ng baboy, no?

**[06:42:00.380] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006873)  
Sa Pilipinas.

**[06:42:02.240] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006874)  
More of Luzon.

**[06:42:03.840] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006875)  
Pero ang presyo na 120.

**[06:42:05.660] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006876)  
That's what kaya ang assumption namin as of the moment

**[06:42:11.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006877)  
is nagpa-fast break din yung mga taga Mindanao

**[06:42:13.780] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006878)  
kasi nga natatakot sa ASF.

**[06:42:16.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006879)  
Ang magandang balita naman,

**[06:42:17.900] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006880)  
siguro in coordination with kong Nicky Briones

**[06:42:21.760] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006881)  
at sa push din niya,

**[06:42:24.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006882)  
ay yung ating vaccine for ASF

**[06:42:26.960] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006883)  
ay ilalabas na natin this week ba, Yusek?

**[06:42:31.900] DA Undersecretary Constante J. Palabrica** (asr-006884)  
Nailabas na, sir.

**[06:42:33.340] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006885)  
Nailabas na yung ating for commercial use

**[06:42:36.540] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006886)  
ang ASF vaccine

**[06:42:37.780] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006887)  
at pwede nang bumili,

**[06:42:41.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006888)  
pag dumating yung stocks ng supplier,

**[06:42:43.960] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006889)  
ang ng ASF vaccine ng ating mga hog raisers

**[06:42:47.380] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006890)  
para maprotektahan nga at mag-stabilize ang presyo

**[06:42:50.620] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006891)  
ng baboy sa farm gate level, Mr. Chair.

**[06:42:55.920] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006892)  
Thank you po, Osek.

**[06:42:57.860] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006893)  
Ang follow-up question ko po dyan,

**[06:42:59.960] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006894)  
kung sinasabing nyo po ang Mindanao at Visayas,

**[06:43:02.360] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006895)  
okay po ang supply po nila ng karne po ngayon?

**[06:43:08.160] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006896)  
No, ang sinasabing ko lang, Mr. Chair,

**[06:43:10.140] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006897)  
is bagsak din ang presyo ng farm gate.

**[06:43:12.420] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006898)  
Sa Visayas at Mindanao.

**[06:43:14.300] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006899)  
Kung bagsak po ang farm gate,

**[06:43:16.800] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006900)  
ang presyo ng karne po sa buong Pilipinas,

**[06:43:20.580] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006901)  
gaano po kalaki ang ini-import po nating karne sa kasalukuyan?

**[06:43:34.300] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006902)  
I'm quite specific sa ating cuts

**[06:43:38.940] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006903)  
kasi ang main, Mr. Chair,

**[06:43:41.360] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006904)  
ang main na apektohan is yung pork cuts

**[06:43:43.860] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006905)  
tsaka bellies.

**[06:43:44.740] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006906)  
Yung pork cuts, yun yung kasim tsaka pigi.

**[06:43:49.020] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006907)  
Sa data ng DA, actually,

**[06:43:51.740] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006908)  
2025 was the highest in recent history, no?

**[06:43:55.060] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006909)  
Pero katulad ng pork bellies,

**[06:44:00.540] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006910)  
noong 2025, 9,000 tons a month ang importasyon.

**[06:44:06.600] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006911)  
Pero for 2026, bumaba siya to 8,000 tons a month lang.

**[06:44:11.480] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006912)  
Pagdating sa pork cuts noong 2025,

**[06:44:15.860] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006913)  
29,800 tons.

**[06:44:19.020] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006914)  
Tons a month siya.

**[06:44:22.540] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006915)  
Pero 2026, as of August 24, 25,000 tons lang.

**[06:44:28.400] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006916)  
So bumaba actually ang importasyon for this year

**[06:44:31.440] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006917)  
compared sa last year.

**[06:44:33.420] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006918)  
So it means healthy yung ating industry.

**[06:44:38.740] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006919)  
Problema lang talaga, nagpa-fast break ang industriya

**[06:44:41.360] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006920)  
base sa datos ko.

**[06:44:42.560] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006921)  
Thank you.

**[06:44:44.400] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006922)  
Okay po.

**[06:44:45.860] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006923)  
Kasi po, Sek,

**[06:44:47.580] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006924)  
bagsak po ang presyo.

**[06:44:48.680] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006925)  
Sa Farmgate, pero ang presyo ng karne,

**[06:44:51.780] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006926)  
mataas pa rin po.

**[06:44:54.000] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006927)  
Ano po?

**[06:44:54.940] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006928)  
Ano po ba yung intervention na ginagawa po ng DA?

**[06:44:58.460] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006929)  
Aside from importasyon.

**[06:45:00.000] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006930)  
Tama po, okay lang po yan, may importation tayo. Pero short term, short term yung goal lang po ito.

**[06:45:08.540] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006931)  
Ano po yung pang long term natin para po tayo makatulong sa ating mga farmers, sa ating hog raisers?

**[06:45:15.560] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006932)  
Mr. Chair, I would just like to clarify na hindi, ang DA is not in favor of continuous importation.

**[06:45:25.720] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006933)  
Ang position namin, kung pwedeng i-restrict yan, but unfortunately, meron tayong tinusunod na WTO rules.

**[06:45:33.620] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006934)  
At pwede lang natin i-increase ay taripa.

**[06:45:36.820] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006935)  
Nag-request na ho ang DA sa ating economic managers na kung pwedeng itaas ang taripa ng baboy at ibalik sa dating taripa.

**[06:45:45.080] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006936)  
At ngayon, meron din tayong move na, and we are working with the tariff commission na yung jowls na tinatawag,

**[06:45:52.160] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006937)  
o kaya yung pangaba yun, ay i-classify.

**[06:45:55.720] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006938)  
I-classify na hindi na siya o-false, i-classify na siya at passport cut para itaas yung taripa.

**[06:46:04.040] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006939)  
Yun ho, yun lang ang madalis at pinakamabilis na supportang pwedeng ibigay natin sa ating mga hog raisers, Mr. Chair.

**[06:46:15.730] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006940)  
Mr. Chair, I appreciate the explanation of Secretary Laurel.

**[06:46:20.710] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006941)  
Siyempre po, ito po naman na inquiry lamang, pero siyempre po, at the end of the day,

**[06:46:25.530] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006942)  
this representation fully supports the approval of your budget.

**[06:46:30.070] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006943)  
Sa bagong budget ng DA po at saka ng NIA, sana po huwag po natin kalimutan ang ating mga maliliit na backyard hog raisers.

**[06:46:39.190] Rep. Ma. Cristina Talavera Lopez** (asr-006944)  
Yun lamang po, and thank you po, Mr. Chair.

**[06:46:43.860] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006945)  
Yes, definitely the DA would like to help the livestock sector, lalo na yung mga nagbababoy at nagmamanok.

**[06:46:52.660] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006946)  
At sa bagong budget na makukuha namin for the livestock, talagang repopulation and increase in population,

**[06:47:01.740] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-006947)  
langan para mabawasan ang importasyon at makalaban tayo, Mr. Chair.

**[06:47:06.380] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-006948)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary. Thank you, Congresswoman Christina. The next

**[06:47:13.960] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-006949)  
Sa isa, from the minority, Representative Ma'am Zubiri.

**[06:47:20.980] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006950)  
Maraming salamat po, Mr. Chair. At magandang hapon po sa ating mga bisita galing sa Department of Agriculture,

**[06:47:27.780] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006951)  
lalong-lalo na sa ating butihing Secretary, Secretary Francisco Chula.

**[06:47:31.740] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006952)  
Magandang hapon po.

**[06:47:34.180] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006953)  
Bago po ako mag-umpisa, sana payagan niyo po ako na magbida ng konti tungkol sa Bukidnon.

**[06:47:40.440] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006954)  
Because this year po, naglabasan ng report for 2025 na Bukidnon is the top province in value of production in agriculture and fisheries and crop production

**[06:47:52.220] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006955)  
out of the 2.42 trillion pesos output of the Philippines.

**[06:47:57.620] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006956)  
And so out of that 2.42, ang Bukidnon po.

**[06:48:01.740] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006957)  
Sa agriculture and fisheries, 162.3 billion pesos.

**[06:48:07.460] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006958)  
Habang ang sumusunod po ay nasa 94.2 billion.

**[06:48:11.240] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006959)  
Ang crop production po ng Bukidnon ay nasa 105.58 billion pesos.

**[06:48:18.020] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006960)  
Habang ang sumusunod ay nasa 50.18 billion.

**[06:48:21.580] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006961)  
Habang ang livestock po, out of the 420 billion ng country,

**[06:48:26.640] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006962)  
ang output coming from Bukidnon is 36.09 billion pesos.

**[06:48:31.740] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006963)  
And syempre, gusto ko po magpasalamat sa ating mga kaibigan sa DA.

**[06:48:36.420] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006964)  
Dahil syempre, alam ko naman po na hindi natin ma-achieve ang mga numbers na ito kung wala ang full support ng Department of Agriculture

**[06:48:43.920] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006965)  
sa aming farms, sa aming mga farmers, and sa local government unit and provincial government unit ng Bukidnon.

**[06:48:51.200] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006966)  
So thank you very much, Secretary, sa inyong full support.

**[06:48:54.700] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006967)  
And we hope na ngayong 2026, kami pa rin ang top performer.

**[06:48:58.980] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006968)  
Now, having said this, no?

**[06:49:01.740] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006969)  
I would like to inquire, kasi given nga our numbers, may nabasa lang ako dito sa ating reports na medyo nalito lang ako ng konti.

**[06:49:10.740] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006970)  
So I would like to ask for clarification from our friends sa NIA tungkol sa ating Bukidnon 3rd District budget from the NIA.

**[06:49:21.520] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006971)  
Kasi ang nakalagay po dito was yung RDC endorsed noong 2025, nasa 772 million.

**[06:49:28.700] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006972)  
Noong 2026, bumaba sa 500 million.

**[06:49:31.740] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006973)  
Noong 2010, habang sa 2027, ang RDC endorsed is 273 million.

**[06:49:37.820] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006974)  
And may I clarify lang, kasi dito sa ating form dito, ang nakalagay sa NEP for Bukidnon 3rd District ay zero.

**[06:49:48.040] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006975)  
But I would like to clarify this, no?

**[06:49:50.140] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006976)  
So we can discuss this further kung ano ba talaga ang tamang amount para sa Bukidnon with our friends from the NIA.

**[06:49:57.420] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006977)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[06:50:02.220] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006978)  
Please just give me a time.

**[06:50:04.100] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006979)  
Kasi siguro, gaya nga nang sabi ko, napakaganda ng mga output natin sa Bukidnon.

**[06:50:12.760] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006980)  
So I would like to clarify this.

**[06:50:21.630] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006981)  
Hindi naman siguro, kaya maganda mag-clarify muna.

**[06:50:29.070] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-006982)  
Pinakamagandang record ang Bukidnon, tapos according to Congresswoman, is zero.

**[06:50:43.030] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006983)  
Yes, ma'am actually

**[06:50:45.510] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006984)  
Kaya ako nagulat din dahil na...

**[06:50:49.110] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006985)  
Alam ko naman, ang Bukidnon talaga isa sa pinapangandang probinsya pagdating sa agrikultura.

**[06:50:54.070] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006986)  
So marami po kaming proposals.

**[06:50:55.910] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006987)  
And you know, pupunta kami sa opisina niyo, makikipag-tulungan po kami.

**[06:51:03.950] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006988)  
Mag-courtesy na lang po kami.

**[06:51:06.910] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006989)  
Ah, sige.

**[06:51:07.450] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006990)  
So itong ating current figure dito, hindi pa naman final?

**[06:51:14.970] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006991)  
Hindi lang nila na-update, Mr. Chair.

**[06:51:17.490] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006992)  
Hindi lang nila na-update po.

**[06:51:18.610] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006993)  
I'm sorry, could you repeat that please?

**[06:51:21.610] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006994)  
Hindi ko masyadong marinig.

**[06:51:22.390] NIA Administrator Eduardo Eddie G. Guillen** (asr-006995)  
Yes, bukang hindi po nila, hindi po updated yung...

**[06:51:25.490] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006996)  
Ah, okay. Hindi siya updated.

**[06:51:26.850] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006997)  
Bukang, baka nahulog yung isang one.

**[06:51:30.330] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006998)  
Sige, okay. I'm glad to hear that.

**[06:51:31.950] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-006999)  
Kasi syempre, you know, ayoko naman na hindi natin ma-keep up yung ating reputation as Bukidnon being the top producer.

**[06:51:39.530] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007000)  
E pag nawalan kami ng support from the NIA, may hirapan kami to produce all this agriculture,

**[06:51:44.990] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007001)  
which I think is very badly needed by our country.

**[06:51:48.610] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007002)  
And I would like to think that Department of Agriculture, especially NIA, will continue to support Bukidnon

**[06:51:54.290] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007003)  
para we can continue to support the country's agricultural needs.

**[06:51:58.090] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007004)  
Okay, thank you very much to our director.

**[06:52:01.130] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007005)  
Our second doc, my second question, of course, goes to our friends from the SRA.

**[06:52:06.330] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007006)  
Hi, good afternoon, Admin.

**[06:52:08.410] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007007)  
Kamusta na, Admin Ascona?

**[06:52:11.170] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007008)  
Good afternoon po, Mr. Chair. Good afternoon, ma'am.

**[06:52:14.350] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007009)  
May hapon.

**[06:52:15.410] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007010)  
So, last year, I remember we worked quite closely.

**[06:52:18.610] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007011)  
And trying to increase the budget, or actually trying to increase the budget of the SRA.

**[06:52:23.690] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007012)  
Because, syempre, as a sugar-producing province, talagang nakikita namin ang impact at ang napakalaking effect at tulong ng SRA.

**[06:52:30.770] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007013)  
And we wanted to increase your budget from 2 billion to maybe 3 or 4, no?

**[06:52:35.510] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007014)  
Kaya lang, I was very disappointed to find out that, number one, hindi natin na-increase ang inyong budget.

**[06:52:41.930] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007015)  
And even more so, more disappointed to discover na yung 2 billion na dapat naka-alot sa inyo,

**[06:52:47.730] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007016)  
1 billion na naka-alot sa inyo.

**[06:52:48.610] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007017)  
So, 1 billion pa rin lang ang naibigay sa inyo sa ating last budget.

**[06:52:52.210] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007018)  
Do you mind updating me on why, what was the final explanation? Bakit 1 billion lang napunta sa inyo?

**[06:52:59.610] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007019)  
Yeah, Mr. Chair, we were informed of, once again, utilization problems.

**[06:53:06.170] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007020)  
So, there has been new guidelines on procurement, which actually delayed a lot of our utilization.

**[06:53:13.930] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007021)  
So, we're still hoping to make it up in the last 3 years.

**[06:53:18.610] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007022)  
We're also planning po, ma'am, to reallocate some of the budget into our quick response fund for the RSSI infection,

**[06:53:29.690] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007023)  
as well as the mitigation on El Niño.

**[06:53:35.610] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007024)  
What programs or mandates of the SRA are currently affected by this lack of budget?

**[06:53:40.750] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007025)  
Would you be able to tell me?

**[06:53:43.830] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007026)  
Or because of the insufficient appropriations?

**[06:53:45.790] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007027)  
Yeah, well, Mr. Chair,

**[06:53:48.610] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007028)  
since the SIDA program po is pre-allocated by the law,

**[06:53:52.890] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007029)  
so, from 2 billion to 1 billion po, it cuts everything by 50%.

**[06:53:57.430] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007030)  
So, malaki po yung effect sa amin po.

**[06:54:01.190] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007031)  
So, parang across the board, talagang naka-annuation proportion din siya?

**[06:54:05.430] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007032)  
So, lahat yan, kasama yung ating mga infrastructure, yung ating mga block farm programs, ating mga scholarships, no?

**[06:54:12.490] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007033)  
Lahat yan matatamaan, especially, and I think this is not the time for that to happen,

**[06:54:17.270] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007034)  
given na ang lakas.

**[06:54:18.610] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007035)  
Okay, ng threat ng El Niño na hinaharap natin, and syempre yung ating RSSI.

**[06:54:25.470] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007036)  
And with regard to RSSI, I'm very happy because the last time nag-usap tayo,

**[06:54:29.370] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007037)  
alam ko na nga ako ang SRA na maghahatid kayo ng Presidential Assistance for Farmers program sa Bukidnon.

**[06:54:37.270] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007038)  
May I ask on the status of that current program?

**[06:54:40.290] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007039)  
Yeah, we're still ongoing po, ma'am, on the releases.

**[06:54:43.210] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007040)  
Yung total po na ayuda for the whole industry, umabot na ng 420-something.

**[06:54:48.610] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007041)  
Million.

**[06:54:49.970] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007042)  
So, we have, this is, most of it is coming from the SWD, and as of my last knowledge,

**[06:54:59.050] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007043)  
we released, I think, almost 100 million already.

**[06:55:02.530] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007044)  
So, this is farmers and workers na po.

**[06:55:04.750] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007045)  
Out of that 100 billion, how much was allocated for Bukidnon?

**[06:55:08.990] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007046)  
I am not currently aware of the percentage, ma'am.

**[06:55:11.690] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007047)  
I'll provide you with the information.

**[06:55:13.670] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007048)  
Okay, I will look forward to that information.

**[06:55:15.870] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007049)  
Sana, since given that Bukidnon is one of the...

**[06:55:18.450] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007050)  
one of the biggest sugar-producing portions of our country,

**[06:55:21.930] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007051)  
and currently, actually, nag-umpisa tayo at only about 30 hectares.

**[06:55:26.730] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007052)  
Pero sa ngayon, ang aking latest calculation na natanggap ay nasa 576 hectares na in just a span of 3 months.

**[06:55:34.670] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007053)  
So, I'm deeply concerned as to how much more we're going to be looking at.

**[06:55:39.170] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007054)  
And my last question is, of course, for our friends from the DA and Region 10,

**[06:55:44.290] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007055)  
ang understanding ko is, meron kayong request, no?

**[06:55:48.450] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007056)  
For expanding our laboratory budget, for our laboratory budget from our DA in Region 10.

**[06:55:58.250] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007057)  
Currently, magkano ba ang budget na nakatanggap ng ating regional office

**[06:56:03.990] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007058)  
para sa mga biological control and other issues such as that?

**[06:56:09.830] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007059)  
Hello po.

**[06:56:15.100] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007060)  
Yung, in terms of capacity to respond to RISIS-I,

**[06:56:19.700] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007061)  
early first quarter, yun.

**[06:56:21.520] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007062)  
We have 5 hectares per day.

**[06:56:22.980] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007063)  
Now, when the RISIS-I reaches up to 1.1% infection rate,

**[06:56:27.800] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007064)  
we raise our capacity to 20 hectares.

**[06:56:30.740] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007065)  
Three weeks ago, we have a discussion with the SRA in Bukidnon.

**[06:56:36.020] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007066)  
We raise it to 30 hectares, which means every day,

**[06:56:40.120] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007067)  
we can supply biological control up to 30 hectares per day,

**[06:56:43.440] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007068)  
totaling to, in a week, we can actually supply up to 3, 6, 9, 12, 15,

**[06:56:51.520] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007069)  
150 hectares.

**[06:56:54.340] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007070)  
We computed it in terms of the infection rate.

**[06:56:57.960] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007071)  
I don't have the specific budget,

**[06:56:59.780] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007072)  
but I want to respond on our capacity to deliver response to RISIS-I.

**[06:57:04.840] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007073)  
Before the end of this year,

**[06:57:06.240] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007074)  
our estimate is that we can respond up to 100 hectares per day,

**[06:57:09.460] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007075)  
which is more than enough with the infection rate of around 0.7% per month since January.

**[06:57:15.860] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007076)  
So, we have also a plan to increase first quarter of next year.

**[06:57:21.520] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007077)  
So, that we can apply every day 100 hectares,

**[06:57:27.620] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007078)  
so that it is more than enough with the existing 2.2% infected sugarcane out of 45,000 hectares.

**[06:57:38.380] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007079)  
So, yung estimate namin, if we work together with SRA in responding farmers who want biological control,

**[06:57:48.360] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007080)  
in the next three months,

**[06:57:50.200] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007081)  
kaya namin i-maintain,

**[06:57:51.520] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007082)  
2.5% o yung makarecover po.

**[06:57:55.180] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007083)  
Ang tinitinan lang namin, yung early infestation at saka yung nasa low pa,

**[06:58:03.000] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007084)  
yun yung i-respond namin.

**[06:58:04.400] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007085)  
Ngayon, we have mapped out yung direction.

**[06:58:06.260] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007086)  
Saan ba pumupunta yung RISIS-I?

**[06:58:08.040] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007087)  
From east to west, from west to ano.

**[06:58:09.880] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007088)  
So that, more or less, we can preposition our inputs.

**[06:58:13.740] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007089)  
Right now, we have 150 million,

**[06:58:16.380] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007090)  
more or less, 150 million that we propose as a quick response fund

**[06:58:20.320] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007091)  
to increase our...

**[06:58:21.520] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007092)  
capacity sa laboratory

**[06:58:22.840] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007093)  
so that we will not reach up to 5% infection rate.

**[06:58:28.300] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007094)  
Right now, the DE has identified three levels of infection.

**[06:58:31.720] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007095)  
The below, low, moderate, at saka severe.

**[06:58:39.360] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007096)  
So, according with that classification,

**[06:58:42.880] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007097)  
we can exactly respond to the situation

**[06:58:47.080] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007098)  
knowing that before the end of this year,

**[06:58:49.140] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007099)  
we can increase our capacity po.

**[06:58:51.520] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007100)  
Ang gays.

**[06:58:56.870] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007101)  
Okay.

**[06:58:57.370] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007102)  
So, I'm glad to hear na meron tayong mga plano.

**[06:58:59.630] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007103)  
Pero ang tanong ko is, kahit ma-increase natin yung capacity natin,

**[06:59:02.810] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007104)  
eventually before the end of the year,

**[06:59:04.510] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007105)  
as you said, to how many hectares per day,

**[06:59:06.810] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007106)  
mahahabol ba natin yung ating infection rate?

**[06:59:10.050] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007107)  
Kasi right now, nakita natin from 30 hectares,

**[06:59:12.230] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007108)  
nasa 576 na tayo in just three months.

**[06:59:15.530] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007109)  
So, in another one to two months,

**[06:59:17.830] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007110)  
baka yung 576 nasa 1,000 plus hectares na or even 2,000.

**[06:59:22.270] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007111)  
So, mahahabol ba?

**[06:59:23.990] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007112)  
Kahit ma-increase?

**[06:59:24.830] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007113)  
Itong ating capacity?

**[06:59:26.030] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007114)  
Yes po.

**[06:59:26.670] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007115)  
Kasi kasama nung ginawa yung Crystal Milling Corporation,

**[06:59:32.510] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007116)  
we helped them start their own laboratory.

**[06:59:35.190] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007117)  
Kahit yung sa Busco.

**[06:59:36.310] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007118)  
In fact, our meeting last time with Mr. Lorenzo,

**[06:59:39.490] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007119)  
who heads the farmers group for sugarcane,

**[06:59:42.270] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007120)  
he only needs 20 hectares per day.

**[06:59:44.250] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007121)  
That was July.

**[06:59:45.450] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007122)  
But we were able to increase it up to 30.

**[06:59:47.690] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007123)  
So, with the participation of private sector who owns laboratory,

**[06:59:52.310] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007124)  
we can actually maintain it, ma'am,

**[06:59:55.170] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007125)  
not beyond 5 percent.

**[06:59:56.410] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007126)  
Right now, with the increasing participation of owners of...

**[07:00:00.000] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007127)  
milling company na dalawa sa bukid nun. Sa tingin ko before the end of this year, kaya namin nating hindi lalag pa sa 5% po.

**[07:00:09.720] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007128)  
That's good to hear. That's good news. Thank you very much, Director Pacamalan.

**[07:00:13.220] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007129)  
And so as a final question, magkano kaya ang kailangan na budget ng DA Region 10 in order to reach that capacity,

**[07:00:21.220] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007130)  
to increase para maabot niyo yung capacity na yan?

**[07:00:23.520] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007131)  
Yung disubmit namin as part ng Quick Response Fund is nasa P150 million po.

**[07:00:31.060] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007132)  
Magkano po?

**[07:00:31.900] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007133)  
P150 million.

**[07:00:32.860] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007134)  
P150 million, yes. And sa tingin natin itong magkano ang mawawala sa atin kung ang bukid nun,

**[07:00:42.680] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007135)  
hindi natin makontrol ang RSSI at grumabit talaga ito, mawalan talaga ng kontrol, magkano mawawala sa atin?

**[07:00:47.920] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007136)  
Malaki po kasi bukid nun has 45,000 hectares of sugarcane and I think that's more than a billion.

**[07:00:53.520] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007137)  
And the reason why we automatically increase our capacity is we are very conscious about not going beyond 5%.

**[07:01:01.960] DA Region X Regional Executive Director Jose Apollo Y. Pacamalan** (asr-007138)  
It's very important commodity in bukid nun talaga.

**[07:01:05.440] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007139)  
So parang bali yung ating increase is not even 10% of how much value we are getting from bukid nun, no?

**[07:01:11.800] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007140)  
And given the fact na bukid nun is producing not just sugar but also corn and palay, which can also be affected by RSSI,

**[07:01:17.520] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007141)  
I think this is a very small investment with very large returns for the DA.

**[07:01:23.520] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007142)  
We can get that funded po, Secretary.

**[07:01:26.200] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007143)  
And of course, Mr. Secretary, I would also like to add na yun nga yung ating sa SRA,

**[07:01:33.080] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007144)  
given that SRA is also helping out by using the money for quick response,

**[07:01:38.260] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007145)  
sana ma-continue natin yung ating goal of not just giving SRA the right budget but really increasing the SRA budget.

**[07:01:46.180] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007146)  
What are these problems with acquisition?

**[07:01:49.000] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007147)  
I'm sorry, you were saying earlier, I'd like to go back to that.

**[07:01:51.400] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007148)  
Ano ba yung nagiging bottleneck?

**[07:01:53.520] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007149)  
Nagiging bottleneck natin sa acquisition ba, you said, sa procedure?

**[07:01:57.520] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007150)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[07:02:02.080] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007151)  
There were new rules, I think, MARB and stuff like that, which delayed our procurement.

**[07:02:07.560] SRA Administrator Pablo Luis S. Azcona** (asr-007152)  
But we're making it up, no, actually, for the last quarter of the year.

**[07:02:14.300] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007153)  
I'm glad to hear that.

**[07:02:15.540] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007154)  
My time is up.

**[07:02:16.660] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007155)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[07:02:18.600] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007156)  
Thank you to our Administrator Ascona, to Director.

**[07:02:23.520] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007157)  
Mr. Pamalacan, and of course, to Secretary Chula Aurel.

**[07:02:26.560] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007158)  
Rest assured, this representation is more than happy not just to support but to add.

**[07:02:31.460] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007159)  
I really wish we can add more to our DA.

**[07:02:33.920] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007160)  
So let's work on that together para Bukidnon can continue to also add to the production value of the country.

**[07:02:39.480] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-007161)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[07:02:41.500] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-007162)  
Thank you, Congresswoman Sabiri.

**[07:02:44.300] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-007163)  
The next is from the majority, Representative Nicanor Briones.

**[07:02:49.860] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007164)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[07:02:57.070] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007165)  
And our President of the Partylist Coalition, President Pina Cacigang, and also good afternoon to Secretary Chula Aurel and the family,

**[07:03:10.950] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007166)  
also to Administrator Guillen and NIA family.

**[07:03:23.790] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007167)  
First, I would like to...

**[07:03:27.070] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007168)  
First, I would like to thank our hardworking and good secretary of the Department of Agriculture and Culture

**[07:03:34.790] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007169)  
for the request that she mentioned earlier, the use of the ASF vaccine for commercial use.

**[07:03:43.950] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007170)  
First, what we are looking for is a special import permit for quick implementation and import.

**[07:03:53.770] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007171)  
Ang ibibigay niya ay ang approval ng commercial use.

**[07:03:56.910] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007175)  
ng commercial use ibig sabihin yan yung ating 16 million piglets to partner for one year

**[07:04:07.630] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007176)  
ay magkakaroon na ng bakuna libre na yung one month old up to six months old

**[07:04:15.430] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007177)  
na tamaan ng asm so thank you mr secretary kasama yung ating usek danti palabre ka

**[07:04:24.430] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007178)  
ating tinawagan ka o tag gulan delikado pero nagawa ng paraan thank you very much

**[07:04:31.750] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007179)  
so isa pang maganda yung kay usek ano na maari asisperes usek na ini-increase yung ating taripa

**[07:04:45.290] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007180)  
ng jowls from five percent na isinasama sa mga opals alam nyo ba yung opals yung opals yung

**[07:04:54.070] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007181)  
lamanloon

**[07:04:54.430] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007182)  
so tama lang na ating increase yan from five percent to 15 to 25 percent and eventually

**[07:05:08.210] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007183)  
pagka increase yung 30 to 40 na sinabi naman ang ating palihim ating usek na itataas din

**[07:05:16.750] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007184)  
isa pang dapat namin pagpasalamat yung pagdaalaw ng

**[07:05:24.430] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007185)  
presidente sa anong sekretary kiko laurel doon sa batangas egg producers cooperative

**[07:05:31.830] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007186)  
na itinayo natin sometime 2010 and then ngayon problema namin ang pagbibenta ng itlog

**[07:05:46.170] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007187)  
pagkabagsak ang presyo so ito yung ito yung remedyo na magkaroon ng

**[07:05:54.430] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007188)  
itlog na ika nga may innovation at modern facilities para matulungan yung ating mag-iitlog

**[07:06:03.110] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007189)  
which is yung batangas especially yung san Jose egg basket of the Philippines we are

**[07:06:08.570] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007190)  
producing 12 million eggs a day so dahil meron ng liquefied pasteurized egg na two weeks

**[07:06:18.230] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007191)  
na dalawang linggo hanggang dalawang buwan na tatagal ang itlog.

**[07:06:22.430] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007192)  
Meron tayong pag nilagyan ng asin, dalawang taon.

**[07:06:26.430] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007196)  
Meron tayong powdered eggs, up to two years, pwede. Nagawa nila na magkaroon tayo ng mga equipment na ganyan.

**[07:06:33.370] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007197)  
Yung boiled eggs in brine solution, six months na pwedeng tumagal.

**[07:06:43.370] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007198)  
Yung boiled eggs at yung frozen egg, six months.

**[07:06:48.430] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007199)  
So napakagandang tulong nito at pag nagamit, wala nang magiging problema

**[07:06:52.870] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007200)  
kahit na bumagsak yung presyo ng itlog.

**[07:06:53.950] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007201)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Secretary, and especially to President BBM, because the funds that are

**[07:07:01.950] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007202)  
being given to the cooperatives are huge, like BEPCO, CIDC, LIMCOMA, Vision, all of

**[07:07:10.950] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007203)  
them have hundreds of millions of grants.

**[07:07:15.950] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007204)  
So, thank you.

**[07:07:17.950] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007205)  
Now, Mr. Secretary, when it comes to our budget, we have already mentioned the IDCA,

**[07:07:25.950] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007206)  
Animal Industry Development Competitiveness Enhancement Fund Act, that we have an ANSEP

**[07:07:33.950] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007207)  
of more or less 20, it can be 30 billion a year for 10 years.

**[07:07:39.950] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007208)  
I know that it is included in our…

**[07:07:43.950] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007209)  
Hopefully, we can find a way to have a budget.

**[07:07:47.950] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007210)  
The ASF and AI vaccine, I think it is more or less 1 billion, Secretary, because once

**[07:07:59.950] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007211)  
there is an outbreak, we already have funds that we can use to stop the spread of ASF

**[07:08:08.950] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007212)  
or do you think, Secretary, that we will have a budget?

**[07:08:14.950] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-007213)  
Mr. Chair, thank you very much.

**[07:08:16.950] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-007214)  
Thank you.

**[07:08:17.950] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-007215)  
Thank you for your suggestion.

**[07:08:18.950] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-007216)  
And we will put that in our request for an amendment to the BBM and we can put a 1 billion

**[07:08:26.950] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-007217)  
budget on vaccines, right?

**[07:08:28.950] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-007218)  
But the 1 billion budget for vaccines can be for AI or for ASF, Mr. Chair.

**[07:08:34.950] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007219)  
There, Secretary, thank you, if we can do it.

**[07:08:42.820] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007220)  
One more, Secretary, in the ANSEP, I…

**[07:08:47.820] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007221)  
Last question.

**[07:08:50.820] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007222)  
If we can put a fund, we can put a fund on the SEPA or all examination facilities for

**[07:09:00.820] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007223)  
agriculture, or the first border inspection facilities, because we can avoid biosecurity

**[07:09:09.820] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007224)  
and not bring diseases to the imported agricultural products.

**[07:09:16.820] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007225)  
And secondly, we can reduce smuggling.

**[07:09:19.820] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-007226)  
Mr. Chair, meron na tayong pondo for 2026 for implementation na namin for three sites

**[07:09:29.060] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-007227)  
sa Subic, sa Manila, tsaka sa Davao.

**[07:09:32.300] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-007228)  
But of course, kung kulang pa yun, magagamit din natin yung pera from IDCA para doon for

**[07:09:40.060] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-007229)  
the future.

**[07:09:41.660] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-007230)  
Meron lang kaming konting issue sa maning dahil hindi kami makapag-hire ng dagdag

**[07:09:46.800] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-007231)  
tao dahil sa ruling na linabas while may batas naman na pwede kami dapat.

**[07:09:54.260] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-007232)  
So, we're just sorting it out with DBM, Mr. Chair.

**[07:09:57.620] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007233)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[07:10:00.460] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007234)  
Last question, Mr. Secretary.

**[07:10:03.820] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007235)  
Alam niyo, hindi po kami total sa importation.

**[07:10:07.000] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007236)  
Pero sana, doon lang sa pangangailangan na ating mamamayan.

**[07:10:12.980] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007237)  
Baka po mali yung mga reports.

**[07:10:16.800] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007238)  
Kasi pag tinumpit po natin yung in-import this year, kung meron 851 million, yung total

**[07:10:26.120] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007239)  
meat imports ng pork, which is alam naman natin, yung opals, yung skin, fats, may possibility

**[07:10:32.540] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007240)  
na yan ay tunay na pork.

**[07:10:35.720] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007241)  
So, ang hiling lang namin, Mr. Secretary, bawasan naman kasi lahat ng ating ginagawa,

**[07:10:42.820] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007242)  
kung magbebenta ang ating mga farmers ng lugi.

**[07:10:46.800] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007243)  
Mababali wala, Mr. Secretary.

**[07:10:49.800] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007244)  
So, hopefully, yung reference value, magkaroon kayo ng transaction value na maa-advise ang

**[07:10:59.180] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007245)  
DA at saka mga industry farmers na ito yung tamang transaction value para hindi tayo nagagawa

**[07:11:05.840] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007246)  
ng reference value na napakalayo sa presyo ng ini-import na karning agricultural products,

**[07:11:12.380] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007247)  
Mr. Secretary.

**[07:11:13.800] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-007248)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[07:11:15.100] DA Secretary Francisco P. Tiu Laurel Jr.** (asr-007249)  
We will do that, Mr. Chair.

**[07:11:16.800] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007250)  
Thank you, Secretary,

**[07:11:17.800] Rep. Nicanor M. Briones** (asr-007251)  
Thank you Mr. Chair.
